# RulesPal — Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition FAQ

Source: https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/faq (downloaded 2026-10-30, page updated 2026-10-23).
Entries graded "Best reading — unconfirmed" were NOT used as a source on the site; they are marked below.


## Golden Rules, Timing and Components

      ### When several players want to resolve abilities at the same time, who goes first?
      - id: `order-of-simultaneous-abilities` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: In the action phase, the active player goes first, then the other players in initiative order counting on from the active player's number and wrapping around. With the active player on 5 and others on 6, 1, 3 and 8, the order is 5, 6, 8, 1, 3. In the strategy and agenda phases, go in order starting with the speaker instead.
        - Clipper · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27747638#27747638
        - hooliganj · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27748716#27748716
        - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27763331#27763331
        - hooliganj · 2018-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920234/article/27984503#27984503
        - Clipper · 2019-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2301830/article/33256158#33256158
        - theepicwinguy · 2018-01-16 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920234/article/27976225#27976225

    ### If I pass on playing an action card or ability in a timing window, can I still play one later in that window?
- id: `passing-in-a-timing-window` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — passing is not final. Players keep taking turns in the window, and if anyone plays something after you passed, you get another chance. The window only closes once every player passes in a row. This applies to riders, Sabotage, combat cards like Skilled Retreat, and any other timed ability.
  - Clipper · 2018-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983065/article/29047825#29047825
  - hooliganj · 2018-04-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983065/article/28950367#28950367
  - germos · 2018-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920234/article/27969119#27969119
  - AgentDrake · 2021-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2588484/article/36839687#36839687
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2491878/article/35675241#35675241
  - TerTer · 2021-01-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2588484/article/36839570#36839570

      ### Once I resolve an ability in a timing window, can I take it back after my opponent responds?
      - id: `resolved-abilities-cannot-be-undone` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — anything already resolved stays resolved. Your opponent's response just adds to the window; you can answer it with another ability on your next turn in that window, but you cannot withdraw what you already did.
        - AgentDrake · 2021-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2617603/article/37208850#37208850
        - timo93 · 2021-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2617603/article/37210223#37210223

      ### If both players have the same 'after a round of space combat' ability, such as two Exotrireme II dreadnoughts, who resolves first?
      - id: `two-players-same-after-combat-round-ability` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: The active player resolves one first, then the other player, alternating one ability at a time until nobody wants to resolve more. Each use is a separate resolution, so the players take turns rather than one player resolving all of theirs at once.
        - ifaeb · 2025-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3500369/article/45987339#45987339
        - hooliganj · 2025-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3500369/article/45987350#45987350

### Can the defender respond to the active player's Flank Speed with a card like Ceasefire?
- id: `ceasefire-after-flank-speed` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — both are played after a system is activated, so they share one timing window. The active player gets the first chance to play, then the others in order, and the window stays open until everyone passes in succession.
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2518309/article/36000272#36000272

### How do we order two game effects (not player abilities) that trigger at the same time?
- id: `simultaneous-game-effects-speaker-decides` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: The speaker decides the order. Such conflicts are meant to be judged case by case, and absent a specific ruling the speaker resolves the timing question.
  - aegiscruiser · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1858921/article/27155686#27155686

    ### If an ability says 'then', do I have to resolve the first part before the part after 'then' happens?
- id: `then-wording-in-abilities` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — the effect after 'then' only happens if the effect before it was resolved. For example, the Warfare primary gives you no command token back unless you actually removed one from the board. This links effects within a single ability only; a separate paragraph on the same card is its own ability.
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32224622#32224622
  - Somnambulix · 2019-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32250205#32250205
  - IncrediSteve · 2019-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272253/article/32891173#32891173
  - Clipper · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272253/article/32891207#32891207

    ### Is each paragraph on a card a separate ability, for example the 'Then return this card' line on a promissory note?
- id: `separate-paragraphs-are-separate-abilities` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — each paragraph is its own ability and resolves on its own. So a note's final return line still happens even if an earlier paragraph had no effect, and a paragraph you can resolve still works when a different paragraph can't be done.
  - Clipper · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32223907#32223907
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32224622#32224622
  - Clipper · 2018-04-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1982415/article/28923257#28923257
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27929261#27929261

### If an ability tells me to destroy or lose a number of things and I have fewer, do I resolve it partly?
- id: `ability-cannot-be-fully-resolved` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Strictly, no — an ability has to be resolved in its entirety, and only effects joined by 'and' can be resolved separately, so a single effect you cannot fully carry out does nothing. Some players argue for resolving as much as possible. Unstable Planet, for one, was later errata'd to destroy 'up to' 3 infantry.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27928719#27928719
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27929199#27929199
  - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
  - GrumblingGamer · 2018-01-10 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27928633#27928633
  - Padish · 2018-01-11 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27934797#27934797

### If an earlier ruling by the developer contradicts the Living Rules Reference FAQ, which one wins?
- id: `living-rules-reference-overrides-old-rulings` — grade: Designer ruling
- A: The Living Rules Reference and its FAQ win. When FFG released it, the developer said anything he had said before that contradicts the FAQ is overruled by it, and the LRR is the definitive current rule set.
  - CreussEmissary · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/28474625#28474625
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/30760387#30760387

### If I only play the base game, which Living Rules Reference should I use?
- id: `which-rules-reference-for-base-game` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Use the current Prophecy of Kings version of the Living Rules Reference and skip the expansion-only parts. It made no major changes to base rules, though rule numbers shifted from v1.3 (59.4 became 68.4, for example). The revised Diplomacy and Construction strategy cards are recommended in base-only games too.
  - Ndjcirnc · 2023-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3021497/article/41654561#41654561
  - Clipper · 2023-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3021497/article/41655153#41655153
  - Asrial · 2023-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3176917/article/43230000#43230000

### Are the Codex cards official, and do I have to use them?
- id: `codex-cards-official-optional` — grade: Community consensus
- A: They are official FFG content but optional. Cards marked with the Omega symbol replace the original cards of the same name when you use them; Codex cards without the symbol are additions. The Omega versions are widely recommended, since they were made to fix weak originals.
  - Surge72 · 2020-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542301/article/36293377#36293377
  - Phoenix1147 · 2020-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542301/article/36293427#36293427
  - hooliganj · 2023-11-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3187758/article/43263475#43263475
  - Clipper · 2022-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2967879/article/41130990#41130990
  - Hroost · 2020-06-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2449030/article/35117058#35117058

        ### Can I show my action cards or secret objectives to other players?
        - id: `showing-hidden-cards` — grade: Community consensus
        - A: Yes — you may reveal your own hidden cards to anyone at any time, and they still count as hidden afterwards. The old penalty for revealing a secret objective was removed. This only covers your own hidden components, not hidden information you have merely been allowed to look at.
          - dromkaken · 2019-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32341816#32341816
          - hooliganj · 2019-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32344210#32344210
          - Sande24 · 2022-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2819267/article/39530194#39530194
          - AgentDrake · 2022-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2819267/article/39530512#39530512
          - hooliganj · 2019-02-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2156500/article/31328890#31328890

    ### After looking at another player's action cards (for example with Mageon Implants), can I tell other players what I saw?
    - id: `sharing-information-from-peeking` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — nothing in the rules stops you from sharing or trading that information. Restricting it is a house rule.
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-02-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142969/article/31144700#31144700
      - mliszewski · 2019-02-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142969/article/31144755#31144755

      ### Is the action card discard pile face up, and can players look through it?
      - id: `action-card-discard-pile-public` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes to both — discards are face up and public, so anyone may search the pile, for example to count how many Sabotages have been played. Secret objectives have no discard pile; discarded or unchosen ones are shuffled back into their deck.
        - hooliganj · 2019-02-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2156141/article/31325026#31325026
        - IndyBart · 2019-02-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2156141/article/31325146#31325146
        - Clipper · 2020-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2362505/article/33958550#33958550
        - 0 hr · 2018-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2005549/article/29254422#29254422
        - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2795414/article/39260194#39260194
        - Clipper · 2019-11-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2318629/article/33446060#33446060

        ### Can I use an effect that destroys units, like Lucky Shot, Unstable Planet or X-89 Bacterial Weapon, on my own units?
        - id: `destroy-effects-on-own-units` — grade: Community consensus
        - A: No — the Living Rules Reference FAQ rules that abilities that destroy units can only be used against other players' units and planets. Readings that allowed it by the card's wording were overturned by that FAQ entry.
          - GAFBlizzard · 2026-07-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1979413/article/47929759#47929759
          - hooliganj · 2024-06-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3310929/article/44355496#44355496
          - mliszewski · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281004/article/33000268#33000268
          - Clipper · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281004/article/33005538#33005538
          - IncrediSteve · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281004/article/33005706#33005706
          - TomBoombs · 2018-04-20 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1979413/article/28879523#28879523

    ### Are fighters ships?
- id: `fighters-are-ships` — grade: Per the rulebook — Components and Key Concepts
- A: Yes — the ships are carriers, cruisers, dreadnoughts, destroyers, fighters, war suns and flagships. Fighters count for anything that refers to ships unless it says non-fighter ships. Saar Floating Factories move like ships but are not ships.
  - MollyMae · 2022-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2800932/article/39323824#39323824
  - hooliganj · 2019-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2260838/article/32757595#32757595
  - NeoTiger · 2019-08-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2260838/article/32764223#32764223

    ### Are PDS and space docks ground forces?
- id: `pds-and-space-docks-are-structures` — grade: Per the rulebook — Components and Key Concepts
- A: No — PDS and space docks are structures, a separate unit type. In the base game, infantry are the only ground forces.
  - AgentDrake · 2018-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2119535/article/30820833#30820833
  - hooliganj · 2018-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2119535/article/30820852#30820852

    ### Do faction units like the Muaat Prototype War Sun count as their base unit type?
    - id: `faction-units-count-as-base-type` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — a faction unit counts as its base unit type for every purpose, so the Prototype War Sun is a war sun. Only the researched upgrade card is a technology: Prototype War Sun II counts as a war sun technology, but the Prototype War Sun I printed on the faction sheet does not.
      - Clipper · 2022-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2910669/article/40561211#40561211
      - KimAndersen1982 · 2022-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2910669/article/40562480#40562480

### What do the small arrows and the dots by the combat value on a faction sheet mean?
- id: `faction-sheet-arrows-and-dots` — grade: Per the rulebook — Unit Attributes
- A: An arrow marks a value that improves when you research that unit's upgrade. Each dot or burst by a combat value is one die the unit rolls, so two dots means two dice.
  - TwoAMArts · 2023-05-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3080111/article/42240927#42240927
  - jeffwagner · 2023-05-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3080111/article/42241949#42241949
  - AgentDrake · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886738/article/27477257#27477257

    ### Are fighter and infantry tokens limited, and what are they for?
    - id: `fighter-and-infantry-tokens` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Tokens are unlimited, but each stack of tokens must sit with at least one plastic piece of the same type. So your plastic fighters and infantry limit how many places you can have them, not how many you have. You may swap a plastic piece on the board for a token at any time to free the plastic for use elsewhere.
      - Share · 2020-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2373906/article/34101973#34101973
      - TerTer · 2020-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2373906/article/34102025#34102025
      - Clipper · 2018-11-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2096053/article/30497684#30497684
      - hehaheho · 2021-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2730326/article/38515912#38515912
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2730326/article/38516269#38516269

    ### Is there a limit to how many infantry can be on one planet?
    - id: `infantry-limit-per-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — any number of infantry can be on a planet. You are only limited by your components, and tokens can stand in for extra infantry.
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2479450/article/35512999#35512999
      - GIIANT · 2020-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2479450/article/35514874#35514874

    ### What happens if I would gain a command token but have none left in my reinforcements?
    - id: `command-tokens-are-limited` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: You don't gain it. Your 16 command tokens are a hard limit. Tokens you spend, such as for a secondary ability, go back to your reinforcements, not to your command sheet.
      - donmakaron · 2018-07-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2034549/article/29656027#29656027
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2034549/article/29656668#29656668
      - IncrediSteve · 2025-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3558589/article/46466863#46466863
      - zamoose · 2025-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3558589/article/46467155#46467155

    ### What if an effect makes me place a command token from my reinforcements, such as Diplomacy or a retreat, and I have none there?
- id: `must-place-command-token-none-in-reinforcements` — grade: Community consensus
- A: You take one of your choice from your command sheet instead. Component actions are different: one that cannot be fully resolved cannot be played at all, so it can't target you if it needs a token you don't have.
  - possumman · 2017-12-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907110/article/27792935#27792935
  - thomasrobb · 2017-12-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907110/article/27793319#27793319
  - DauntlessK · 2020-05-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2419816/article/34707886#34707886
  - Marcial · 2020-05-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2419816/article/34707902#34707902

    ### If my reinforcements are empty, must I take units from the board to place a new one?
- id: `taking-units-from-board-when-out` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the rules let you remove a unit from the board to place it elsewhere, but you are not required to. This matters for effects such as L1Z1X Assimilate or Swords to Plowshares.
  - Kinan_rod · 2019-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2222161/article/32248016#32248016
  - Clipper · 2019-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2222161/article/32248032#32248032


## Setup and Map Building

### Can Twilight Imperium 4 be played with two players?
- id: `two-player-game` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Not officially — the game is designed for 3 to 6 players and has no two-player rules. Two-player games rely on fan variants.
  - Kinan_rod · 2019-06-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2225080/article/32289706#32289706
  - iswearihaveajob · 2019-06-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2225080/article/32289828#32289828
  - AgentDrake · 2018-02-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1948400/article/28397419#28397419
  - possumman · 2018-02-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1948400/article/28397185#28397185
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843895/article/26841449#26841449

### How are factions, colors and seats chosen at setup?
- id: `choosing-factions-colors-seats` — grade: Community consensus
- A: However your group likes — the rules deliberately leave the method open. Free choice, random draw, or dealing each player a few factions to pick from are all fine. If you pick in turn, go clockwise from the speaker, as with other unspecified orders.
  - Clipper · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916986/article/27922794#27922794
  - Clipper · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916986/article/27922871#27922871
  - hooliganj · 2017-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1830904/article/26654557#26654557
  - Clipper · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953990/article/28487970#28487970
  - Old_Man_gi · 2026-03-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3685890/article/47492257#47492257

### When building the galaxy, can I look at my dealt system tiles and choose which one to place?
- id: `look-at-dealt-system-tiles` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — the dealt tiles are effectively a hand. You see them all and pick which tile to place and where on each turn, within the placement restrictions, which lets you put poor systems near your opponents.
  - Marcial · 2020-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2562386/article/36548738#36548738
  - IncrediSteve · 2019-03-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2176554/article/31615767#31615767
  - Clipper · 2019-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2148912/article/31223742#31223742
  - TomBoombs · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917319/article/27928942#27928942
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904380/article/27748645#27748645
  - Tompy · 2019-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2295910/article/33184373#33184373

### During galaxy setup, when can anomalies go next to each other, and can they go next to Mecatol Rex?
- id: `anomaly-and-wormhole-placement-restriction` — grade: Per the rulebook — Setup (Advanced Game)
- A: Anomalies may only go next to another anomaly when you have no other legal option, and holding any non-anomaly tile counts as an option. Matching wormholes follow the same rule. Nothing stops an anomaly from going next to Mecatol Rex.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-08-24 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - TomBoombs · 2017-08-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1835992/article/26728977#26728977
  - sigmazero13 · 2022-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2901961/article/40468469#40468469
  - MollyMae · 2019-03-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2176554/article/31615862#31615862

    ### Are red-backed empty systems anomalies for the setup placement rule?
- id: `red-backed-empty-systems-not-anomalies` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — only tiles with a red border on the front are anomalies. The red back only matters for dealing, so empty systems may go next to anomalies.
  - donmakaron · 2019-06-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2218246/article/32189045#32189045
  - YinBro · 2019-06-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2218246/article/32189434#32189434
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907990/article/27802102#27802102
  - trud · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907990/article/27802140#27802140
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907990/article/27803520#27803520

### Is it a problem if an alpha or beta wormhole ends up on the map without its partner?
- id: `unmatched-wormhole-on-map` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — that is a legal galaxy. The lone wormhole just has nothing to connect to, though it is still a wormhole system for Creuss and for agendas or cards that care about wormholes. Swapping tiles to fix it is a house rule.
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1898423/article/27661755#27661755
  - maverikki · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892702/article/27565041#27565041
  - rubberchicken · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892702/article/27565207#27565207

### Is a randomly built galaxy supposed to be balanced?
- id: `random-map-balance` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the standard placement method isn't meant to produce equal slices, so an uneven map is still correct. Groups wanting balance use fixed or drafted maps.
  - christhomas226 · 2021-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607364/article/37073246#37073246
  - donmakaron · 2021-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607364/article/37073818#37073818

### Is the gravity rift tile only for four-player games?
- id: `gravity-rift-any-player-count` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it only shows up in the four-player Learn to Play map because that map uses nearly every tile. In a normal setup it can be dealt at any player count.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2009117/article/29305251#29305251

### In a five-player game, which players get starting trade goods?
- id: `five-player-starting-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
- A: In the standard five-player layout, the players whose home systems sit closer to their neighbors receive trade goods as compensation; the others get none. Since the galaxy can't be built until home positions are known, decide who sits where (and so who gets them) first. The Mentak can't pillage these, since nobody is a neighbor during setup.
  - MonkeyKnifeFight · 2017-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1839014/article/26777067#26777067
  - Fadeout4 · 2017-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1839014/article/26776123#26776123
  - Clipper · 2019-10-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2299818/article/33233266#33233266
  - MollyMae · 2019-10-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2299818/article/33246409#33246409
  - Avemo3 · 2018-02-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937512/article/28228683#28228683
  - csouth154 · 2019-10-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2299818/article/33232657#33232657

      ### Do players start the game with commodities, trade goods or action cards?
      - id: `no-starting-commodities` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — nobody starts with commodities, trade goods or action cards. The only exception is the trade goods some players get in the standard five-player setup.
        - lebowskiachiever · 2020-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2456403/article/35215157#35215157
        - GrumblingGamer · 2020-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2456403/article/35215223#35215223
        - AgentDrake · 2018-11-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095923/article/30496133#30496133
        - Clipper · 2018-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947274/article/28378957#28378957
        - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1840742/article/26801843#26801843
        - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843354/article/26833169#26833169

### Do factions start with their faction technologies, and does adding them remove any generic technologies?
- id: `faction-technologies-at-start` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No to both. Faction technologies must be researched or otherwise gained; you only own the starting technologies listed on your faction sheet. The Nekro Virus is the exception, since its two Valefar Assimilator technologies are listed as starting technologies. Non-unit faction technologies, such as the Xxcha's, are simply added to your deck without removing anything.
  - AgentDrake · 2025-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3559123/article/46471670#46471670
  - Dulkal · 2019-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2339047/article/33677221#33677221
  - Volverbot · 2019-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2339047/article/33677226#33677226

    ### Can I put all my starting ground forces on one home planet, and do I control the empty one?
- id: `starting-units-on-home-planets` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — split them however you like, all on one planet if you wish. You control every planet in your home system regardless. Put your space dock on the planet that suits your production best.
  - IncrediSteve · 2026-04-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3700363/article/47609837#47609837
  - Marcial · 2026-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3700363/article/47613308#47613308
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-05-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2203702/article/31992178#31992178
  - csouth154 · 2019-05-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2203702/article/31992207#31992207

### Which promissory notes does each player start with?
- id: `promissory-notes-at-setup` — grade: Per the rulebook — Setup (Advanced Game)
- A: The four generic notes of your color plus your faction's unique note.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-17 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903426/article/27741080#27741080

### Are there errors in the Learn to Play setup illustrations?
- id: `learn-to-play-misprints` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes, two known ones. The Learn to Play shows the wrong resource and influence values for Jord; the planet card and system tile are correct. A starting-units example also doesn't match the faction's real starting units, so use the list on your faction sheet.
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1846159/article/26875974#26875974
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868048/article/40087884#40087884

### How do the two Creuss tiles work at setup, and where do their starting units go?
- id: `creuss-home-system-setup` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Place the Creuss Gate (tile 17) where a home system would normally go and keep the Creuss home system tile in your play area, off the map but visible. Your starting units go in the home system tile. The Creuss Gate has no planet and so no planet card; the card is for the planet Creuss.
  - maverikki · 2017-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899462/article/27675706#27675706
  - Clipper · 2017-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899462/article/27675845#27675845
  - pkitty · 2024-07-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3341043/article/44627224#44627224
  - GrumblingGamer · 2019-01-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2135135/article/31029942#31029942
  - AgentDrake · 2020-05-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2429647/article/34845329#34845329

### When are galactic events chosen at setup?
- id: `codex-galactic-events-setup` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Before anything else, including building the galaxy, since events can change how the galaxy and home systems are set up.
  - zamoose · 2025-07-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3549547/article/46389705#46389705
  - hooliganj · 2025-07-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3549547/article/46390545#46390545


    ## Strategy Phase and Strategy Cards

    ### How do strategy cards work in three- and four-player games?
    - id: `two-strategy-cards-in-3-4-player` — grade: Per the rulebook — 1. Strategy Phase
    - A: Each player picks two strategy cards, in a second pass of the selection. In a four-player game every card is taken; with three players two cards remain and collect trade goods as usual.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2022-07-31 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Cacotopos · 2022-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2908633/article/40541723#40541723
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900034/article/27683494#27683494
      - DeePee · 2017-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900034/article/27689647#27689647

    ### With two strategy cards, which one sets my initiative, and must I use the lower card first?
    - id: `initiative-with-two-strategy-cards` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only your lower-numbered card sets your initiative. You still take one turn at a time like everyone else, never two in a row, and you may use either card first.
      - hooliganj · 2025-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3558097/article/46462918#46462918
      - TreacleMiner · 2025-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3558097/article/46463060#46463060
      - Clipper · 2020-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2517925/article/35994768#35994768
      - mpappy · 2023-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3053864/article/41978380#41978380
      - McBartsomeville · 2023-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3053864/article/41978381#41978381

    ### Does a strategy card's primary ability cost a command token, and does the Leadership secondary?
    - id: `strategy-primary-costs-no-token` — grade: Per the rulebook — Strategic Action
    - A: No to both. A primary ability never costs a token. Secondaries cost a strategy pool token, except Leadership's, where you just spend influence: 1 command token per 3 influence.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2025-03-14 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Cacotopos · 2025-03-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3478313/article/45804665#45804665
      - AgentDrake · 2021-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594370/article/36913978#36913978
      - jeffwagner · 2021-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594370/article/36913986#36913986

    ### Which parts of a strategy card's primary ability are mandatory?
- id: `strategy-card-abilities-mandatory-parts` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Resolve the card top to bottom and do everything it says unless it uses 'may' or 'or'. Leadership's 3 tokens, choosing a Diplomacy system, Trade's replenish (for you and the players you pick), removing a token with Warfare if you can, the first Technology research and naming a new speaker with Politics are all required. Extras that cost you something, such as more Leadership tokens or a second technology for 6 resources, are optional, and Imperial never forces you to pay to score.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707444/article/38256883#38256883
  - hooliganj · 2025-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3591364/article/46729197#46729197
  - Falkman · 2025-10-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3591364/article/46734549#46734549
  - selwyth · 2020-03-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2393709/article/34355337#34355337
  - McBartsomeville · 2023-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3015922/article/41596465#41596465
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2442779/article/35035673#35035673

### Does Leadership let me redistribute the command tokens already on my sheet?
- id: `leadership-no-redistribution` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — you only place the newly gained tokens in any pools. Redistributing existing tokens happens in the status phase and with the Warfare primary.
  - hooliganj · 2019-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2138851/article/31088099#31088099

    ### How does the revised Diplomacy strategy card work?
    - id: `diplomacy-revised-card` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The revised card (in the Living Rules Reference errata, later printings and Prophecy of Kings) lets the primary player also ready up to 2 exhausted planets they control, giving them the same refresh as the secondary. The secondary readies up to 2 exhausted planets you control for a strategy token. The older section text in the LRR wasn't updated, but the errata governs. The revised card is recommended for base-only games too.
      - pkitty · 2020-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/34863555#34863555
      - andrewvcook · 2020-05-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2426875/article/34804898#34804898
      - Clipper · 2020-05-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2426875/article/34814468#34814468
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-05-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2426875/article/34809454#34809454
      - zamoose · 2018-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2061316/article/30018234#30018234
      - manatee_x · 2022-07-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2900040/article/40446901#40446901

    ### Which system can I choose with Diplomacy, and can it be Mecatol Rex?
- id: `diplomacy-which-system` — grade: Per the rulebook — 1. Strategy Phase
- A: Any system containing a planet you control, except the Mecatol Rex system, which can never be chosen even if you control Mecatol Rex.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2023-01-05 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - joespleen · 2022-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2998711/article/41426764#41426764
  - DistantOasis · 2022-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2998711/article/41426947#41426947
  - CarrotVision3D · 2022-12-31 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014810/article/41426738#41426738

    ### What do the Diplomacy tokens do to other players?
- id: `diplomacy-effect-on-other-players` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Each other player places a command token from reinforcements in the chosen system, so they can't activate it for the rest of the round. Nothing moves, and the system is not activated. A player who already has a token there doesn't add another. A player with none in reinforcements takes one from their command sheet.
  - donmakaron · 2019-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2163955/article/31436495#31436495
  - rosie_187 · 2019-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2163955/article/31436496#31436496
  - McBartsomeville · 2021-08-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707132/article/38253081#38253081
  - Dulkal · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912913/article/27864370#27864370
  - Jagokok · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679725/article/37944694#37944694

    ### Can Warfare, Counterstroke or other effects remove a token placed by Diplomacy?
    - id: `removing-diplomacy-tokens` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — once placed it is an ordinary command token, and the game doesn't track how it got there. Warfare, Counterstroke, Unexpected Action and similar effects can remove it.
      - toskk · 2025-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3527885/article/46215152#46215152
      - hooliganj · 2025-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3527885/article/46215175#46215175
      - Sande24 · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912913/article/27864368#27864368
      - Dulkal · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912913/article/27864370#27864370

    ### Who can I make speaker with Politics, and is it mandatory?
- id: `politics-choosing-speaker` — grade: Per the rulebook — 1. Strategy Phase
- A: You must choose a new speaker, and it must be someone other than the current speaker; that can be you if you aren't already the speaker. The speaker token doesn't rotate on its own and only changes hands this way, so if nobody takes Politics the speaker stays the same.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-01-02 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - csouth154 · 2019-01-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2123140/article/31031310#31031310
  - AgentDrake · 2019-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2123140/article/31031469#31031469
  - Jimb0v · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1863356/article/27151277#27151277
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1840742/article/26801843#26801843

    ### Can the speaker token be traded or sold?
    - id: `selling-the-speaker-token` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Not in a transaction — it isn't a tradeable component. But the Politics player can take payment, or make deals, in exchange for naming someone speaker. Only trade goods, commodities and promissory notes can change hands, and a payment is a transaction, so it must be with a neighbor at that moment.
      - AgentDrake · 2020-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2478865/article/35505508#35505508
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2478865/article/35505515#35505515
      - hooliganj · 2024-04-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3286431/article/44140642#44140642
      - Marcial · 2024-04-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3286431/article/44141961#44141961
      - Marcial · 2020-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2356552/article/33885077#33885077
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2043758/article/29785660#29785660

    ### Can Politics look at and rearrange the agenda deck before anyone takes Mecatol Rex?
    - id: `politics-agenda-deck-before-mecatol` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the agenda deck exists from the start and nothing restricts that part of Politics before the agenda phase begins.
      - AgentDrake · 2019-11-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2304857/article/33290864#33290864
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2304857/article/33297012#33297012
      - Clipper · 2019-10-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2296611/article/33190858#33190858

      ### After Politics, is it public whether the agenda cards went to the top or bottom of the deck?
      - id: `politics-top-or-bottom-public` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — everyone can see where each card goes; only the faces stay secret. You also can't show the cards themselves to other players, since you may only reveal hidden components you own, but you can tell them what they are (truthfully or not).
        - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554121/article/36440000#36440000
        - Clipper · 2018-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2121729/article/30845287#30845287
        - hooliganj · 2018-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2121729/article/30845313#30845313
        - pkitty · 2021-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2736009/article/38600485#38600485
        - Clipper · 2021-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2736009/article/38603638#38603638
        - Saboter · 2021-10-04 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2736009/article/38577694#38577694

    ### Do I pay resources for the structures placed with Construction?
    - id: `construction-structures-free` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — space docks and PDS have no cost. Construction places them; it isn't production, so production rules don't apply. The primary places a PDS or space dock and then also a PDS.
      - Clipper · 2019-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2122704/article/30855388#30855388
      - Victor Mancha · 2019-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2122704/article/30855398#30855398
      - csouth154 · 2019-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2227242/article/32315876#32315876
      - ajmarshall · 2023-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3024578/article/41685466#41685466
      - halbower · 2020-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2442779/article/35035235#35035235

### Can I use the Construction secondary in a system that already has my command token, and does it lock the system?
- id: `construction-secondary-with-own-token` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes, you can use it there: you still spend a strategy token, but it goes to reinforcements instead of a second token in the system. Otherwise the spent token is placed in the system, which then counts as having your token for the rest of the round, so you can't activate it later. Either way you must build on a planet you control.
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891865/article/27550461#27550461
  - Bizud · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891865/article/27550468#27550468
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2442895/article/35036633#35036633
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-08-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2442895/article/35544993#35544993
  - zamoose · 2022-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801454/article/39329891#39329891
  - JesperPersson · 2017-11-19 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886997/article/27479649#27479649

### Can the Clan of Saar use Construction to place a Floating Factory in a system without a planet they control?
- id: `saar-construction-needs-planet` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — Construction still needs a planet you control. The Saar choose an eligible planet and the Floating Factory then goes in that system's space area, so an asteroid field or other system without your planet can't get one.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033437/article/29667289#29667289
  - hooliganj · 2018-07-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033437/article/29642013#29642013
