  - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933291/article/28161755#28161755
  - Polarstern · 2018-08-01 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033437/article/29663795#29663795

### Does the Warfare primary give me two extra command tokens?
- id: `warfare-primary-one-token` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — you remove one of your tokens from the board and gain that same token back to your command sheet, then redistribute. If you have no token on the board to remove, you gain nothing, though you may still play the card.
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2306331/article/33300734#33300734
  - byrdru · 2019-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2306331/article/33300544#33300544
  - UnalignedMagi · 2019-11-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2306331/article/33308347#33308347
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-07-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2463518/article/35313009#35313009
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-07-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2463518/article/35313689#35313689

    ### How does the Warfare secondary work?
    - id: `warfare-secondary-home-system` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Spend a strategy token to use the Production of one space dock in your home system. It doesn't activate the system and works even if your home system already has your token; if it doesn't, the new ships can still move out later that round. Only one dock can be used, so a Saar player whose docks have left home can't use it.
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886985/article/27479520#27479520
      - JesperPersson · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886985/article/27479569#27479569
      - GrumblingGamer · 2018-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1914755/article/27895700#27895700
      - Jagokok · 2020-08-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2480861/article/35532738#35532738
      - Jagokok · 2024-07-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3329493/article/44523043#44523043
      - pfischer · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1908074/article/27802962#27802962

    ### Must I research a second technology with the Technology primary?
- id: `technology-primary-second-tech-optional` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the second technology costs 6 resources and is optional, even though you must play your strategy card before passing.
  - McBartsomeville · 2023-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3015922/article/41596465#41596465
  - gpoirier · 2023-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3015922/article/41596462#41596462
  - Clipper · 2020-03-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2393709/article/34356690#34356690

    ### What do the Imperial abilities give, and when is Mecatol Rex control checked?
    - id: `imperial-secondary-secret-objective` — grade: Per the rulebook — 1. Strategy Phase
    - A: The primary scores one public objective you qualify for, then gives 1 victory point if you control Mecatol Rex, checked when you perform the strategic action; otherwise you draw a secret objective. The secondary draws one secret objective, not two to keep one. Scoring with Imperial doesn't stop you scoring a public objective in the status phase too.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-03-10 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Clipper · 2019-03-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2165181/article/31453556#31453556
      - Clipper · 2019-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2315706/article/33412374#33412374
      - Clipper · 2017-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1910114/article/27829805#27829805
      - pkitty · 2022-07-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2903823/article/40492650#40492650

  ### If Public Disgrace makes a player give back a strategy card, who gets its trade goods?
  - id: `public-disgrace-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Whoever ends up choosing that card. Trade goods are collected once the choice sticks, so they stay on the returned card for the next chooser.
    - hooliganj · 2019-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2175771/article/31603948#31603948
    - AgentDrake · 2019-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2175771/article/31602579#31602579
    - Marcial · 2019-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2175771/article/31603169#31603169
    - MollyMae · 2019-03-28 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2175771/article/31602488#31602488

    ### Can Public Disgrace be played against a strategy card swap from Quantum Datahub Node or Imperial Arbiter?
    - id: `public-disgrace-vs-swaps` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Public Disgrace triggers when a player chooses a strategy card, and those swaps at the end of the strategy phase aren't choices. Quantum Datahub Node can, however, answer Imperial Arbiter in the same end-of-phase window.
      - Nagual84 · 2021-05-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2653548/article/37638968#37638968
      - Clipper · 2021-05-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2653548/article/37639132#37639132
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586756/article/36820594#36820594
      - Clipper · 2021-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586756/article/36820616#36820616

    ### How does Hacan's Quantum Datahub Node swap strategy cards?
    - id: `quantum-datahub-node-swap` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Hacan chooses both which of their cards to give and which face-up card to take. Only the cards move: trade goods were already collected when the cards were picked, and the Naalu 0 token stays with the Naalu, which only their promissory note can move.
      - hooliganj · 2023-11-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3189947/article/43280735#43280735
      - sshiftyp · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587586/article/36828916#36828916
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1847729/article/26900515#26900515
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1847729/article/26901942#26901942
      - Painkeeper · 2019-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302511/article/33260453#33260453

    ### Must the Naalu place their 0 initiative token?
    - id: `naalu-zero-token-mandatory` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Telepathic has no 'may', so the Naalu always go first in initiative unless they've given the token away with Gift of Prescience.
      - MobRule · 2024-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3240135/article/43729294#43729294
      - TwoAMArts · 2024-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3240135/article/43729319#43729319

    ### What does Jol-Nar pay to use Brilliant, and does it work on other strategy cards?
- id: `jol-nar-brilliant-cost` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only the strategy token for the Technology secondary — not the 4 resources — and then they resolve the primary, with the optional second tech for 6 resources. It only applies to the Technology card.
  - korric2000 · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911992/article/27850039#27850039
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911992/article/27850071#27850071
  - Clipper · 2018-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933876/article/28166079#28166079
  - pkitty · 2018-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933876/article/28166083#28166083
  - McBartsomeville · 2022-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2869707/article/40117698#40117698

### In a three- or four-player game, does Political Stability let you keep both strategy cards?
- id: `political-stability-two-cards` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — you keep both and skip choosing next strategy phase. The Third Edition restriction about specific cards doesn't apply in Fourth Edition.
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302420/article/33259451#33259451
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302420/article/33259504#33259504


## Action Phase and Tactical Actions

    ### How many actions do I take per turn, and in what order must I use action types?
    - id: `one-action-per-turn` — grade: Per the rulebook — 2. Action Phase
    - A: Exactly one action per turn — strategic, tactical or component — or you pass. You may use them in any order over the round and repeat types on later turns; the only restriction is that you can't pass until you have used your strategy card (both cards in three- and four-player games).
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-06-07 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Clipper · 2020-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2443470/article/35043001#35043001
      - Dulkal · 2017-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900968/article/27699070#27699070
      - Clipper · 2019-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2224767/article/32285524#32285524
      - AgentDrake · 2020-08-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2480827/article/35531700#35531700

    ### Do I have to move ships or do anything after activating a system?
- id: `tactical-action-steps-optional` — grade: Per the rulebook — Tactical Action
- A: No — only placing the command token is required. Every later step is optional (combat aside, which is mandatory if both sides have ships), so you may activate a system just to fire Space Cannon, to stall, or even one nobody can move into. All the steps happen on that one turn, in order, before play passes on.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-06-07 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - ChimBlade · 2022-05-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2871263/article/40125931#40125931
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3222056/article/43574201#43574201
  - selwyth · 2023-06-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3094723/article/42390019#42390019
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2509173/article/35886402#35886402

    ### Can I activate a system that already contains my command token?
- id: `cannot-activate-system-with-own-token` — grade: Per the rulebook — Tactical Action
- A: No — however the token got there (your earlier activation, Diplomacy, Construction's secondary), you can't activate that system again until the token is removed, for example by the status phase, the Warfare primary or Unexpected Action. Other players' activations of nearby systems can still trigger your Space Cannon.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-08-22 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Jacky Blue Note · 2018-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921448/article/27985060#27985060
  - Kazadoom · 2018-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921448/article/27985249#27985249
  - pkitty · 2020-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2481380/article/35538049#35538049
  - Clipper · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904661/article/27754381#27754381

    ### How many systems can I activate in one tactical action, and why do I end up with tokens in many systems?
- id: `one-system-per-tactical-action` — grade: Community consensus
- A: One. Each tactical action spends one tactic token to activate one system. Your tokens accumulate on the board because the action phase goes around the table many times, and they stay until the status phase. Ships in, or produced in, a system holding your token can't move out for the rest of the round.
  - oatesatm · 2017-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1906151/article/27781517#27781517
  - Clipper · 2017-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1906151/article/27781625#27781625
  - MollyMae · 2022-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801036/article/39324930#39324930
  - christhomas226 · 2021-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2628024/article/37339695#37339695
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-03-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2388402/article/34286509#34286509
  - zamoose · 2018-10-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2082965/article/30327991#30327991

    ### Does a system I activated stay the active system for the rest of the round?
- id: `system-active-only-during-action` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — it is the active system only for that tactical action. Your command token stays there until the status phase (or until an effect removes it), which is what stops you activating it again.
  - zamoose · 2022-10-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2943743/article/40892094#40892094
  - hooliganj · 2022-10-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2943743/article/40892200#40892200
  - Clipper · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904661/article/27754381#27754381

    ### Can a system ever hold two of my command tokens?
- id: `never-two-own-tokens-in-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — if an effect would place your token in a system that already has one, you don't place a second. Construction says the spent token goes to your reinforcements instead, and other effects work the same way.
  - Padish · 2018-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919264/article/27958614#27958614
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919264/article/27958634#27958634
  - Clipper · 2020-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2353256/article/33844279#33844279
  - Marcial · 2020-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2353256/article/33846041#33846041

      ### Do Diplomacy tokens, retreats or action cards like Ghost Ship or Plague count as activating a system?
      - id: `what-counts-as-activating` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — a system is activated only by placing a command token as the first step of a tactical action. Tokens placed by Diplomacy or a retreat and cards aimed at a system don't activate it, so they don't trigger Space Cannon Offense, Support for the Throne returns or other 'activate' effects.
        - Cacotopos · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587525/article/36827870#36827870
        - Clipper · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587525/article/36827901#36827901
        - Dulkal · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912913/article/27864370#27864370
        - zamoose · 2019-09-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2278511/article/32969267#32969267
        - AgentDrake · 2023-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3000964/article/41445299#41445299
        - pkitty · 2022-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2929299/article/40745434#40745434

    ### Can I move into a system, fight, invade and then produce there in the same tactical action?
    - id: `move-fight-invade-then-produce` — grade: Per the rulebook — Tactical Action
    - A: Yes — the steps run activation, movement, space combat, invasion, then production. Because production comes last, units produced in that action can't join its combat or land on planets that turn. Ships you moved in count toward your fleet pool when you produce.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2023-05-02 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - tommygunn2011 · 2022-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2995754/article/41403747#41403747
      - christhomas226 · 2020-07-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2461007/article/35272890#35272890
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1936377/article/28209147#28209147
      - GrumblingGamer · 2018-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2121262/article/30840366#30840366

    ### If another player activates a system with my space dock, can I produce there during their action?
- id: `only-active-player-produces` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — only the active player resolves the steps of a tactical action. Other players join in only through things like Space Cannon and combat. To build there, activate it on your own turn.
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2306395/article/33301364#33301364
  - Weltenreiter · 2019-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2306395/article/33301368#33301368

    ### Can I produce in a system that already holds my command token from an earlier turn?
- id: `produce-in-system-with-own-token` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Not with a tactical action, since you can't activate it. Only effects that don't need activation, such as the Warfare secondary in your home system, let you produce there.
  - Keineui · 2023-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3000208/article/41438104#41438104
  - HP12C_guy · 2023-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3000208/article/41438128#41438128

    ### Must I resolve my strategy card right away, and do I need command tokens to do it?
- id: `when-to-take-strategic-action` — grade: Per the rulebook — 2. Action Phase
- A: No to both. You choose which turn to take your strategic action, as long as it is before you pass. Strategic and component actions need no command tokens, so a player out of tokens can still play their remaining strategy card and then pass.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2025-08-12 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - McBartsomeville · 2025-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3557981/article/46461949#46461949
  - jsciv · 2018-07-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033330/article/29641099#29641099
  - Pete Walsh · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903986/article/27742763#27742763
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903986/article/27742773#27742773

      ### When a strategy card is played, do other players resolve the secondary right away or on their own turn?
      - id: `secondaries-resolve-immediately` — grade: Per the rulebook — Strategic Action
      - A: Right away, clockwise from the active player, as part of the active player's strategic action — it doesn't use their turns. Afterwards play passes to the next player in initiative order. End-of-turn effects can't be slipped in between the primary and the secondaries.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2025-12-28 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - Tanhauser69 · 2025-12-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3634935/article/47077622#47077622
        - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900040/article/27683741#27683741
        - argon435 · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900040/article/27683775#27683775
        - hooliganj · 2023-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3035323/article/41792230#41792230

    ### Can I still resolve other players' strategy card secondaries after I have passed?
- id: `secondaries-after-passing` — grade: Per the rulebook — 2. Action Phase
- A: Yes — passing ends your turns, not your ability to follow secondaries by spending a strategy token.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-10-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - oatesatm · 2018-03-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1950267/article/28431624#28431624
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1950267/article/28431990#28431990
  - Nagual84 · 2020-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2509173/article/35886533#35886533

        ### When do 'at the start of your turn' abilities happen, and do they work in other phases?
        - id: `start-of-turn-abilities` — grade: Community consensus
        - A: Before you choose your action or pass, so they come before activating a system and before anything activation triggers, like Ceasefire. They only exist in the action phase: you don't have turns in the strategy, status or agenda phases.
          - IncrediSteve · 2021-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2676842/article/37910529#37910529
          - Clipper · 2021-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2676842/article/37911445#37911445
          - Clipper · 2020-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2344964/article/33739596#33739596

      ### Are component actions limited to once per round?
      - id: `component-actions-no-round-limit` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — each one simply uses your action for the turn. The only limits are those printed on the component itself.
        - csouth154 · 2019-05-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2212812/article/32117053#32117053
        - Clipper · 2019-05-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2212812/article/32117104#32117104

    ### Can I play a component action that I can't fully resolve?
    - id: `component-action-must-fully-resolve` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — a component action can only be performed if you can carry out the whole ability. You can't play Signal Jamming on a player with no tokens in reinforcements, use Sling Relay with nowhere to produce, use Uprising on an exhausted planet, or stall with Yssaril's ability without a card to discard. Unstable Planet is different, because its effects are joined by 'and'.
      - Clipper · 2017-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901183/article/27702312#27702312
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901183/article/27702391#27702391
      - zamoose · 2025-11-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3603628/article/46827859#46827859
      - Clipper · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953751/article/28484775#28484775
      - Padish · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27939725#27939725
      - Clipper · 2018-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/28386987#28386987

    `  ### Can I play an action card whose effect would amount to nothing, like Plague on a planet with no infantry?
      - id: `action-card-with-zero-effect` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — doing the card's text with a result of zero still counts as resolving it, and 'up to' includes zero. Players relaying an official reply confirmed Plague for zero. You must still meet any targeting requirement and do every part that is possible.
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280071/article/34312353#34312353
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280071/article/34312411#34312411
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280071/article/32994955#32994955
        - Clipper · 2019-10-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280071/article/33206093#33206093
        - AgentDrake · 2019-09-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2276445/article/32943901#32943901
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2276445/article/32963501#32963501`

    ### Can I remove my own units from the board when I want to?
    - id: `removing-own-units` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — units leave the board only through the game (destroyed, captured and so on). The one exception is taking a unit off the board when you need to place one and none are in reinforcements.
      - chrisdk · 2023-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3202159/article/43398308#43398308
      - isterbat · 2023-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3202159/article/43398357#43398357


## Status Phase

    ### In the status phase, do I get back all the command tokens I used on the board?
- id: `command-tokens-in-status-phase` — grade: Per the rulebook — 3. Status Phase
- A: No — tokens on the board go back to your reinforcements, and you then gain just 2 tokens to place on your command sheet. Spent tokens are not refunded, so to grow your supply you need Leadership or similar effects. Newly gained tokens may go in any pool.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2026-05-22 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Clipper · 2017-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1897480/article/27645064#27645064
  - Painkeeper · 2020-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2453865/article/35181556#35181556
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1840742/article/26801843#26801843

    ### In what order do players score objectives in the status phase?
- id: `status-phase-scoring-order` — grade: Per the rulebook — 3. Status Phase
- A: In initiative order, not from the speaker. The Naalu's 0 token therefore makes them score first, unless they gave the token away with their promissory note.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-01-06 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - zamoose · 2018-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092325/article/30447984#30447984
  - Clipper · 2019-12-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2331124/article/33590820#33590820
  - IncrediSteve · 2019-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2331124/article/33592254#33592254

    ### Can I score more than one public objective in a single status phase?
- id: `one-public-one-secret-per-status-phase` — grade: Per the rulebook — 3. Status Phase
- A: No — each status phase you may score at most one public and one secret objective, both on your scoring turn. The Imperial primary is the main way to score an extra public objective, during the action phase.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-12-11 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2257483/article/32716547#32716547
  - Tigerherz · 2019-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2257483/article/32716795#32716795

### Does Arborec's Mitosis place an infantry on every planet they control?
- id: `arborec-mitosis-one-infantry` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — it places a single infantry on one planet you control each status phase.
  - Krizzik · 2022-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2996240/article/41407335#41407335
  - HP12C_guy · 2022-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2996240/article/41407477#41407477
  - KimAndersen1982 · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893090/article/27571107#27571107
  - TomBoombs · 2018-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1930227/article/28111311#28111311
  - Tregannon · 2018-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1930227/article/28111979#28111979

  ### If two players have abilities at the same point of the status phase, such as both using Wormhole Generator, who goes first?
  - id: `status-phase-same-timing-abilities` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Initiative order: the player with the lower initiative number resolves first. The status phase runs in initiative order, and simultaneous abilities follow it.
    - sigmazero13 · 2019-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2200935/article/31953537#31953537
    - MollyMae · 2019-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2200935/article/31953845#31953845

      ### In what order are action cards drawn in the status phase?
      - id: `action-card-draw-order` — grade: Per the rulebook — 3. Status Phase
      - A: In initiative order, though it rarely matters in practice.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - pkitty · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904465/article/27750001#27750001

### Are control tokens removed along with command tokens in the status phase?
- id: `control-tokens-stay-in-status-phase` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — only command tokens come off the board. Control tokens stay on planets until you lose control of them.
  - brettellmd · 2018-04-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1970535/article/28738361#28738361


## Agenda Phase and Voting

    ### When does the agenda phase happen, and when are planets readied?
    - id: `agenda-phase-timing-and-readying` — grade: Per the rulebook — 4. Agenda Phase
    - A: Once the custodians token is off Mecatol Rex, the agenda phase follows the status phase every round. Planets ready in the status phase, so they can vote, but not between the two agendas: influence you spend on the first vote is gone for the second. Planets ready again at the end of the agenda phase for the next action phase.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-11-26 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890700/article/27535009#27535009
      - Clipper · 2018-04-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1982680/article/28927983#28927983
      - Santiago · 2017-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1895962/article/27614720#27614720
      - Johnnymaxx · 2017-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891940/article/27551989#27551989

    ### How does voting work, and does it go around the table more than once?
- id: `how-voting-works` — grade: Per the rulebook — 4. Agenda Phase
- A: Each player votes once per agenda, openly, starting to the speaker's left and ending with the speaker. On your turn you name an outcome and exhaust planets for that many votes, or abstain. Votes are committed as they are declared.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-02-06 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-02-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2146016/article/31184643#31184643
  - HP12C_guy · 2024-04-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3283782/article/44117474#44117474
  - hooliganj · 2024-04-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3283782/article/44117638#44117638
  - CBpegasus · 2017-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1847859/article/26902426#26902426

    ### Can I spend only part of a planet's influence when voting?
- id: `vote-with-part-of-planet-influence` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — exhausting a planet casts all of its influence as votes. You can only fine-tune your total by choosing which planets to exhaust.
  - MollyMae · 2019-10-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2301369/article/33246333#33246333
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2301369/article/33247337#33247337

    ### Can trade goods be spent to cast votes?
- id: `trade-goods-cannot-vote` — grade: Per the rulebook — 4. Agenda Phase
- A: No — trade goods count as influence for everything except voting. You can still pay them to other players to sway their votes.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-08-16 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1830979/article/26656079#26656079

      ### Can I abstain, and does casting zero votes let me use Distinguished Councilor or Bribery?
      - id: `abstaining-and-zero-votes` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: You can always abstain, whether or not you have votes available. Casting zero votes is the same as abstaining, so cards that add to votes you cast, like Distinguished Councilor, need at least one real vote first; a single 1-influence planet is enough.
        - StrangeBeacon · 2026-02-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3663296/article/47304859#47304859
        - IncrediSteve · 2020-06-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2453062/article/35170096#35170096
        - Clipper · 2018-03-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946418/article/28436113#28436113
        - ksfga · 2018-03-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946418/article/28427636#28427636
        - Clipper · 2021-06-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2675853/article/37900687#37900687
        - Clipper · 2017-12-17 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902973/article/27730114#27730114

    ### What happens if the vote ties or everyone abstains?
- id: `ties-and-everyone-abstaining` — grade: Per the rulebook — 4. Agenda Phase
- A: The speaker picks which tied outcome resolves; at 0 to 0 that means any eligible outcome. The agenda is never discarded for lack of votes. The speaker's choice is not a vote, so it doesn't count as voting for that outcome, but riders that predicted it still pay out.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-01-05 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Clipper · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913539/article/27874766#27874766
  - Clipper · 2018-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2037415/article/29698082#29698082
  - Old_Man_gi · 2022-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2796996/article/39279584#39279584
  - denmla · 2018-01-06 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913539/article/27890022#27890022

      ### Can a speaker who can't vote, such as the Nekro Virus or a speaker who played a rider, still break ties?
      - id: `speaker-who-cannot-vote-breaks-ties` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — breaking a tie isn't voting, so the speaker always does it.
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273270/article/32904191#32904191
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273270/article/32904664#32904664
        - Sande24 · 2022-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2819267/article/39530194#39530194
        - Clipper · 2017-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900311/article/27688459#27688459
        - hooliganj · 2018-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2114395/article/30754182#30754182

      ### Does an agenda's outcome affect everyone, or only the players who voted for it?
      - id: `agenda-outcome-applies-to-whom` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Everyone, unless the card itself limits the effect to players who voted a certain way, as Mutiny does. Abstaining doesn't exempt you.
        - zamoose · 2019-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2125450/article/30890938#30890938
        - hooliganj · 2019-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2125450/article/30891056#30891056
        - IncrediSteve · 2021-05-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2659938/article/37717617#37717617

    ### What is the difference between a law and a directive?
- id: `laws-versus-directives` — grade: Per the rulebook — 4. Agenda Phase
- A: If a law's For (or elect) outcome wins, the card stays in play as a lasting effect, even if part of it is one-time. A law's Against outcome, and either outcome of a directive, resolves once and the card is discarded.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-01-12 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Marcial · 2020-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2347337/article/33769621#33769621
  - Ender02 · 2023-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3024565/article/41685243#41685243
  - ajmarshall · 2023-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3024565/article/41685453#41685453

    ### How do 'Elect Planet' agendas work, and can an uncontrolled planet be chosen?
- id: `elect-planet-agendas` — grade: Community consensus
- A: There's no nomination step: on your vote you back an eligible planet someone already named, name a new one, or abstain. Only planets some player controls are eligible, and only those that already have the required trait, since electing never changes a planet's trait.
  - ksfga · 2020-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2384763/article/34235107#34235107
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017891/article/29427893#29427893
  - Clipper · 2020-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2523424/article/36062213#36062213
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900628/article/27693188#27693188
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-05-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2419818/article/34708081#34708081
  - IndyBart · 2020-03-09 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2384763/article/34235053#34235053

    ### When must Rider action cards be played, and can we discuss the agenda first?
- id: `when-riders-are-played` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Riders go after the agenda is revealed and before the first vote is cast. Talking and negotiating don't close that window, and the speaker can't rush to the vote to shut riders out: everyone gets their chance to respond.
  - jeffwagner · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/27582291#27582291
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/27583298#27583298
  - hooliganj · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/27563805#27563805
  - hooliganj · 2018-04-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983065/article/28936619#28936619
  - hooliganj · 2020-03-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2381220/article/34191398#34191398
  - Mordenthral · 2017-11-29 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/27563314#27563314

    ### Do 'when an agenda is revealed' effects like Veto and Quash come before Riders?
    - id: `when-effects-before-after-effects` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — 'when' effects (Quash, Veto, Political Favor, Political Secret) resolve first, then 'after' effects (Riders, Assassinate Representative, Galactic Threat), then voting. Veto and Political Favor were errata'd to 'when' timing to match Quash, so they cancel an agenda before anyone has played riders on it.
      - Clipper · 2018-04-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983065/article/28944137#28944137
      - hooliganj · 2018-04-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983065/article/28948685#28948685
      - maverikki · 2017-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893943/article/27773156#27773156
      - Clipper · 2017-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27773824#27773824
      - pkitty · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/28488700#28488700
      - Clipper · 2019-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2270520/article/32872802#32872802

    ### In what order do players play riders and other agenda-phase cards?
- id: `rider-playing-order` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Starting with the speaker and going clockwise, each player plays one card or passes, and it keeps going around until everyone passes in a row. Passing doesn't lock you out: if someone plays a card afterwards, you can still play one.
  - Clipper · 2019-10-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2292414/article/33141994#33141994
  - Clipper · 2019-10-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2292414/article/33148840#33148840
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2292414/article/33198187#33198187
  - Clipper · 2018-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2005282/article/29249873#29249873
  - MollyMae · 2019-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2205586/article/32018745#32018745
  - Ragnarwarbar · 2019-10-15 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2292414/article/33154116#33154116

    ### Do I have to reveal my Rider and its prediction when I play it?
- id: `riders-are-public` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — action cards are read aloud and played openly, so the whole card, reward included, and your prediction are public. The prediction is made when you play the card, before voting, never after the result is known.
  - hooliganj · 2025-07-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3540057/article/46314939#46314939
  - Falkman · 2025-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3534382/article/46269945#46269945
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911546/article/27844419#27844419
  - Geoff Watson · 2020-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2519743/article/36015127#36015127
  - AgentDrake · 2018-11-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2094154/article/30469554#30469554
  - AgentDrake · 2025-08-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3560679/article/46483883#46483883

    ### Can one player play several riders on the same agenda, on different outcomes?
    - id: `multiple-riders-one-agenda` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — FFG confirmed by email that you can play several riders in the same vote. Each rider is played on its own, so each can predict a different outcome, letting you cover both For and Against.
      - MATTPROV3 · 2017-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1870105/article/27463574#27463574
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-10-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1870105/article/27253438#27253438
      - Clipper · 2018-01-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911546/article/27854994#27854994
      - ksfga · 2019-02-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2146078/article/31185488#31185488
      - mliszewski · 2019-02-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2146078/article/31185510#31185510
      - pkitty · 2017-10-24 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1870105/article/27252787#27252787

  ### Who can play Bribery, and when?
  - id: `bribery-rules` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Only a player who actually voted. Per its errata, Bribery is played after the speaker votes and each trade good spent adds a vote to the outcome you voted for. A player who played a rider can't vote and so can't use it, nor can a speaker who abstained, but others who voted still can. If an Argent speaker votes first with Zeal, Bribery comes right after that vote.
    - hooliganj · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281582/article/33006106#33006106
    - Clipper · 2019-09-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281582/article/33006644#33006644
    - Bautazar · 2018-03-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1952573/article/28463738#28463738
    - DrewDecimal · 2020-06-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2442403/article/35029810#35029810
    - Clipper · 2021-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2709578/article/38279897#38279897
    - Clipper · 2021-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2710123/article/38289656#38289656

    ### Can a player hit by Assassinate Representative still play riders?
    - id: `assassinate-representative-riders` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it only stops that player voting; unlike Political Secret, it doesn't block action cards.
      - TomBoombs · 2018-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2037415/article/29696958#29696958
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1989197/article/29018347#29018347
      - TomBoombs · 2018-05-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1989197/article/29018731#29018731

      ### When an agenda outcome and a rider both award points, which resolves first?
      - id: `agenda-before-riders-resolution` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: The agenda's outcome resolves first, then the riders that predicted it. This can decide the game if the agenda's points push someone to the goal.
        - halbower · 2020-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2417840/article/34680572#34680572
        - halbower · 2020-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2417840/article/34680621#34680621
        - Clipper · 2018-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2061252/article/30017610#30017610

    ### How do Confusing and Confounding Legal Text interact with riders and Drive the Debate?
    - id: `legal-text-cards-and-riders` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Both only work on 'Elect Player' agendas, and only the player originally elected can play them. They act before the outcome resolves, so riders and agenda-phase secrets like Drive the Debate use the final elected player.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2714161/article/38344059#38344059
      - Clipper · 2018-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915912/article/27905747#27905747
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915912/article/27906610#27906610
      - Clipper · 2023-02-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3036703/article/41802266#41802266
      - hooliganj · 2021-03-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2625728/article/37310474#37310474
      - McBartsomeville · 2023-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3180966/article/43199818#43199818

      ### What exactly does Political Secret stop the affected player from doing?
  - id: `political-secret-what-it-blocks` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: For that agenda, they can't vote, play action cards, or use optional abilities, those with 'may' or another choice. Mandatory and passive abilities still apply, such as Saar's Scavenge, Nekro's Propagation or Hacan's Arbiters, and laws they own still work.
    - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707451/article/38256798#38256798
    - Clipper · 2021-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707451/article/38289548#38289548
    - Clipper · 2018-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946928/article/28377140#28377140
    - Clipper · 2019-06-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2217927/article/32185651#32185651
    - Clipper · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273220/article/32905118#32905118
    - antmarcar1991 · 2021-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722632/article/38427827#38427827

### If Political Secret is played and the agenda is then Quashed or Vetoed, does it carry over to the replacement?
- id: `political-secret-vs-quash` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — discarding the agenda counts as resolving it, so Political Secret ends and is wasted. The replacement is handled from scratch: it can be Quashed in turn, and players can make fresh transactions for it. Since they share timing, Xxcha may also Quash before anyone plays Political Secret on them.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2001558/article/29253729#29253729
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2001558/article/29345926#29345926
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2001558/article/29196487#29196487
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2001558/article/29195660#29195660
  - Clipper · 2018-05-30 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2001558/article/29197010#29197010

    ### Can the Xxcha Quash more than once, including the replacement agenda?
    - id: `xxcha-quash-repeatedly` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Quash can be used on any newly revealed agenda as long as Xxcha pays a strategy token each time, including the replacement Quash itself reveals. A Veto card, by contrast, is one card and one use.
      - IncrediSteve · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2153356/article/31285213#31285213
      - AgentDrake · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2153356/article/31286077#31286077
      - Marcial · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2153356/article/31286456#31286456
      - Clipper · 2021-06-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2683620/article/37990079#37990079
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2269745/article/32861470#32861470
      - csouth154 · 2019-02-18 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2153356/article/31285883#31285883

    ### When does the Nekro make its Galactic Threat prediction, and what can it predict?
    - id: `nekro-galactic-threat-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: In the same window as riders: after 'when revealed' effects such as Veto or Quash, before anyone votes. It predicts an outcome; abstaining isn't one. The Nekro may also play riders on the same or different outcomes.
      - Clipper · 2018-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/28350545#28350545
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/28348374#28348374
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/28367919#28367919
      - Clipper · 2018-02-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/28281704#28281704
      - Clipper · 2020-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2491537/article/35670740#35670740
      - Clipper · 2020-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542866/article/36300265#36300265

    ### Does Galactic Threat gain a technology if nobody voted for the predicted outcome?
    - id: `nekro-galactic-threat-no-voters` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — the Nekro gains a technology from a player who voted for that outcome. If it won through the speaker's tie-break, Committee Formation or a Legal Text change, with no player voting for it, there is nobody to take from.
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/27183335#27183335
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/28656216#28656216
      - hooliganj · 2018-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2037415/article/29698316#29698316
      - Clipper · 2021-08-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2716411/article/38357500#38357500
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2774032/article/39021857#39021857
      - dontnormally · 2018-03-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1865712/article/28656106#28656106

    ### Can I exhaust planets for resources or technology specialties during the agenda phase?
    - id: `exhausting-planets-in-agenda-phase` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — nothing limits planets to voting then. You can spend them on things like Inheritance Systems or research from a Technology Rider, but those planets then can't vote. Exhausted cards other than planets don't ready at the end of the agenda phase.
      - UnalignedMagi · 2019-08-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2264947/article/32805334#32805334
      - Clipper · 2020-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2344964/article/33739596#33739596
      - SwissQueso · 2020-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2344964/article/33739314#33739314
      - Clipper · 2019-09-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2270520/article/32973393#32973393


## Agenda Cards

  ### If Shard of the Throne, The Crown of Emphidia or Holy Planet of Ixth is discarded or re-elected, does the previous owner lose its victory point?
  - id: `law-victory-points-kept-when-discarded` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — a point from these laws is lost only through the condition printed on the card. If the law is discarded some other way, such as Repeal Law, or re-elected via Miscount Disclosed, the old owner keeps the point; a newly elected owner gains one, and the same player re-elected scores again.
    - THLY · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286288/article/33066918#33066918
    - Clipper · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286288/article/33066487#33066487
    - Clipper · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954109/article/28491190#28491190
    - Ressless · 2024-05-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3294101/article/44205834#44205834
    - Clipper · 2021-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38310918#38310918
    - Santiago · 2019-10-02 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286288/article/33066054#33066054

    ### Do I lose The Crown of Emphidia if I lose a planet I control in another player's home system?
    - id: `crown-of-emphidia-home-system-only` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — only planets in your own home system count for it. Likewise, A New Constitution only exhausts the planets each player controls in their own home system.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - TomBoombs · 2021-08-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38372975#38372975

    ### If players are tied for the most or fewest points when Seed of an Empire resolves, who scores?
- id: `ties-for-most-or-fewest-points` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Every tied player gains the point. Effects aimed at the player with the most or fewest points hit everyone tied; the speaker doesn't break this kind of tie. Trading Support for the Throne after the votes can change who qualifies before it resolves. Under Colonial Redistribution, by contrast, the planet's controller chooses one player among those tied for fewest points.
  - Weltenreiter · 2018-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014739/article/29384079#29384079
  - aegiscruiser · 2018-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014739/article/29384934#29384934
  - Clipper · 2018-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014739/article/29384144#29384144
  - ajmarshall · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719699/article/38394568#38394568
  - Clipper · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719699/article/38394861#38394861
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331

    ### When Miscount Disclosed forces a revote on a law, does that law stay in effect during the revote?
    - id: `miscount-disclosed-law-stays-in-effect` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the law remains in play until the revote resolves, so Representative Government, for example, still limits everyone to 1 vote. The old outcome is replaced only when the new one resolves, so a new Demilitarized Zone planet replaces the old one rather than adding a second.
      - TomBoombs · 2021-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38310697#38310697
      - kaiteinomen · 2019-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2136731/article/32635572#32635572
      - Clipper · 2019-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2136731/article/32635893#32635893
      - Clipper · 2021-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38311128#38311128
      - Clipper · 2021-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38275766#38275766
      - MercifulBiscuit · 2019-01-23 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2136731/article/31054347#31054347

    ### How does the revote from Miscount Disclosed work?
    - id: `miscount-disclosed-procedure` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: First vote on Miscount Disclosed, with riders predicting only which law is chosen. Then vote on that law as if it had just been revealed, so Veto, Quash and a fresh round of riders can be played on it. Planets are not readied in between, and the revote doesn't replace the phase's second agenda, so a phase can have three votes.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1990979/article/29046692#29046692
      - Clipper · 2018-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1990979/article/29046729#29046729
      - TomBoombs · 2019-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2136731/article/31060936#31060936
      - Marcial · 2021-09-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719268/article/38389823#38389823
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911441/article/27844064#27844064

    ### How does a secret objective made public by Classified Document Leaks work?
    - id: `classified-document-leaks` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: While leaked it is a public objective and no longer counts toward its owner's secret objectives, so they may hold another secret. Nobody can score it twice, its original owner included. If the law is later discarded, the card returns to its owner as a secret, and anyone who scored it keeps the point.
      - Clipper · 2018-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092367/article/30450132#30450132
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092367/article/30448736#30448736
      - Xelvonar · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907841/article/27801339#27801339
      - Bizud · 2017-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907841/article/27808812#27808812
      - hooliganj · 2019-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2137555/article/31065523#31065523
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331

    ### What does Enforced Travel Ban do, and does it stop Space Cannon or the Creuss?
    - id: `enforced-travel-ban` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: For: alpha and beta wormholes have no effect on movement, but the systems stay adjacent for everything else, so Space Cannon still fires through them, as the errata confirms. It also cancels the adjacency from Lost Star Chart for movement. The Creuss ignore it, since no game effect can prevent Quantum Entanglement. Against: every PDS in or next to a system with any wormhole, gamma and delta included, is destroyed.
      - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
      - dheck · 2019-04-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2181342/article/31688067#31688067
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38256827#38256827
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38261101#38261101
      - alercah · 2020-09-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2511304/article/35911516#35911516
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2340566/article/33691965#33691965

    ### Which units does The Ixthian Artifact destroy?
    - id: `ixthian-artifact-adjacency` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: All units in the Mecatol Rex system, ships included, and 3 units of each player in every adjacent system (all of them if a player has fewer). Adjacency is the normal kind for everyone: a matching wormhole on Mecatol counts, but Creuss's Quantum Entanglement doesn't extend it. With Wormhole Reconstruction in play, alpha and beta systems both count.
      - Clipper · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2152935/article/31279913#31279913
      - Clipper · 2021-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38270389#38270389
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38261123#38261123
      - Milty · 2019-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2274467/article/32923493#32923493
      - zamoose · 2021-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2708012/article/38263251#38263251
      - Clipper · 2021-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38267404#38267404

    ### Does Arms Reduction touch ships other than dreadnoughts and cruisers, and what about capacity?
    - id: `arms-reduction-scope` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — For only cuts dreadnoughts beyond 2 and cruisers beyond 4. Afterwards check capacity; fighters and ground forces that no longer fit are removed, which is not the same as being destroyed.
      - pkitty · 2018-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1964092/article/28641831#28641831
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1964092/article/28641837#28641837
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903825/article/27740354#27740354
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331

    ### When Fleet Regulations passes, what happens to fleet pool tokens above 4 and ships over the limit?
    - id: `fleet-regulations-excess` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Excess fleet tokens return to reinforcements; you can't move them to other pools. Any ships over the new limit must be removed immediately. Abilities that raise fleet limits, like Letnev's, still add on top.
      - IncrediSteve · 2019-05-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2196105/article/31881400#31881400
      - ascuris · 2017-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894975/article/27600762#27600762
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894975/article/27600774#27600774

    ### How does Anti-Intellectual Revolution work?
    - id: `anti-intellectual-revolution` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: For: after you research a technology you must destroy one of your non-fighter ships; fighters don't count. It is an after-research effect, not a cost, so a player with no such ships can still research. The Nekro are unaffected, since Propagation gains tokens instead of researching. Against applies only at the start of the very next strategy phase, then the card is discarded.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331
      - Clipper · 2018-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920113/article/27967515#27967515
      - eurofabio · 2018-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920113/article/27967538#27967538
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-01-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2803675/article/39357498#39357498
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2803675/article/39358443#39358443

### Does Distinguished Councilor still add 5 votes under Representative Government?
- id: `representative-government-extra-votes` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Not with the newer printing, which says players can't cast additional votes. Under the original wording it did, since you still cast votes; groups with older cards should follow the reprint.
  - Fuzzychub · 2024-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017691/article/44451081#44451081
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-01 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017691/article/29426422#29426422

    ### Does Public Execution stop the elected player voting in the next agenda phase, and does Yssaril's Crafty save their hand?
    - id: `public-execution-effects` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both. The voting ban only covers that agenda phase. The discard comes from being elected, not from the hand limit, so Yssaril must discard too. Crafty does beat effects that cap hand size, such as Executive Sanctions.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - McBartsomeville · 2024-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3406780/article/45194771#45194771
      - pkitty · 2024-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3406780/article/45195686#45195686
      - MollyMae · 2019-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2157871/article/31345342#31345342

### Under Shared Research's Against outcome, what if I have no command tokens in reinforcements?
- id: `shared-research-against-no-token` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Then you place nothing — the outcome places a token only if able, so you don't take one from your sheet.
  - Clipper · 2021-09-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2718959/article/38385478#38385478
  - Clipper · 2019-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2274399/article/32923119#32923119
  - hehaheho · 2021-09-02 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2718959/article/38385098#38385098

    ### When is Committee Formation used, and does it combine with riders?
    - id: `committee-formation-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Just before voting, after the 'when' and 'after' windows, so riders are already down. The agenda it settles still counts as one of the phase's two. Riders that predicted the chosen player pay out, so playing a rider and then electing that player works. The Nekro can hold and use it despite not voting, and Political Secret doesn't stop it.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-10-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1872402/article/27280296#27280296
      - Dalek5 · 2017-10-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1872402/article/27283385#27283385
      - McBartsomeville · 2024-05-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3293538/article/44201134#44201134
      - Clipper · 2021-08-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2716411/article/38357428#38357428

  ### If Judicial Abolishment comes up with only one law in play, can that law be saved?
    - id: `judicial-abolishment-one-law` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — that law is the only eligible outcome. Abstaining isn't an outcome, and if nobody votes the speaker must break the 0-0 tie by choosing it.
      - halbower · 2020-12-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559734/article/36510975#36510975
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-12-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559734/article/36511051#36511051
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1889735/article/27523566#27523566

  ### Can Minister of War be used after any action?
  - id: `minister-of-war-any-action` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — its owner can discard it after any action to remove one of their command tokens from the board and take another action; it doesn't need to follow an action that removed a token. A player relays an FFG email confirming this.
    - IncrediSteve · 2018-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1934428/article/28174153#28174153
    - Clipper · 2018-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1934428/article/28174757#28174757

    - mAIOR · 2018-02-05 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1934428/article/28174165#28174165

    ### When is Minister of Peace used, and can other after-activation abilities follow it?
    - id: `minister-of-peace-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Right after the system is activated, before any ships move. Once it ends the active player's turn, no other abilities in that window can be resolved, per the developer's later FAQ ruling.
      - hooliganj · 2019-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2143806/article/31154734#31154734
      - Clipper · 2019-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2143806/article/31155104#31155104
      - Clipper · 2021-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2750411/article/38749986#38749986
      - BradleySigma · 2021-11-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2750411/article/38835002#38835002

  ### What triggers Minister of Industry?
  - id: `minister-of-industry-trigger` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Placing a space dock, which means putting one on the board from reinforcements. A Saar Floating Factory moving into a system doesn't trigger it, but a dock placed by a Construction Rider in the agenda phase does, payable only with planets you haven't exhausted.
    - Clipper · 2018-12-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2106114/article/30633308#30633308
    - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903947/article/27742050#27742050
    - Clipper · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903947/article/27742112#27742112

### How does Minister of Sciences interact with the Technology strategy card?
- id: `minister-of-sciences-technology` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Its owner pays no resources when researching through either Technology ability: two free techs with the primary, one with the secondary.
  - Painkeeper · 2018-01-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916301/article/27911383#27911383
  - pfischer · 2018-01-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916301/article/27911514#27911514

### Under Regulated Conscription, how much do fighters and infantry cost?
- id: `regulated-conscription-cost` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: 1 resource each; the law removes the two-for-one from their cost.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945

    ### How does Swords to Plowshares work on a planet with one infantry, and how often can Mentak pillage the result?
    - id: `swords-to-plowshares-one-infantry` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Half of one rounded up is one, so the lone infantry is destroyed and you gain 1 trade good. All the trade goods from Swords to Plowshares or Compensated Disarmament arrive in a single step, so Mentak can pillage each affected neighbor only once.
      - afrag · 2018-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933269/article/28158771#28158771
      - Alwin · 2018-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933269/article/28160088#28160088

      - Clipper · 2021-09-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2718934/article/38384890#38384890

    ### Which rolls can The Crown of Thalnos reroll?
    - id: `crown-of-thalnos-rerolls` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only combat rolls, not Anti-Fighter Barrage, Bombardment (L1Z1X Harrow included) or Sardakk's Courageous to the End. You may reroll units that look doomed, because hits aren't assigned until rolling ends; any unit whose reroll fails to hit is destroyed, even if its other dice hit.
      - Marcial · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719552/article/38392403#38392403
      - Clipper · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719552/article/38395091#38395091

      - The_Chiprel · 2019-06-17 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2221426/article/32249255#32249255

### If Homeland Defense Act is discarded, must players remove PDS beyond 2 per planet?
- id: `homeland-defense-act-repealed` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the normal limit of 2 PDS per planet applies again, and extras are removed.
  - DrewDecimal · 2020-05-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2434253/article/34914161#34914161

    ### Do gamma and delta wormholes count for Wormhole Research and Wormhole Reconstruction?
    - id: `wormhole-research-and-reconstruction` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: For Wormhole Research, ships in any wormhole system, gamma and delta included, qualify, but each player researches at most one technology; only ships in alpha or beta systems are then destroyed. Wormhole Reconstruction's Against outcome hits every wormhole system, delta included, which a player confirmed with the developer as intended.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - Clipper · 2021-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38273108#38273108
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017078/article/29418515#29418515
      - hooliganj · 2018-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017078/article/29418485#29418485

        ### Can a home planet, or the planet with Holy Planet of Ixth, be elected for Demilitarized Zone or Colonial Redistribution?
    - id: `cultural-planet-agendas` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Home planets can't: they have no trait, and these agendas elect a cultural planet. The Holy Planet of Ixth planet is still cultural, so it is a legal choice. A Demilitarized Zone planet given away by Colonial Redistribution gets no units, since none can be placed there.
      - KingOfStumps · 2020-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2485027/article/35587287#35587287
      - GenusPongo · 2020-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2485027/article/35587360#35587360
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38256827#38256827
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38258010#38258010

### Why vote against Conventions of War, and does it stop X-89 Bacterial Weapon?
- id: `conventions-of-war` — grade: Community consensus
- A: For makes it a law that nobody may bombard cultural planets, so players who rely on Bombardment vote Against, at the cost of their action cards. X-89 can still be used on cultural planets under it.
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919323/article/27959001#27959001
  - Santiago · 2018-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919323/article/27959011#27959011
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331

### What happens with Archived Secret if the elected player already holds the maximum secret objectives?
- id: `archived-secret-full-hand` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: They draw one, look at it and discard it right away.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331

### When an attached agenda says 'exhaust this card', do I exhaust the planet?
- id: `attached-agenda-exhaust-this-card` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — 'this card' always means the card the text is on, so you exhaust the attached agenda and the planet stays ready.
  - Clipper · 2019-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237328/article/32452354#32452354

  ### How do Minister of Commerce, Industry, Peace and War interact with other effects?
  - id: `minister-cards-details` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Minister of Commerce still pays 1 trade good per neighbor when your commodities were already full, since you still replenished. Minister of Industry lets you produce in that system right after placing the dock. Minister of Peace's owner needn't have units in the activated system. Minister of War isn't an action itself, so with Fleet Logistics you can take three actions in a turn.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945
      - Marcial · 2021-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38274470#38274470

### Does Minister of Sciences waive the 2 resources for L1Z1X's Inheritance Systems?
- id: `minister-of-sciences-inheritance-systems` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — those 2 resources pay to ignore prerequisites, not to research the technology.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945

    ### Does Shard of the Throne change hands if both sides' ground forces are wiped out?
    - id: `shard-of-the-throne-draw` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — if neither player has ground forces left, nobody won the combat, so the Shard stays put. It also never moves through combats that don't happen, such as landing unopposed or bombarding the last defenders.
      - halbower · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903157/article/27731847#27731847
      - Clipper · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903157/article/27731861#27731861
      - Darkmancer · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939056/article/28250027#28250027
      - Pete Walsh · 2020-05-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2438531/article/34975327#34975327

    ### How do the Research Team agendas work?
    - id: `research-team-agendas` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: You exhaust the Research Team card, not the planet, to skip a prerequisite, so the planet can still be spent. They don't give the planet a technology specialty, so they don't count for objectives about specialty planets.
      - Clipper · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939060/article/28250509#28250509
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939060/article/28250543#28250543
      - halbower · 2020-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2349578/article/33799690#33799690
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2349578/article/33800590#33800590
      - halbower · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939083/article/28250306#28250306
      - Clipper · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939083/article/28250404#28250404

    ### In what order are agenda-phase secret objectives scored?
- id: `agenda-phase-secret-scoring-order` — grade: Community consensus
- A: In speaker order, clockwise; it only matters if several players would win from the points, a tie that also goes clockwise from the speaker.
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2562213/article/36546790#36546790
  - hooliganj · 2020-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2562213/article/36547064#36547064

### When must The Prophecy of Ixth be discarded?
- id: `prophecy-of-ixth-discard` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Only when you use a unit's Production and don't produce 2 fighters with it. Skipping production, or placing units by other effects like Integrated Economy, keeps the card.
  - Clipper · 2021-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722144/article/38419299#38419299
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38260945#38260945

### If an agenda exhausts a planet with a Research Team attached, is the Research Team exhausted too?
- id: `research-team-exhausted-planet` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — exhausting a planet doesn't exhaust its attachment, so the Research Team can still be used.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707289/article/38255331#38255331


## Objectives and Victory Points

    ### Can I score objectives if another player controls a planet in my home system?
    - id: `home-system-needed-for-public-objectives` — grade: Per the rulebook — Objectives and Victory Points
    - A: Not public objectives — you must control every planet in your home system to score one, and the Imperial card doesn't override that. Everything else still works: secret objectives, Imperial's Mecatol Rex point, agendas, relics, Support for the Throne and the custodians token. Enemy ships blockading your home system don't stop you; only losing a planet there does.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-12-26 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Clipper · 2020-02-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2373593/article/34097906#34097906
      - pfischer · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953574/article/28481519#28481519
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2785869/article/39149387#39149387
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1887343/article/27484803#27484803

    ### Does the limit of three secret objectives include ones I've scored?
- id: `secret-objective-limit-three` — grade: Per the rulebook — Objectives and Victory Points
- A: Yes — you may have three in total, scored and unscored together, so three is also the most you can score. If you go over, you return an unscored one; scored objectives are never discarded.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-02-23 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Webhead123 · 2018-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014398/article/29380422#29380422
  - Marcial · 2020-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2374224/article/34108069#34108069
  - Marcial · 2019-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302080/article/33256101#33256101
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1914516/article/27888101#27888101

    ### How do I get more secret objectives after setup?
- id: `getting-more-secret-objectives` — grade: Per the rulebook — 1. Strategy Phase
- A: Mainly through Imperial: its secondary draws one for a strategy token, and its primary draws one if you don't control Mecatol Rex. You draw one card, not two to keep one; that's only at setup.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-02-07 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - SwissQueso · 2019-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2146652/article/31195381#31195381
  - Clipper · 2019-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2315706/article/33412374#33412374

    ### For objectives that require spending resources, influence, tokens or trade goods, does earlier spending count?
    - id: `spend-objectives-pay-when-scoring` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — you pay the full amount in one go when you score the objective, as its cost; nothing spent earlier in the round counts. Trade goods can stand in for resources or influence there, though a 'spend trade goods' objective needs actual trade goods. If you score one through Imperial, you pay right then in the action phase.
      - zamoose · 2020-07-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2458803/article/35247327#35247327
      - AgentDrake · 2021-03-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2629281/article/37355757#37355757
      - axibis · 2026-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3712262/article/47716044#47716044
      - AgentDrake · 2020-09-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2511327/article/35911642#35911642
