      - Clipper · 2019-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2125487/article/30891359#30891359

      ### Can I mark an objective as soon as I meet it and score it later, or keep a point if I stop qualifying?
      - id: `must-meet-objective-when-scoring` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: You must qualify at the moment you score. Status-phase objectives check the condition then, so a blockade you lifted earlier doesn't count for Cut Supply Lines. Once scored, points are kept even if you stop qualifying; only effects that say so, like Support for the Throne returning, take points away.
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-03-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2167860/article/31491303#31491303
        - Clipper · 2019-03-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2167860/article/31492300#31492300

        - isterbat · 2025-07-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3543490/article/46341896#46341896
        - Marcial · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2285746/article/33058768#33058768
        - RedEyedGhost · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2285746/article/33058930#33058930

      ### Do revealed public objectives stay in play, and can several players score the same one?
      - id: `public-objectives-stay-in-play` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — they stay for the rest of the game unless an effect removes them. Any number of players can score each, but each player scores a given objective only once.
        - AgentDrake · 2020-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2452043/article/35158022#35158022
        - hooliganj · 2020-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2452043/article/35159067#35159067

    ### How and when are action-phase secret objectives scored?
    - id: `action-phase-secret-objectives` — grade: Per the rulebook — Objectives and Victory Points
    - A: Secret objectives marked Action Phase are scored the moment you meet them, even on another player's turn and even after you have passed. You may score several in a turn but only one per combat or step: one in a space combat and another in the ground combat that follows is fine, two from the same combat is not.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-02-28 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554673/article/36446478#36446478
      - hooliganj · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912007/article/27850435#27850435
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2039645/article/29727478#29727478
      - MollyMae · 2019-06-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2227828/article/32325310#32325310

    ### If two players could each score an action-phase objective in the same combat, who scores first?
    - id: `two-players-score-in-same-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Whichever condition is met first. Destroying a flagship or war sun happens during a combat round, before the combat ends and has a winner. If both trigger at the same moment, the active player goes first.
      - Armand7734 · 2020-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2513814/article/35944719#35944719
      - Clipper · 2020-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2513814/article/35946181#35946181

    ### Can I score a public objective with Imperial and another in the same round's status phase?
    - id: `imperial-and-status-phase-scoring` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — they are separate scoring windows. Imperial scores a public objective immediately even if it says status phase, and you can still score one more public objective in the status phase.
      - MercifulBiscuit · 2019-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2137428/article/31063602#31063602
      - Clipper · 2019-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2137428/article/31065195#31065195
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890953/article/27542298#27542298
      - pkitty · 2022-07-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2903823/article/40492650#40492650

### Can Imperial's point for controlling Mecatol Rex be gained more than once?
- id: `imperial-mecatol-point-repeatable` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — each time you resolve the Imperial primary while controlling Mecatol Rex you gain it, separately from the one-time custodians token point.
  - Clipper · 2022-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2805221/article/39372319#39372319

      ### Do Space Cannon, Bombardment or landing on an empty planet count as winning a combat for objectives?
      - id: `what-counts-as-winning-a-combat` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — a combat is a space combat or a ground combat, and you win when your units remain and the opponent's don't. Destroying ships with Space Cannon during movement, bombarding away all defenders, or landing unopposed involves no combat, so it can't score 'win a combat' objectives or take Shard of the Throne. A Naalu Foresight escape means no combat happened either. A space dock or PDS alone doesn't make a combat.
        - MollyMae · 2024-07-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3335106/article/44574227#44574227
        - hooliganj · 2024-07-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3335106/article/44575013#44575013
        - Pete Walsh · 2020-05-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2438531/article/34975327#34975327
        - dr_worm_md · 2018-03-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1951202/article/28444689#28444689
        - AgentDrake · 2021-03-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2622714/article/37273434#37273434
        - sigmazero13 · 2022-04-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2850542/article/39890280#39890280

    ### Does a ship destroyed by Space Cannon plus Direct Hit, or fighters lost for capacity after Space Cannon kills their carrier, count for Turn Their Fleets to Dust?
    - id: `space-cannon-and-direct-hit-for-fleets-to-dust` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both. Direct Hit's own text destroys the ship, not the Space Cannon, and fighters lost to the capacity rule weren't destroyed by Space Cannon; FFG confirmed both by email. Space Cannon fire resolves player by player, so only the player whose own shots meet the condition scores.
      - 0 hr · 2018-08-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843549/article/29669860#29669860

  ### Can I score an objective for beating 'the player with the most victory points', like Spark a Rebellion, if I'm the leader?
  - id: `beat-player-with-most-points` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Only if you share the lead: when players are tied for most points, each of them counts as having the most. If you lead alone, nobody else qualifies. The check is made at the time of the combat.
    - bleached_lizard · 2017-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1848160/article/26907825#26907825

    - csouth154 · 2019-03-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2162458/article/31413826#31413826

  ### When several players are tied for the most or fewest victory points, which of them does an effect apply to?
  - id: `tied-for-most-or-fewest-points` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: All of them — every tied player counts. Tied agenda votes are different, since the speaker breaks those.
    - maverikki · 2017-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886825/article/27483129#27483129

    ### If I beat the Shard of the Throne holder, who leads on points, can I score Spark a Rebellion and take the Shard?
    - id: `spark-a-rebellion-and-shard` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes, both. Players differ on whether the objective scores before the Shard moves or after, but either way you won a combat against the leader.
      - Pete Walsh · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587965/article/36833406#36833406

    ### Do other players' home planets count for 'non-home system' objectives like Subdue the Galaxy?
    - id: `non-home-planets-objectives` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — 'non-home' excludes every home system, including other players' that you control. A separate objective covers holding a planet in another player's home system.
      - hooliganj · 2018-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1914642/article/27889588#27889588

      ### Do ships in Mecatol Rex's system, or in an anomaly, count as adjacent to it for objectives?
      - id: `adjacent-objectives-not-in-system` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — a system isn't adjacent to itself. Intimidate Council needs ships in two different systems next to Mecatol Rex, and ships inside an anomaly don't count as adjacent to it.

    ### What counts for technology-colour objectives like Diversify Research and Master the Sciences?
- id: `technology-colour-objectives` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only researched technology cards with a colour. Diversify Research needs two of each of two colours (four total); Master the Sciences two of each of the four colours. Unit upgrades have no colour, and planets with technology specialties are not technologies.
  - Weltenreiter · 2019-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2251326/article/32635609#32635609

  - Xelvonar · 2018-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1910873/article/27837186#27837186

    ### Do technologies the Nekro copy with Valefar Assimilator count for objectives?
    - id: `nekro-faction-tech-objectives` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — a copied technology counts with its colour and type, so the Nekro can score faction-technology objectives that way. But when a copied faction upgrade covers a generic one, like Dreadnought II, only one of the two counts.

    ### Does the Muaat Prototype War Sun count as a war sun for objectives?
- id: `faction-war-sun-objectives` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it is the Muaat war sun under another name, as its subtitle shows.
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2457195/article/35225190#35225190

  ### Can Support for the Throne be given away even if it hands someone the winning point, and can the giver attack the holder?
  - id: `support-for-the-throne-trading` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes to both. Trading it is a normal transaction, and a ban is a house rule. Only the holder risks losing it, by activating a system with the giver's units, any system, even by mistake. The giver can attack freely.

    - Dulkal · 2018-03-20 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1961149/article/28599311#28599311

### If I reveal a secret objective to score it but turn out not to qualify, do I lose it?
- id: `revealed-unmet-secret-objective` — grade: Community consensus
- A: There's no rule for it in Fourth Edition. Most players keep the objective, now known to everyone, and score it later as normal.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1961149/article/28600172#28600172

  ### Are there common clarifications for specific objectives like Establish a Perimeter, Unveil Flagship, Become the Gatekeeper or Make an Example of Their World?
  - id: `objective-specific-clarifications` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes: 5 PDS satisfies 'own 4 PDS'; Unveil Flagship needs your flagship to survive the combat; Become the Gatekeeper needs ships in both an alpha and a beta system, as Creuss's Quantum Entanglement only makes them adjacent; Rule Distant Lands needs planets near two different players' home systems; and destroying the last ground forces with L1Z1X's Harrow counts for Make an Example, since Harrow is Bombardment.

      - Clipper · 2018-06-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2007138/article/29277007#29277007

    ### When do I discard the 5 action cards for Form a Spy Network?
    - id: `form-a-spy-network-discard` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: When you score it in the status phase, as its cost, without resolving the cards. You can't discard cards freely at other times.
      - Sande24 · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886629/article/27476259#27476259

  ### How does the Total War variant's commodity tally work?
  - id: `total-war-variant` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Each time you destroy enemy units you take commodities equal to their combined cost and keep them on a planet in your home system; with 10 there you may take the component action for a point. Whoever takes that home system takes the stored commodities too. Units captured without being destroyed, as by the Vuil'raith hero, add nothing.

## Space Combat

      ### Does the 0 on the ten-sided die count as 0 or 10?
      - id: `zero-on-die-is-ten` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: 10 — the Living Rules Reference FAQ confirms the 0 face is a 10, the best result. Otherwise units with combat 10 could never hit.
        - ninjamatic3000 · 2019-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1842037/article/31802559#31802559
        - Becq · 2017-09-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1842037/article/26846023#26846023
        - Sande24 · 2017-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1842037/article/26825735#26825735
        - Marcial · 2024-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3418889/article/45301696#45301696
        - michaelwood · 2021-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2570433/article/36631512#36631512

    ### Does a 10 always hit or a 1 always miss?
- id: `no-critical-hits` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — there are no automatic hits or misses. A roll hits when the result, after modifiers, equals or beats the unit's combat value, so with enough bonuses even a 1 can hit.
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3372925/article/44903484#44903484
  - HP12C_guy · 2024-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3372925/article/44903545#44903545

    ### Can more than two players fight in the same space combat?
- id: `combat-only-two-players` — grade: Per the rulebook — Space Combat
- A: No — a space combat is always between two players, and it continues until only one player's ships remain or one side retreats. Other players can still have ground forces or structures on planets in that system.
  - phunkysai · 2024-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3225024/article/43603686#43603686
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3225024/article/43603731#43603731

    ### How does Sustain Damage work, and is it optional?
- id: `sustain-damage-basics` — grade: Per the rulebook — Space Combat
- A: Before hits are assigned, a unit with Sustain Damage may cancel one hit; it is placed on its side as damaged and can't do so again until repaired in the status phase. It is optional: you may skip it and assign the hit to the unit, destroying it. An undamaged dreadnought therefore needs two hits, which can come in different rounds.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-06 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27636848#27636848
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27647521#27647521
  - Old_Man_gi · 2025-04-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3495598/article/45949083#45949083
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1943957/article/28324715#28324715

  ### Can Sustain Damage save a ship from Exotrireme II, Assault Cannon or other 'destroy' effects?
  - id: `sustain-damage-vs-destroy-effects` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — Sustain Damage only cancels hits, and these effects destroy units without producing hits. For Exotrireme II, the Sardakk player chooses which ships (up to 2) are destroyed; it is used after a round of combat, and each Exotrireme II may use it.
    - Jatta Pake · 2017-12-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27589396#27589396
    - hooliganj · 2017-12-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27588981#27588981
    - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27590206#27590206
    - garfielder · 2017-09-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845272/article/26954840#26954840
    - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845272/article/26866869#26866869
    - csouth154 · 2019-03-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845272/article/31420609#31420609

    ### When Dimensional Splicer or Yin's Devotion hits my ship, who picks the target, and can I use Sustain Damage?
  - id: `assigned-ability-hits-and-sustain` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: The Creuss or Yin player chooses which of your ships takes the hit, and assigns it immediately when the ability resolves. Only that ship can use its own Sustain Damage to cancel it; you can't move the hit elsewhere.
    - Bonitatem · 2018-05-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29120069#29120069
    - IncrediSteve · 2018-05-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29120164#29120164
    - The_Chiprel · 2020-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2377204/article/34141156#34141156
    - Jatta Pake · 2017-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27645205#27645205
    - Clipper · 2017-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894145/article/27645233#27645233
    - Clipper · 2019-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32304534#32304534

    ### Are 'start of combat' and 'start of the first combat round' abilities in the same timing window, and how often do they happen?
    - id: `start-of-combat-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — they are the same window, before Anti-Fighter Barrage, and ordered by the usual timing rules. Later rounds only have 'start of a round' windows, so start-of-combat abilities like Assault Cannon happen once per combat. Anything destroyed in that window, such as a destroyer hit by Dimensional Splicer, never fires its barrage.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702835/article/38203274#38203274
      - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702835/article/38207881#38207881
      - Clipper · 2018-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1957836/article/28951927#28951927
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1957836/article/28955253#28955253
      - hooliganj · 2026-08-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3746177/article/47997804#47997804
      - Lowecore · 2026-08-01 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3746177/article/47997750#47997750

    ### If both players have start-of-combat abilities like Assault Cannon or Skilled Retreat, which resolves first?
    - id: `assault-cannon-order-and-targets` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The active player's, then the other player's, with each trigger re-checked when its turn comes. So the attacker's Assault Cannon can push the defender below 3 non-fighter ships and deny the defender's own Assault Cannon, and it destroys a ship before the defender's Skilled Retreat moves the rest away. Assault Cannon is mandatory when it applies and can never destroy a fighter.
      - Clipper · 2018-03-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1957836/article/28549840#28549840
      - adrim · 2018-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1957836/article/30803824#30803824
      - IndyBart · 2021-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2591340/article/36877482#36877482
      - hooliganj · 2021-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2591340/article/36877670#36877670
      - csouth154 · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273270/article/32905480#32905480
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-04-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2647210/article/37565541#37565541

    ### With rerolls or die modifiers like the Hacan flagship or Crown of Thalnos, must I decide before rolling my other ships?
- id: `rerolls-after-all-dice` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — since the Codex rules update, rerolls and modifiers are applied after you have rolled all your dice, so you see every result before choosing. The older reading of deciding ship type by ship type is out of date.
  - russe · 2023-03-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2018641/article/41899043#41899043
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-06-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2445088/article/35064736#35064736
  - Clipper · 2020-06-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2445088/article/35065922#35065922
  - Ndjcirnc · 2023-02-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3030607/article/41747275#41747275
  - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2703288/article/38208419#38208419
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904114/article/27744810#27744810

    ### Who can retreat from a space combat, and does an announced retreat still happen if the enemy is wiped out?
    - id: `retreat-announcement-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Either player can announce a retreat, the attacker included, but the defender announces first, and if the defender retreats the attacker can't that round. You can only announce one if you have a legal system to retreat to (so Rout needs one too). If your opponent has no ships left after the round, the combat simply ends and your retreat doesn't happen; answers from 2017 saying you still had to leave predate that rule.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-10-27 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - MATTtheGM · 2022-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2851938/article/39902870#39902870
      - brettellmd · 2022-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2851938/article/39903091#39903091
      - Clipper · 2019-01-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2139994/article/31097526#31097526
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900069/article/27684405#27684405

    ### Where can my ships retreat to?
    - id: `retreat-destinations` — grade: Per the rulebook — Space Combat
    - A: To an adjacent system with no other player's ships that contains your units or a planet you control; Dark Energy Tap drops the second requirement. Movement bonuses don't matter, as a retreat always goes to an adjacent system. Under Enforced Travel Ban, alpha and beta wormholes give no adjacency for a retreat. Whether a nebula is a legal destination is disputed; see the nebula question under movement.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-10-16 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2742656/article/38658450#38658450
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2063574/article/30055125#30055125
      - Clipper · 2018-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2063574/article/30055126#30055126
      - Clipper · 2018-02-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28272502#28272502

      ### When my ships retreat, can they pick up ground forces from my planets in the combat system?
      - id: `retreat-with-ground-forces` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes, under the current Living Rules Reference: retreating ships move and transport units like any other movement, so they may pick up ground forces from planets there within their capacity. The old rule of lifting ground forces into space when you announce the retreat was removed in v1.2 (some translations still have it), which superseded the developer's 2018 answers about choosing units at announcement.
        - GrumblingGamer · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32221350#32221350
        - hooliganj · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32221378#32221378
        - sigmazero13 · 2019-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2216228/article/32221803#32221803
        - Clipper · 2021-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581021/article/36757459#36757459
        - CreussEmissary · 2018-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30370789#30370789
        - Grunt778083 · 2021-01-16 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581021/article/36754716#36754716

    ### What happens to units that can't go with a retreat?
    - id: `units-left-behind-after-retreat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: All your ships with a move value retreat together. Fighters and ground forces in the space area that the retreating ships can't carry are destroyed, even if a space dock could have supported them. Ground forces on planets are not destroyed; they stay, and the winner may bombard and invade them.
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2743880/article/38670712#38670712
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2026406/article/29548107#29548107
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2026406/article/29558833#29558833
      - 0 hr · 2018-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2026406/article/29559593#29559593
      - Padish · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899636/article/27936556#27936556
      - Clipper · 2019-06-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2216228/article/32185449#32185449

    ### How does Skilled Retreat differ from a normal retreat?
    - id: `skilled-retreat-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It isn't a retreat in rules terms but a move, so Intercept can't cancel it (only Sabotage), and the usual transport rules apply: ships can pick up ground forces from planets, and fighters move with the fleet without needing capacity. Capacity and fleet limits apply again at the destination, and you choose which excess units to lose. It can go to any adjacent system without other players' ships, including the one the attacker came from.
      - JamesG · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954990/article/28525860#28525860
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954990/article/30349026#30349026
      - harven · 2025-04-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947520/article/45962109#45962109
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-02-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31370453#31370453
      - Marcial · 2019-02-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31367667#31367667
      - Clipper · 2018-03-10 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954990/article/28506815#28506815

    ### Can I retreat or Skilled Retreat into, or carry units out of, systems that contain my command token?
    - id: `skilled-retreat-own-command-token` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes on both counts. A normal retreat or Skilled Retreat may go into a system that already has your command token; you just don't place a second one. And retreating ships can carry units out of the combat system even if your token is there, because the transport restriction excludes the active system, whoever activated it.
      - Clipper · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587977/article/36834488#36834488
      - Clipper · 2019-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2169722/article/31515168#31515168
      - Clipper · 2020-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2353256/article/33844279#33844279
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954990/article/30363304#30363304
      - Clipper · 2022-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581021/article/40464493#40464493
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581021/article/36756161#36756161

    ### What happens if neither side can ever win a space combat?
- id: `combat-stalemate` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The Living Rules Reference FAQ says the attacker must retreat. If the attacker has nowhere legal to go, players generally treat those ships as destroyed.
  - russe · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904694/article/28476677#28476677
  - Clipper · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904694/article/28478086#28478086
  - pkitty · 2017-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904694/article/27785669#27785669

    ### Can two players agree not to fight when their ships share a system, or not to roll or assign hits?
- id: `combat-is-mandatory` — grade: Per the rulebook — Space Combat
- A: No — when two players have ships in the same space area (or ground forces on the same planet), combat happens and continues until one side is gone; rolling and assigning hits are mandatory and a deal can't override the rules. Retreating, after at least one round, is the only legal way out. Ships and ground forces never fight each other.
  - zamoose · 2021-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2700877/article/38180868#38180868
  - Marcial · 2021-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2700877/article/38181339#38181339
  - to_mars · 2021-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2773187/article/39013502#39013502
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2773187/article/39014163#39014163
  - hooliganj · 2025-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3559728/article/46476876#46476876

### In space combat, who assigns hits first?
- id: `hit-assignment-simultaneous` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Nobody first — both players assign hits in the same step. Assigning hits isn't an ability, so no initiative order applies; cancelling hits, as with Sustain Damage, is.
  - zamoose · 2023-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3033726/article/41775954#41775954
  - zamoose · 2023-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3033726/article/41776052#41776052

    ### Can a unit with Sustain Damage be destroyed by two hits in one round?
- id: `two-hits-on-sustain-unit` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — Sustain Damage cancels one hit, not every hit. If you must take more hits than you can cancel and that unit is what's left, the next hit destroys it; it is not a second hit point. Non-Euclidean Shielding lets it cancel two.
  - AgentDrake · 2019-09-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2284266/article/33039652#33039652
  - Clipper · 2023-03-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3044534/article/41886301#41886301
  - Clipper · 2017-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1897888/article/27653153#27653153
  - Truji · 2025-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3634580/article/47074849#47074849
  - TomKyrre · 2025-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3634580/article/47075217#47075217

    ### If my carrier is destroyed in combat, are the fighters and ground forces it carried destroyed at once?
    - id: `capacity-during-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — capacity isn't checked during combat, so they stay while it lasts. When combat ends, each player checks capacity and removes fighters and ground forces in space that are no longer supported. If all your ships are destroyed, the units they carried go with them. If the side left in space has only fighters it can no longer support, they are removed and the combat counts as a draw.
      - hooliganj · 2024-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3388282/article/45031342#45031342
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2953449/article/40986562#40986562
      - Dubledox · 2022-10-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2953449/article/40990294#40990294
      - Weltenreiter · 2021-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2643346/article/37520627#37520627
      - donmakaron · 2021-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2643346/article/37520728#37520728
      - zamoose · 2018-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2104169/article/30604606#30604606

    ### If the defender leaves with Skilled Retreat or Foresight, does the attacker still invade, and has anyone won a combat?
- id: `after-skilled-retreat-attacker-continues` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: The attacker carries on to bombardment, invasion and production as normal. But no combat was won: after a normal retreat the player left in the system wins, whereas Foresight happens before combat and Skilled Retreat leaves no winner, so 'win a combat' objectives and effects don't trigger. The destination system isn't activated, so nobody fires Space Cannon or invades there.
    - Clipper · 2020-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2351347/article/33824969#33824969
    - IncrediSteve · 2018-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1914612/article/27889265#27889265
    - hooliganj · 2025-05-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3520303/article/46151147#46151147
    - Bigland · 2017-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1889092/article/27515123#27515123
    - McBartsomeville · 2023-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3164528/article/43054869#43054869
    - MollyMae · 2018-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2046050/article/29813629#29813629

### What happens to the attacker's ships if the invasion fails?
- id: `failed-invasion-ships-stay` — grade: Per the rulebook — Producing Units
- A: They stay in the system; nothing makes them leave. While they remain, the defender's space dock there is blockaded and can only produce ground forces.
  - ifaeb · 2024-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3225949/article/43610283#43610283

    ### If Space Cannon Offense destroys all of the active player's ships, did a space combat take place?
- id: `space-cannon-wipes-attacker-no-combat` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — Space Cannon Offense happens at the end of movement, outside space combat. If it destroys every ship, no combat occurs and 'win a combat' effects don't apply.
  - csouth154 · 2019-04-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2189731/article/31794581#31794581
  - Clipper · 2019-04-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2189731/article/31794673#31794673

      ### Do Morale Boost, faction modifiers and rerolls affect Anti-Fighter Barrage, Space Cannon, Bombardment or Ambush?
      - id: `what-is-a-combat-roll` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — only rolls made against a unit's combat value are combat rolls. Anti-Fighter Barrage, Space Cannon, Bombardment, Mentak's Ambush and Sardakk's Courageous to the End are ability rolls, so Morale Boost, Sardakk's +1, Jol-Nar's Fragile and effects that reroll combat rolls don't touch them. Anti-Fighter Barrage happens only in the first round.
        - Clipper · 2021-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594219/article/36911887#36911887
        - Nurgle84 · 2021-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594219/article/36915353#36915353
        - hooliganj · 2017-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1885373/article/27459971#27459971
        - IncrediSteve · 2018-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1914859/article/27892155#27892155
        - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2016124/article/29404383#29404383
        - Surge72 · 2020-12-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556692/article/36471497#36471497

    ### Can I roll all my combat dice together when my ships have different combat values?
- id: `rolling-dice-by-combat-value` — grade: Per the rulebook — Space Combat
- A: Only for ships with the same combat value; ships with different values roll separately, so each die stays with the unit that rolled it. Modifiers are added to the die result, so +1 makes a hit easier.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-08-13 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - AgentDrake · 2020-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2484032/article/35573441#35573441
  - JaHeit · 2021-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2574466/article/36673652#36673652

    ### Do ships that announced a retreat still roll that round?
    - id: `retreating-ships-still-roll` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — announcing a retreat doesn't stop you firing; you roll normally and then leave in the retreat step. Retreating in that step also escapes Exotrireme II, which acts at the end of the round.
      - DarthHenryAllen · 2018-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1935289/article/28189665#28189665
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1935289/article/28189694#28189694
      - hooliganj · 2018-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2050894/article/29882424#29882424

    ### Can ground forces that retreated with my ships, or moved with Skilled Retreat, land on a planet in the new system?
    - id: `retreated-ground-forces-cannot-land` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — landing only happens in the invasion step of a tactical action in the active system, and a retreat doesn't activate anything. They stay in space on your ships.
      - GrumblingGamer · 2019-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220738/article/32230582#32230582
      - donmakaron · 2019-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220738/article/32230653#32230653
      - GamerdadMich · 2018-06-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2015716/article/29398315#29398315
      - Normative · 2018-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902385/article/27904184#27904184
      - csouth154 · 2019-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273456/article/32912605#32912605
      - Clipper · 2018-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1932580/article/28150343#28150343

    ### When both players have end-of-round abilities, like Yin's Devotion and Sardakk's Exotrireme II, who goes first?
    - id: `after-combat-round-ability-order` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The active player resolves one first, then the players alternate one ability at a time; each Exotrireme II is a separate ability.
      - Singhilarity · 2020-10-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528328/article/36121492#36121492
      - hooliganj · 2020-10-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528328/article/36121994#36121994

      - acegravity · 2020-05-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2419831/article/34708234#34708234


## Invasion and Ground Combat

    ### What happens if I move ships into a system where the opponent has only ground forces on planets?
- id: `ships-vs-ground-forces-only` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No space combat — ships only fight ships. You may then bombard and commit ground forces in the invasion step; ships alone do nothing to enemy planets.
  - hooliganj · 2020-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563956/article/36566003#36566003
  - Clipper · 2020-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563956/article/36566059#36566059

    ### After winning a space combat, must I invade the planets in that system?
- id: `invasion-optional` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — committing ground forces is optional. You can leave your ships in space and go straight to production, even sharing the system with enemy planets.
  - MollyMae · 2019-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142367/article/31135354#31135354
  - DaveS · 2019-02-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142367/article/31139159#31139159
  - Bigland · 2017-08-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1833973/article/26695602#26695602
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2044803/article/29800479#29800479

    ### Can I invade several planets in the same system in one tactical action?
- id: `invade-several-planets-one-action` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — in the invasion step you commit ground forces to any or all planets in the active system, declaring which go where. Each ground force lands on only one planet, so two planets need at least two ground forces. Four infantry arriving at an empty two-planet system can put two on each and take both.
  - Pete Walsh · 2023-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3202295/article/43399416#43399416
  - Marcial · 2023-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3202295/article/43399893#43399893
  - SFRR · 2025-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3559591/article/46475425#46475425
  - Teconrad · 2017-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1909983/article/27828570#27828570
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1909983/article/27834483#27834483
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1895765/article/27611098#27611098

    ### Can ground forces already on one planet invade another planet in the same system?
    - id: `commit-from-planet-to-another-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only if a ship with capacity picked them up into the space area during the movement step. Only ground forces in the space area can be committed, so units still on a planet when the invasion step begins stay put.
      - StrangeBeacon · 2026-09-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3773759/article/48221212#48221212
      - Cacotopos · 2026-09-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3773759/article/48222552#48222552
      - GrumblingGamer · 2018-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2121262/article/30840366#30840366
      - Clipper · 2018-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2121262/article/30840420#30840420

    ### Can I land ground forces on a planet, even one I control, outside the invasion step?
    - id: `landing-only-in-invasion-step` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — ground forces land on planets only by being committed in the invasion step of a tactical action, so you must activate the system even to drop infantry on your own planet. Movement abilities used as component actions skip the tactical action steps and can't land anyone.
      - csouth154 · 2019-09-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280754/article/32994114#32994114
      - Clipper · 2022-07-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2897882/article/40421994#40421994
      - Leifr · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1905253/article/27764273#27764273
      - Cacotopos · 2023-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3216789/article/43534328#43534328

    ### Can fighters or dreadnoughts take part in an invasion?
- id: `fighters-cannot-invade` — grade: Per the rulebook — Invasion and Ground Combat
- A: Fighters can't invade (barring specific faction abilities), and ships take part only through abilities like Bombardment. You need ground forces in the active system's space area to commit.
  - Falcone1983 · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888622/article/27505709#27505709

    ### Is there a ground combat if Bombardment or Space Cannon Defense wipes out one side first?
- id: `ground-combat-needs-both-sides` — grade: Per the rulebook — Invasion and Ground Combat
- A: No — ground combat only happens if both players still have ground forces on the planet after those steps. If Bombardment kills every defender, you land unopposed; if Space Cannon Defense kills every invader, nobody lands. Structures don't count as defenders, and start-of-combat abilities such as Yin's Indoctrination can't be used without a combat.
  - ajmarshall · 2020-05-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286628/article/34777180#34777180
  - ajmarshall · 2019-10-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286628/article/33192844#33192844
  - donmakaron · 2019-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286628/article/33070957#33070957
  - Clipper · 2019-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286628/article/33073178#33073178

## Space Cannon, Bombardment and Anti-Fighter Barrage

    ### Does Space Cannon Offense happen if the active player moves nothing into the active system?
- id: `space-cannon-offense-without-moving` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it is a step of every tactical action, not something movement triggers, so enemy Space Cannon can hit the active player's ships already in the system. Equally, you may activate a system purely to fire your own Space Cannon into it. You must finish moving before anyone fires, so you can't see the results first.
  - Padish · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27758616#27758616
  - halbower · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27759025#27759025
  - csouth154 · 2019-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/32869137#32869137
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-03-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3259486/article/43901068#43901068
  - Marcial · 2024-03-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3259486/article/43900966#43900966

      ### During Space Cannon Offense, who can shoot at whom?
      - id: `space-cannon-offense-targets` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: The active player fires first and chooses which other player's ships in the active system take the hits. Then each other player with Space Cannon in range fires, but only at the active player's ships — a third party can't shoot the defender or help one side of someone else's battle.
        - hooliganj · 2017-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890940/article/27537709#27537709
        - hooliganj · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890940/article/27550768#27550768

        - csouth154 · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286065/article/33062716#33062716
        - Crikrunner · 2017-11-27 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890940/article/27546721#27546721

      ### Can a ship use Sustain Damage against Space Cannon hits?
      - id: `sustain-damage-vs-space-cannon` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — Sustain Damage can cancel any hit produced against the unit, Space Cannon included. It only fails against effects that destroy a unit without producing a hit.
        - fynikz · 2017-09-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843549/article/26848964#26848964
        - halbower · 2017-09-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843549/article/26850559#26850559

        - Clipper · 2019-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2220180/article/32226081#32226081
        - Jatta Pake · 2017-09-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843549/article/26855753#26855753

    ### When can Experimental Battlestation be used, and what can it shoot?
    - id: `experimental-battlestation-trigger` — grade: Designer ruling
    - A: Only when another player's ships actually move during the movement step of a tactical action: not on retreats, not when nothing moves in, and never on your own turn as active player. It fires as part of Space Cannon Offense and can target only the active player's ships in the active system. An earlier emailed ruling allowing any movement, retreats included, was walked back by the developer.
      - CreussEmissary · 2018-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/30492450#30492450
      - Clipper · 2018-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/30492596#30492596
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-11-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/30496644#30496644
      - Clipper · 2019-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/32872571#32872571
      - 0 hr · 2018-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2095190/article/30485080#30485080

    ### Can Space Cannon fire at ships outside the active system, such as ships passing by, retreating, or in a neighboring system?
    - id: `space-cannon-only-active-system` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Space Cannon only ever targets the active system: Offense after movement, Defense during invasion. PDS II's Deep Space Cannon and the Xxcha flagship let a unit in an adjacent system fire into the active system, never out of it. Ships passing through systems in range or retreating into one aren't shot at. A unit with the ability that moved into the active system fires as normal.
      - Marcial · 2021-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2778834/article/39091197#39091197
      - csouth154 · 2019-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2286065/article/33062716#33062716

      - toskk · 2025-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3614925/article/46917713#46917713
      - IncrediSteve · 2026-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3684700/article/47482643#47482643
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862122/article/40021554#40021554

    ### When several players fire Space Cannon Offense, are they resolved together?
    - id: `space-cannon-one-player-at-a-time` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — one player at a time, starting with the active player and then the others in order. Each player's roll, hit assignment, Sustain Damage and responses like Direct Hit finish before the next fires, so a unit destroyed earlier, such as an Xxcha flagship, never gets to shoot.
      - pedro2112 · 2020-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2562908/article/36554391#36554391
      - hooliganj · 2020-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2562908/article/36556621#36556621

      - Clipper · 2019-09-09 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272708/article/32898298#32898298

### Can Space Cannon with range reach through a wormhole?
- id: `space-cannon-through-wormholes` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — wormhole adjacency counts for Space Cannon range, including the delta wormhole on the Creuss flagship, and even when a law turns off wormholes for movement. The Nomad flagship's mech adjacency is not a wormhole, so it doesn't extend Space Cannon.
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-03-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3259486/article/43901068#43901068

    ### Is Bombardment mandatory, and can I bombard without landing ground forces?
- id: `bombardment-optional` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The Bombardment step always happens, but using the ability is optional: the active player may bombard. You can also bombard with no ground forces to land and simply not take the planet, since committing ground forces is optional too.
  - blondlostime · 2019-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2144187/article/31194470#31194470
  - AgentDrake · 2019-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2144187/article/31194510#31194510

  - blondlostime · 2019-02-07 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2144187/article/31193542#31193542

    ### How many extra dice does Plasma Scoring add to Bombardment and Space Cannon?
    - id: `plasma-scoring-one-die` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: One extra die per use of the ability, not per unit or per planet. Bombardment is a single step, so one extra die however many planets you bombard. Space Cannon Offense rolls one die per unit in range plus one from Plasma Scoring. Each planet's Space Cannon Defense is a separate roll, so each can get the extra die.
      - MATTPROV3 · 2018-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937996/article/28235090#28235090
      - Clipper · 2018-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937996/article/28236557#28236557

      - csouth154 · 2019-06-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2213830/article/32128112#32128112
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-11 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937996/article/28235033#28235033

    ### Can the active player fire their own Space Cannon, including PDS II in an adjacent system, into the system they activate?
- id: `who-fires-space-cannon-offense` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — every player with Space Cannon in range of the active system may fire, the active player first. You can activate a system purely so your PDS II next door can shoot enemy ships there, even a supernova if someone like Muaat activated it. Fighters in the space area are valid targets; ground forces in space and Saar Floating Factories are not.
  - Platfus · 2022-05-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2873373/article/40152453#40152453

  - NeoTiger · 2019-06-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2225575/article/32297432#32297432

    ### Which PDS fire Space Cannon Defense during an invasion?
- id: `space-cannon-defense-which-pds` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only PDS on the planet being invaded. A PDS on another planet in the system, or a PDS II in an adjacent system, doesn't fire then. A PDS can fire Space Cannon Offense and later Space Cannon Defense in the same tactical action.
  - Clipper · 2021-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2676676/article/37908054#37908054
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-06-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2676676/article/37908565#37908565
  - hooliganj · 2022-07-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2902453/article/40474212#40474212
  - hooliganj · 2025-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3553979/article/46426951#46426951

      ### Can one ship bombard several planets, or split its dice between them?
      - id: `bombardment-assignment` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — there is one Bombardment step per invasion, and before rolling you declare which planet each unit bombards; a unit bombards a single planet with all its dice. You may pick any planet in the system, since bombardment comes before ground forces are committed. A war sun removes Planetary Shield from other players' units in its system, so all your bombarding units can then hit planets with PDS.
        - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567059/article/36598214#36598214
        - Klyvefrog · 2020-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567059/article/36598047#36598047

        - bearsfan1978 · 2020-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2543284/article/36306188#36306188
        - zamoose · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272797/article/32897620#32897620

    ### How many times does a PDS fire in one tactical action?
    - id: `space-cannon-fires-once` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Once at Space Cannon Offense, after movement, however many ships came in, plus once more at Space Cannon Defense if ground forces are committed to its planet. PDS II improves range and combat value, not the number of shots. A PDS needs no ground forces with it to fire, and having your command token in its system doesn't stop it.
      - csouth154 · 2020-05-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2437681/article/34963035#34963035

      - afrag · 2017-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890771/article/27535583#27535583
      - AgentDrake · 2021-05-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2668719/article/37819387#37819387

    ### Do Ceasefire or Disable stop Space Cannon?
    - id: `ceasefire-and-disable-vs-space-cannon` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Ceasefire only stops ships moving in, so Space Cannon, Bombardment, invasion and production all still happen. Disable is played at invasion and only shuts off PDS on the planets being invaded; Space Cannon Offense has already been fired by then.

## Movement, Anomalies and Wormholes

    ### Can I move ships partway toward the active system, or move ships that can't reach it?
- id: `ships-must-end-in-active-system` — grade: Per the rulebook — Movement Details
- A: No — every ship that moves must end its move in the active system, along a path no longer than its move value. A ship that can't get there doesn't move at all, so you can't stop short, for example to set up Space Cannon.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2020-07-20 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Clipper · 2020-07-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2469031/article/35381362#35381362
  - Jatta Pake · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888279/article/27499847#27499847
  - j76goatboy · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888279/article/27499854#27499854

    ### Can a ship leave the active system and come back in the same move, for example to fetch ground forces?
- id: `move-out-and-back-into-active-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — a ship that starts in the active system may move out and back in if its move value allows, and a ship may pass back through a system it already crossed. None of the systems it leaves or starts from may hold your command token, apart from the active system, and enemy ships still block the path.
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888889/article/27512854#27512854
  - Arael · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888889/article/27511724#27511724
  - Clipper · 2019-04-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888889/article/31738655#31738655
  - brisingre · 2017-08-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1832202/article/26675057#26675057
  - Clipper · 2019-01-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2130598/article/30964666#30964666
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915526/article/27899029#27899029

    ### What blocks my ships from moving through a system?
    - id: `what-blocks-movement` — grade: Per the rulebook — Movement Details
    - A: Any other player's ships in it, fighters included. The Living Rules Reference dropped the old exception for fighters, so even Fighter I next to a space dock blocks. Your own command tokens don't block passing through, and neither do enemy ground forces or PDS. A deal can't let you through; only effects like Light/Wave Deflector, In the Silence of Space or the Yssaril flagship do.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-10-03 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - Clipper · 2019-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2064531/article/31130860#31130860
      - Skootur · 2017-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1860109/article/27180025#27180025
      - gpoirier · 2025-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3574512/article/46593459#46593459
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903832/article/27740187#27740187

    ### Can I move more ships into a system that already has my command token on a later turn?
- id: `moving-into-system-with-own-token-later` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — ships only move into the active system, which stops being active when that tactical action ends, and you can't activate a system holding your token. A ship with move 1 can't reach a system two away in one action either; Warfare removes a token but doesn't extend movement.
  - mliszewski · 2018-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2012908/article/29359981#29359981
  - islan · 2018-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2012908/article/29360085#29360085
  - Clipper · 2020-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2347771/article/33772660#33772660

    ### Do ships move one at a time, and can I wait to see gravity rift rolls before moving others?
    - id: `moving-ships-simultaneous` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — you declare every moving ship and they move together; rift rolls come after. Fleet pool isn't checked in systems you pass through, only at the end, and you remove any excess then. The Creuss flagship is the exception: it may move before or after your other ships, but not in the middle of their moves.
      - MerlinGM · 2022-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2870489/article/40117575#40117575
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2870489/article/40118530#40118530
      - Clipper · 2018-03-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1959399/article/28574581#28574581
      - Sande24 · 2019-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302040/article/33255041#33255041
      - RedEyedGhost · 2019-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302040/article/33255060#33255060
      - DarkThomy · 2022-05-22 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2870489/article/40118098#40118098

      ### How do gravity rifts work: when do I get +1 move and when do I roll?
      - id: `gravity-rift-bonus-and-roll` — grade: Per the rulebook — Movement Details
      - A: A ship whose path moves out of or through a rift gets +1 move for that path, one per rift, even if it could otherwise only just reach the rift. Each ship that leaves or passes through a rift rolls, and is removed on a 1-3. A ship that stops in a rift gains nothing and doesn't roll until it later leaves. Trace the whole path first, then check it against the boosted move value.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-10-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - Clipper · 2019-04-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2184378/article/31721651#31721651
        - sigmazero13 · 2017-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1867132/article/27210452#27210452
        - Clipper · 2021-09-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2725400/article/38459011#38459011
        - jboot8 · 2021-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2643118/article/37518425#37518425

      ### When a carrier leaves a gravity rift, do the fighters and ground forces it carries roll too?
      - id: `gravity-rift-transported-units` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — only the ships moving out roll. Declare which ship carries what before rolling; if a carrier is lost, everything it carries goes with it, and other carriers' spare capacity can't save them. Units lost this way are removed rather than destroyed, so effects like Infantry II don't trigger.
        - maverikki · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890686/article/27564703#27564703
        - supercube3000 · 2018-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890686/article/29528798#29528798
        - Clipper · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281209/article/33005607#33005607
        - hooliganj · 2019-06-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2214777/article/32146090#32146090
        - Clipper · 2021-07-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2701339/article/38185817#38185817
        - MrDeadfisch · 2017-11-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890686/article/27538224#27538224

    ### How do nebulae work for movement, and does Gravity Drive help?
- id: `nebula-movement` — grade: Per the rulebook — Movement Details
- A: A ship may only enter a nebula if it is the active system, so it can never pass through one. A ship leaving a nebula has move 1, and bonuses like Gravity Drive or Flank Speed are then added on top.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2025-04-13 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - hooliganj · 2025-04-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3495600/article/45949110#45949110
  - Thecostcutter · 2018-02-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937587/article/28229617#28229617
  - Alwin · 2018-02-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1937587/article/28229632#28229632

    ### Can ships retreat or Skilled Retreat into a nebula?
    - id: `retreat-into-nebula` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
    - A: Disputed. A 2019 developer ruling relayed by players says yes, because the nebula restriction applies to the movement step. Others read the nebula rule literally, since a nebula is only enterable when it is the active system, and say only the Empyrean can. The relayed ruling is the stronger source.
      - Clipper · 2019-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31863415#31863415
      - hooliganj · 2019-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31805295#31805295
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31805941#31805941
      - Kazadoom · 2021-02-22 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607069/article/37069076#37069076
      - ajmarshall · 2021-02-22 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607069/article/37069836#37069836
      - Shomeking · 2021-02-22 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607069/article/37071227#37071227

    ### Who can move into or through a supernova?
    - id: `supernova-and-muaat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Nobody normally. Muaat's faction ability lets their ships move through supernovas; Magmus Reactor lets ships move into and stop in one. A Nekro who copies Magmus Reactor can move into or out of a supernova but not through it. A supernova never destroys ships already there, so losing the technology later changes nothing.
      - Nagual84 · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679726/article/37945945#37945945
      - Marcial · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679726/article/37947321#37947321
      - rico761028 · 2017-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1833544/article/26691310#26691310
      - Dulkal · 2017-08-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1833544/article/26698019#26698019
      - Clipper · 2019-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2326060/article/33533753#33533753
      - Vidyaraja · 2017-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26946549#26946549

    ### If I lose Antimass Deflectors while my ships sit in an asteroid field, or place units in an anomaly, what happens?
- id: `anomalies-restrict-moving-in-only` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Nothing — anomaly rules restrict moving into or through them, not being in them or moving out. Ships already there stay and may leave normally, and effects can place units in anomalies.
  - MollyMae · 2022-01-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2790403/article/39201889#39201889
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2790403/article/39202312#39202312
  - Cacotopos · 2020-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2568967/article/36616857#36616857
  - Kazadoom · 2020-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2568967/article/36617561#36617561

      ### Which systems count as anomalies?
      - id: `which-systems-are-anomalies` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Only tiles with a red border: asteroid fields, nebulae, gravity rifts and supernovas. Empty systems and wormhole systems are not anomalies unless bordered. Antimass Deflectors is the technology that lets ships move into and through asteroid fields.
        - Clipper · 2021-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2713673/article/38325263#38325263
        - mpappy · 2022-05-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2860757/article/40003255#40003255
        - Dirzuga · 2022-05-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2860757/article/40003273#40003273

      ### How do wormholes work, and must my ships use them?
      - id: `wormholes-are-optional-adjacency` — grade: Per the rulebook — Movement Details
      - A: Systems with matching wormholes are adjacent for all purposes, movement, Space Cannon and neighbors included. A wormhole only adds connections: you don't have to use it, and a wormhole system can be activated and moved through like any other. Being in one wormhole system doesn't put you next to the systems around the matching one.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-06-09 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - hooliganj · 2017-10-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1857659/article/27063292#27063292
        - TwoAMArts · 2023-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3140194/article/42831286#42831286
        - csouth154 · 2019-10-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2292740/article/33144380#33144380
        - PistonLoop · 2022-07-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2893227/article/40373659#40373659

    ### Can other players use the Creuss Gate and the Creuss flagship's delta wormhole?
    - id: `creuss-wormholes-usable-by-all` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — delta wormholes follow normal rules, so anyone can travel between the Creuss Gate, the Creuss home system and the flagship's system. Since the flagship's system contains a Creuss ship, enemies need a way to move through it (such as Light/Wave Deflector) or must stop and fight there.
      - AgentDrake · 2019-05-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2199935/article/31937196#31937196
      - Klauseren · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899779/article/27679496#27679496
      - hooliganj · 2023-07-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3117915/article/42621989#42621989
      - hooliganj · 2019-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2133457/article/31008054#31008054
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1885871/article/27467942#27467942
      - Clipper · 2018-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946217/article/28366880#28366880

  ### How does the Creuss flagship's 'move before or after your other ships' work?
  - id: `creuss-flagship-movement` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Its system counts as containing a delta wormhole. If it moves into the active system first, that system becomes adjacent to other delta wormholes such as the Creuss home system, so your other ships can come in through it; or it can move after them. It can also use its own wormhole, for example to go home. If it moves first through a gravity rift and is destroyed, the wormhole is gone before the others move.
    - IncrediSteve · 2017-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1835568/article/26722226#26722226
    - Weltenreiter · 2017-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1835568/article/26722270#26722270
    - truephoenyx · 2017-08-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1839583/article/26782163#26782163
    - CBpegasus · 2017-08-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1839583/article/26782448#26782448
    - HP12C_guy · 2023-08-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3138223/article/42814430#42814430
    - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921381/article/27989106#27989106

    ### What exactly does Creuss's Quantum Entanglement connect, and can others use it?
    - id: `creuss-quantum-entanglement-scope` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: For the Creuss only, systems with alpha wormholes and systems with beta wormholes are adjacent to each other; no game effect, Enforced Travel Ban included, can stop that. Other players can't fire Space Cannon or move through that connection.
      - Clipper · 2019-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2338680/article/33673951#33673951
      - Clipper · 2018-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28270111#28270111
      - pkitty · 2022-07-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2904454/article/40497599#40497599
      - donmakaron · 2021-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2776307/article/39048675#39048675
      - zamoose · 2021-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2776307/article/39054719#39054719
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2278073/article/32963790#32963790

    ### Does Enforced Travel Ban stop Creuss's Slipstream bonus?
    - id: `creuss-slipstream-and-travel-ban` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Slipstream only checks for a wormhole in the ship's starting system; it isn't a wormhole effect, according to a relayed developer ruling.
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28313682#28313682
      - Clipper · 2018-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28270386#28270386
      - Santiago · 2018-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28271373#28271373
      - dheck · 2018-02-14 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28270247#28270247

    ### How does Lost Star Chart work, including with Enforced Travel Ban?
- id: `lost-star-chart` — grade: Community consensus
- A: For the tactical action, every alpha and beta wormhole system is adjacent to every other, for everyone, including other players' Space Cannon. It creates its own adjacency, which a relayed FFG answer says still works under Enforced Travel Ban.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28313682#28313682
  - IncrediSteve · 2018-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946546/article/28370977#28370977
  - Clipper · 2018-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946546/article/28370999#28370999
  - IncrediSteve · 2018-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911027/article/27840616#27840616
  - Clipper · 2018-02-15 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1940243/article/28272502#28272502

    ### Do Flank Speed and Gravity Drive stack?
- id: `movement-bonuses-stack` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — Gravity Drive gives +1 to one ship and Flank Speed +1 to all your ships, so that ship can get +2, plus other bonuses. Two Flank Speeds can't be played together, though.
  - Clipper · 2019-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2141340/article/31119789#31119789
  - Somnambulix · 2019-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2141340/article/31120406#31120406
  - Clipper · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953996/article/28488101#28488101

      ### Do movement effects like Gravity Drive and gravity rifts apply to Saar Floating Factories?
      - id: `saar-floating-factory-movement-effects` — grade: Designer ruling
      - A: Yes — the developer ruled that anything affecting movement affects Floating Factories and the ships moving with them, so they get Gravity Drive, Flank Speed and rift bonuses, and he later confirmed they roll when leaving a gravity rift. Separately, players read a lone Floating Factory as not blocking enemy movement, since it isn't a ship.
        - CreussEmissary · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27882476#27882476
        - iswearihaveajob · 2018-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921381/article/27996331#27996331
        - sigmazero13 · 2018-07-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/29602865#29602865
        - IncrediSteve · 2024-06-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921381/article/44357463#44357463
        - Clipper · 2018-05-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1994395/article/29093997#29093997
        - Xelvonar · 2018-01-17 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1921381/article/27984535#27984535

    ### When an ability moves units outside a tactical action, like the Argent or L1Z1X hero, do normal movement rules apply?
    - id: `ability-movement-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only the ability's own text applies: move values, the command-token restriction and other tactical-action movement rules are ignored, and the moving ships can transport units that start with them. Anomaly rules still apply, so no ability moves ships into a supernova unless it says so.
      - hooliganj · 2021-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2677795/article/37926710#37926710
      - jeffwagner · 2021-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2677795/article/37953939#37953939

      - ajmarshall · 2021-10-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2733997/article/38561698#38561698
      - AgentDrake · 2025-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3569517/article/46552056#46552056
      - hannibal555 · 2020-12-28 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567576/article/36602688#36602688

    ### How do I move ground forces from one planet to another in the same system?
- id: `moving-ground-forces-between-planets` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Make a tactical action on that system: a ship with capacity, already there or moving in, picks them up during movement, and they land on the other planet in the invasion step, even if you already control it. Transit Diodes is the other way.
  - ajmarshall · 2020-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2367786/article/34023499#34023499
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2367786/article/34023713#34023713

  - Wakke · 2018-12-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2108557/article/30671344#30671344
  - Clipper · 2020-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2524074/article/36068640#36068640

      ### Is a system adjacent to itself, and are planets in the same system adjacent?
      - id: `system-not-adjacent-to-itself` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: A system is never adjacent to itself, so being in a system doesn't count as adjacent to it. Planets are treated as adjacent to other planets in their system and in neighboring systems, which matters for effects like Xxcha's Peace Accords.
        - Johnnymaxx · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891316/article/27541967#27541967
