## Fleet Pool, Capacity and Transport

    ### When is the fleet pool limit checked, and can I bring extra ships into a fight and trim afterwards?
    - id: `fleet-pool-when-checked` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: At all times, except for ships that are only moving through other systems. Once ships arrive in the active system, any non-fighter ships over your fleet pool are removed straight away, before Space Cannon Offense and combat, so you can't use extras to soak hits.
      - AgentDrake · 2021-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2756003/article/38815458#38815458
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2047341/article/29833511#29833511
      - Clipper · 2018-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946560/article/28371100#28371100
      - Clipper · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581681/article/36761080#36761080
      - AgentDrake · 2021-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2627916/article/37338234#37338234

    ### Can I deliberately go over my fleet pool by moving or producing, then remove ships of my choice?
    - id: `exceeding-fleet-pool-deliberately` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — nothing stops you; you just remove the excess right away, choosing which. That lets you, say, build a new ship in a full system and scrap an old one, or move extra carriers in to deliver ground forces. Production itself isn't capped by the fleet pool; it is checked once the units are placed.
      - IncrediSteve · 2025-12-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3636391/article/47087923#47087923
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-09-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2511121/article/35912290#35912290
      - Clipper · 2018-08-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2048048/article/29845289#29845289
      - garfielder · 2017-09-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1854697/article/27036594#27036594
      - Strategery21 · 2024-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3222363/article/43576514#43576514
      - JamesG · 2017-10-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1854697/article/27134294#27134294

### If an effect cuts my fleet pool, like Neuroglaive or Fleet Regulations, what happens to the excess ships, and does anyone get credit for them?
- id: `fleet-pool-reduction-destroyed` — grade: Community consensus
- A: You lose the token at once and must bring every system back within the new limit right away. Ships removed this way are destroyed, but no player destroyed them, so 'when you destroy' effects and objectives don't trigger for whoever caused it. Neuroglaive can even drop a fleet pool to zero.
  - adrim · 2019-04-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2178639/article/31641296#31641296
  - Clipper · 2018-04-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1972196/article/28763148#28763148
  - Clipper · 2018-04-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1972196/article/28764393#28764393
  - Clipper · 2018-06-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2011582/article/29339385#29339385
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2011582/article/29339374#29339374

    ### What does capacity cover, and which units have it?
- id: `capacity-what-it-covers` — grade: Per the rulebook — Fleet Pool, Capacity, and Transport
- A: Capacity holds fighters and ground forces in the space area, in any mix: Cruiser II's or a dreadnought's capacity can hold fighters too. Any unit with a capacity value can transport, including some flagships, war suns and faction units; check the faction sheet. Ground forces on planets need no capacity.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-27 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Weltenreiter · 2018-06-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2017539/article/29424243#29424243
  - GAFBlizzard · 2025-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3560329/article/46481204#46481204
  - gpoirier · 2025-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3560119/article/46479839#46479839

    ### Does a space dock have capacity?
    - id: `space-dock-fighter-allowance` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — a normal space dock lets up to 3 fighters in its system ignore capacity, so you can build fighters there with no ships to carry them. It holds no ground forces and adds nothing to your carriers; two docks in one system cover 6 fighters. The Saar Floating Factory works differently, with real capacity of 4.
      - ColtsFan76 · 2022-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2918671/article/40640141#40640141
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801050/article/39325183#39325183
      - Cacotopos · 2022-05-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2869529/article/40111693#40111693
      - Mr Skeletor · 2017-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1887161/article/27481776#27481776
      - dizzybit · 2019-11-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2304602/article/33285052#33285052
      - Negatis · 2018-06-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2015704/article/29398046#29398046

    ### Can fighters stay in a system without a carrier or space dock?
    - id: `fighters-without-capacity` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Not normal fighters: any beyond the capacity of your ships and space docks there are removed. Fighter II can move and stay on its own, but Fighter IIs that don't fit in capacity count against your fleet pool like other ships. 
Letnev's Armada bonus is for non-fighter ships only, so it doesn't extend that.
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556076/article/36463682#36463682
      - AgentDrake · 2020-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556076/article/36463843#36463843
      - IncrediSteve · 2024-07-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3341039/article/44627038#44627038
      - Clipper · 2020-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2509203/article/35886892#35886892
      - csouth154 · 2019-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2295777/article/33183089#33183089
      - AgentDrake · 2019-07-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2238698/article/32470311#32470311

    ### Can Fighter IIs move on their own into a system already at my fleet limit?
    - id: `fighter-ii-moving-alone` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Fighter IIs move independently, and fleet pool and capacity are checked once all movement is done. If ships in the destination have room for them, they don't count against your fleet pool.
      - StrangeBeacon · 2026-04-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3688346/article/47512162#47512162
      - Clipper · 2022-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2970918/article/41159807#41159807
      - Clipper · 2022-11-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556076/article/41154436#41154436
      - McBartsomeville · 2023-07-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3122818/article/42665386#42665386
      - DauntlessK · 2019-06-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2214402/article/32134873#32134873

    ### Where can a moving ship pick up and drop off units?
- id: `transport-pickup-and-dropoff` — grade: Per the rulebook — Fleet Pool, Capacity, and Transport
- A: It can pick up fighters and ground forces in the system it starts in, every system it passes through and the active system, but not from any other system with your command token. Units come off only in the active system, after the ship finishes moving; it can't drop a load and go back for more. Fighters that don't fit stay behind.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-08-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888889/article/29853860#29853860
  - MollyMae · 2019-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2130229/article/30960360#30960360
  - Benj_ · 2018-09-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2065935/article/30085762#30085762
  - TerTer · 2020-04-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2406834/article/34526860#34526860

      ### Can a carrier pick up more units than its capacity so the excess are destroyed, or leave fighters behind to die?
      - id: `over-capacity-to-destroy-own-units` — grade: Designer ruling
      - A: You can't pick up more than the moving ships' capacity, so you can't overload to destroy your own units. But you can load ground forces and leave fighters or infantry already in space behind; capacity is a system total, so those left over are removed if what remains can't hold them.
        - CreussEmissary · 2018-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30368952#30368952
        - CreussEmissary · 2018-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30369222#30369222
        - sigmazero13 · 2018-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30363283#30363283
        - Soul_ · 2018-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30348810#30348810
        - 0 hr · 2018-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2084855/article/30364552#30364552

    ### After ships stop moving, are fighters and ground forces still tied to the ship that carried them?
    - id: `units-not-tied-to-ships-after-moving` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — only while moving (and when leaving a gravity rift). Afterwards only the total capacity in the system matters, so if a carrier is lost, the cargo survives if other ships have room, and you choose which excess units to remove. Units removed for lack of capacity are removed, not destroyed, so Infantry II doesn't trigger.
      - Clipper · 2019-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2270506/article/32872522#32872522
      - Clipper · 2021-03-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2625554/article/37307154#37307154
      - TinTeardrop · 2020-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2356673/article/33886009#33886009
      - THLY · 2019-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2281209/article/33000233#33000233
      - THLY · 2021-12-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2774634/article/39034803#39034803
      - afrag · 2019-09-05 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2270506/article/32872605#32872605

      ### When is capacity checked if a carrier is lost?
      - id: `capacity-checked-outside-combat` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Immediately whenever it isn't combat, for example after each player's Space Cannon Offense volley, before the next player fires. During combat fighters and ground forces may exceed capacity and keep fighting; the excess is removed when the space combat ends, before invasion. So if all your ships die, their ground forces can't invade.
        - csouth154 · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273270/article/32905270#32905270
        - Clipper · 2021-08-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2715851/article/38351047#38351047
        - Clipper · 2019-06-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2216228/article/32164914#32164914
        - hooliganj · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891664/article/27547768#27547768
        - k8rthik · 2026-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3640296/article/47117343#47117343
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-06-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2453062/article/35174709#35174709

    ### Can ground forces stay aboard ships in space instead of landing?
- id: `ground-forces-stay-in-space` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — committing them is optional, and they can stay in space between turns as long as your ships there have enough capacity in total. Ground forces placed in space by effects like the Sol flagship don't fight in space combat; they wait for a later invasion or ride along when your ships move.
  - raptor666 · 2024-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3410738/article/45230186#45230186
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904019/article/27743331#27743331
  - One_Wolf · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904019/article/27743344#27743344
  - Sirusx715 · 2022-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2899632/article/40441651#40441651
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1943281/article/28314126#28314126

    ### With Light/Wave Deflector, can I pick up my ground forces from a system that has enemy ships?
    - id: `lightwave-deflector-pickup` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — moving through such a system is allowed, and ships can pick up units in any system they pass through. Blockades only limit production.
      - halbower · 2017-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899375/article/27674638#27674638
      - Clipper · 2017-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899375/article/27674952#27674952

## Trade, Transactions and Deals

      ### Who can make transactions, and when?
      - id: `who-can-transact-and-when` — grade: Per the rulebook — The Economy: Resources, Trade Goods, and Commodities
      - A: During the action phase, only the active player can transact, and only with neighbors: one transaction with each neighbor per turn. A transaction trades any trade goods and commodities plus at most one promissory note. Deals that don't exchange components can be made with anyone at any time.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2023-02-22 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - GAFBlizzard · 2023-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3033326/article/41777044#41777044

    ### Is the one-transaction limit per turn or per round?
- id: `one-transaction-per-turn-not-round` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Per turn. A round covers all four phases; a turn is one player's turn in the action phase. So two neighbors can trade on one player's turn and again on the other's.
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552962/article/36423337#36423337
  - Clipper · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552962/article/36423345#36423345

      ### Can players make transactions with non-neighbors during the agenda phase?
      - id: `agenda-phase-transactions-non-neighbors` — grade: Designer ruling
      - A: Yes — while each agenda is being resolved, from its reveal until its outcome resolves, every pair of players may make one transaction, neighbors or not. That doesn't make them neighbors for anything else, as the developer confirmed when asked about Mentak's Pillage.
        - CreussEmissary · 2019-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2132987/article/31003989#31003989
        - hooliganj · 2019-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2132987/article/31000799#31000799
        - AgentDrake · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554619/article/36445405#36445405
        - Clipper · 2018-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2106796/article/30640330#30640330
        - hooliganj · 2017-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1898210/article/27659092#27659092

    ### Can players trade action cards?
- id: `trading-action-cards` — grade: Per the rulebook — The Economy: Resources, Trade Goods, and Commodities
- A: No — transactions cover only trade goods, commodities and promissory notes. The Hacan are the exception: their faction ability lets them include action cards, still within the one-transaction-per-player limit.
  - AgentDrake · 2026-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3638644/article/47103830#47103830

    ### When is a deal binding?
    - id: `when-is-a-deal-binding` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only when its terms resolve immediately — for example, taking a trade good not to fire Space Cannon at the moment you decide whether to fire binds you not to fire. Promises about future actions, and if-then conditions, are non-binding and can be broken. You can bind yourself to an act, but not to its result, such as a card that might be Sabotaged.
      - Duskwalker · 2017-08-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831975/article/26728401#26728401
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831975/article/26737847#26737847
      - Sande24 · 2017-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831975/article/26738505#26738505
      - IncrediSteve · 2023-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3033326/article/41771978#41771978
      - pkitty · 2023-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3033326/article/41776783#41776783
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-08-25 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831975/article/26738418#26738418

    ### Is a deal to buy someone's votes binding?
    - id: `vote-deals-binding` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only for a player about to cast votes right now. Paying the next voter in exchange for their vote binds it, since nothing can happen in between; a promise to vote a certain way later can still be broken.
      - Clipper · 2020-10-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528426/article/36122279#36122279
      - Dulkal · 2020-10-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528426/article/36122282#36122282
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892601/article/28288685#28288685
      - hooliganj · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27868811#27868811
      - Xelvonar · 2018-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1933501/article/28161577#28161577

### Must deals and negotiations happen openly at the table?
- id: `private-negotiations` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The rules don't say. Whether players may negotiate in private is up to your group.
  - hooliganj · 2024-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3225074/article/43604198#43604198
  - rubberchicken · 2024-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3225074/article/43604232#43604232

    ### What can be exchanged in a transaction?
- id: `what-can-be-traded` — grade: Per the rulebook — The Economy: Resources, Trade Goods, and Commodities
- A: Any number of trade goods and commodities, plus one promissory note from each side, and relic fragments with Prophecy of Kings. Technologies, units, planets, laws, command tokens, the speaker token and secret objectives can't be traded; action cards only when the Hacan are one of the parties, in any number and either direction. A transaction may be lopsided, even a gift.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2021-06-04 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - zamoose · 2024-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3262517/article/43931276#43931276
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-11-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1884263/article/27442369#27442369
  - Duskwalker · 2019-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142058/article/31130570#31130570
  - CBpegasus · 2017-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845590/article/26869914#26869914

    ### Is a transaction an action, and when during a turn can it happen?
    - id: `transaction-not-an-action` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It isn't an action — the active player can make one with each neighbor at any point in their turn, even mid-combat or between the parts of a strategy card, and still take their action. Either side may propose it, and it counts against the active player's limit with that player. Two non-active players can't transact with each other.
      - TerTer · 2020-06-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2441509/article/35016412#35016412
      - DrewDecimal · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27867934#27867934
      - chrisdk · 2025-10-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3592561/article/46738820#46738820
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563872/article/36564990#36564990
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2367710/article/34022288#34022288
      - Sande24 · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891652/article/27546581#27546581

    ### Can players make transactions in the strategy or status phase?
- id: `no-transactions-strategy-status` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — transactions happen only in the action phase (active player with neighbors) and the agenda phase (anyone). Players can still talk and make non-binding deals at any time, even during galaxy setup.
  - chrisdk · 2026-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3692178/article/47542483#47542483
  - Milty · 2020-07-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2463351/article/35310778#35310778
  - Marcial · 2020-07-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2463351/article/35311796#35311796
  - Vidyaraja · 2017-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26946549#26946549
  - Clipper · 2020-05-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2426859/article/34803337#34803337

    ### Who counts as my neighbor?
    - id: `who-are-neighbors` — grade: Per the rulebook — The Economy: Resources, Trade Goods, and Commodities
    - A: Players with a unit or controlled planet in the same system or adjacent systems, wormholes included, so two players fighting in a system are neighbors. Ships moving through systems don't create neighbors on the way, since they are never in those systems. The Hacan can transact with non-neighbors, and anyone can with them, but that doesn't make them neighbors for Pillage.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-08-26 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - hooliganj · 2024-10-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3386706/article/45018330#45018330
      - Clipper · 2020-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2367804/article/34023772#34023772
      - hooliganj · 2021-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2661503/article/37734941#37734941
      - IncrediSteve · 2019-10-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2293363/article/33151939#33151939

    ### When do commodities turn into trade goods, and can I give them and get them back in one transaction?
    - id: `commodities-become-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: A commodity becomes a trade good the moment another player receives it, whether by transaction, Pillage or another effect. A transaction is one simultaneous exchange, so commodities you receive can't be sent back in it; that would be a second transaction, which must wait for another turn. You can only give what you actually have.
      - Clipper · 2018-05-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1995963/article/29116140#29116140
      - Clipper · 2021-02-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2602981/article/37019453#37019453
      - hooliganj · 2025-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3594448/article/46754367#46754367
      - IncrediSteve · 2025-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3610983/article/46887145#46887145
      - Clipper · 2018-01-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919622/article/27963453#27963453
      - greendayfan333 · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956364/article/28523751#28523751

    ### Can I trade on a promissory note I received from another player, and must I show it?
    - id: `trading-received-promissory-notes` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — any note in your hand can be traded on, and you needn't show it, though the recipient may want to see it first. If the deal named a specific note, you must hand over that one. Notes face up in your play area, like Support for the Throne, can't be traded or handed back voluntarily.
      - kenilazarus · 2018-11-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2094060/article/30468918#30468918
      - IndyBart · 2018-11-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2094060/article/30468214#30468214
      - lauwegast · 2020-10-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2094060/article/36113907#36113907
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1974928/article/28806190#28806190
      - zamoose · 2023-04-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3072115/article/42161193#42161193
      - CBpegasus · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903689/article/27738078#27738078

    ### Is replenishing other players' commodities with Trade a transaction?
    - id: `trade-strategy-replenish-not-transaction` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — it is a strategy card effect: recipients needn't be neighbors, can't refuse, it doesn't use up a transaction, and Mentak can't pillage it as a transaction. The Trade player may still transact during their strategic action, even after the secondaries.
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-10-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947910/article/40933698#40933698
      - Clipper · 2022-10-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947910/article/40934224#40934224
      - Usul_Theg · 2022-10-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947910/article/40990097#40990097
      - Clipper · 2020-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2491537/article/35675385#35675385
      - TheImmortal87 · 2022-10-08 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947910/article/40934135#40934135

    ### Is a deal like 'take these trade goods and don't attack me this turn' binding?
- id: `deal-not-to-attack` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — a promise covering a period of time can't be resolved immediately, so it can be broken. To make something binding, pay for a specific immediate act, such as choosing not to fire Space Cannon right now, or trade for a promissory note like Ceasefire. A deal for future payment is non-binding too. Deals can never override rules like mandatory combat rolls.
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27868127#27868127
  - oatesatm · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27868286#27868286
  - Sande24 · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27875265#27875265
  - Clipper · 2018-05-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1992613/article/29066228#29066228
  - Simboubou · 2017-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892107/article/27554559#27554559
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-05 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913123/article/27880078#27880078

### Can I broker a trade between two players who aren't neighbors?
- id: `three-way-transactions` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only as two separate transactions, one with each, and since you get one transaction per neighbor per turn, part of it will rest on a non-binding promise.
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1863072/article/27148186#27148186
  - broran · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1863072/article/27147484#27147484

    ### Can a planet's resources and influence both be spent, or spent twice?
    - id: `spending-planets-one-use` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — exhausting a planet spends either its resources or its influence, never both, and an exhausted planet can't be spent again until something readies it. Trade goods can stand in for either resources or influence.
      - IncrediSteve · 2026-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3712262/article/47714285#47714285
      - hooliganj · 2026-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3712262/article/47714897#47714897
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2773661/article/39018634#39018634
      - THLY · 2021-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2773661/article/39019889#39019889
      - hooliganj · 2023-11-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3189939/article/43280816#43280816
      - isterbat · 2023-11-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3189939/article/43281763#43281763

    ### How does Mentak's Mirror Computing interact with Bribery or spend-trade-goods objectives?
    - id: `mirror-computing-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Each trade good is worth 2 when spent as resources or influence, but still 1 when spent as trade goods, so Bribery and objectives that spend trade goods get no bonus.
      - Clipper · 2021-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722342/article/38422460#38422460
      - hooliganj · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272913/article/32899180#32899180
      - IncrediSteve · 2018-07-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2030947/article/29611237#29611237

    ### Is returning a used promissory note to its owner a transaction?
    - id: `returning-used-note-not-transaction` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — returning it is part of resolving the card, so it needs no agreement and happens even if you are no longer neighbors.
      - Blaze_Of_Glory · 2022-08-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2924734/article/40700727#40700727
      - HeavyGear · 2022-08-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2924734/article/40700767#40700767
      - DistantOasis · 2022-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2924734/article/40708807#40708807

    ### How often can Sol's Military Support be used?
    - id: `military-support-once-per-sol-turn` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: At most once per Sol turn: it is used at the start of Sol's turn and then returns to Sol. If Sol trades it out again that turn, the start of that turn has passed, so it waits until Sol's next turn.
      - chrisdk · 2023-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3099937/article/42442696#42442696
      - isterbat · 2023-06-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3099937/article/42443307#42443307


## Producing Units

    ### How many units can a space dock produce?
    - id: `space-dock-production-value` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: A Space Dock I has Production equal to its planet's resource value plus 2 (plus 4 with Space Dock II), so on a 3-resource planet it builds up to 5 units. The planet being exhausted doesn't matter.
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1830979/article/26656721#26656721
      - Weltenreiter · 2017-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1830979/article/26656748#26656748
      - Clipper · 2020-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2520136/article/36021476#36021476
      - IncrediSteve · 2018-09-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2053270/article/29913354#29913354
      - Pete Walsh · 2018-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920559/article/27973242#27973242
      - Clipper · 2018-06-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2010379/article/29324641#29324641

    ### Does Production limit the number of units or their total cost?
    - id: `production-limits-units-not-cost` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The number of units. Each fighter or infantry counts as one unit, even though two cost 1 resource, so a war sun uses one point of Production and you pay its 12 cost separately. The value isn't used up: you can produce up to it every time you get a chance to produce there.
      - dradice · 2018-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1932047/article/28141538#28141538
      - DrewDecimal · 2018-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1932047/article/28141569#28141569
      - AgentDrake · 2018-12-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2119249/article/30817681#30817681
      - dradice · 2020-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2371729/article/34072621#34072621
      - Clipper · 2020-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2443469/article/35043041#35043041
      - Clipper · 2022-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801050/article/39365509#39365509

    ### When producing, can I exhaust planets outside the active system to pay?
- id: `pay-with-any-planets` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — spend resources from any planets you control, wherever they are, plus trade goods. Only the number of units is limited by the Production in the active system.
  - Painkeeper · 2019-07-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2235833/article/32432608#32432608
  - Clipper · 2019-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2253272/article/32665008#32665008
  - Jatta Pake · 2017-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1895156/article/27602674#27602674
  - Clipper · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903178/article/27732006#27732006
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302242/article/33259468#33259468

    ### Do the Production values of several units in the active system add together?
- id: `production-pooled-in-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — all your Production in the active system is pooled into one production: two space docks of 4 and 3 give 7, and two Arborec infantry with Production 1 can build 2 infantry for 1 resource, even on different planets. You work out the total before producing, so units produced in that step can't add their own Production to it.
  - Clipper · 2018-05-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1989588/article/29024319#29024319
  - MollyMae · 2019-01-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2134935/article/31029637#31029637
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1995897/article/29115569#29115569
  - jeffwagner · 2017-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838752/article/26769557#26769557
  - Clipper · 2018-07-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2029620/article/29591441#29591441
  - halbower · 2017-08-28 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838752/article/26769331#26769331

    ### Where are produced ground forces placed?
    - id: `where-produced-ground-forces-go` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: On a planet that holds a unit that used Production, never on other planets in the system. With several producing planets you may split them among those planets however you like, even all on one. If the producing unit is in space, like a Saar Floating Factory or Letani Warriors aboard ships, they can go in that space area or on any planet you control in the system.
      - tommygunn2011 · 2018-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2049089/article/29858563#29858563
      - Ndjcirnc · 2022-10-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2946009/article/40913796#40913796
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2536336/article/36222789#36222789
      - Clipper · 2020-05-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2437749/article/34966657#34966657
      - ajmarshall · 2020-05-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2430436/article/34855000#34855000
      - TerTer · 2020-05-29 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2437749/article/34964677#34964677

    ### Can ground forces I produce take an uncontrolled planet in the same tactical action?
    - id: `produced-units-cannot-invade` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — production is the last step, after invasion, and produced ground forces only go where described above. They can invade in a later action.
      - christhomas226 · 2020-09-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2509883/article/35896058#35896058
      - Dulkal · 2020-09-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2509883/article/35896109#35896109
      - pedro2112 · 2021-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586120/article/36815436#36815436
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2342464/article/33711588#33711588

    ### Can I produce at my home space dock during a tactical action elsewhere, or at a system I activated earlier?
- id: `produce-only-in-active-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the production step only uses units in the system you just activated. Producing without activating needs a specific effect such as the Warfare secondary or Sling Relay.
  - zamoose · 2019-09-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2284475/article/33041404#33041404
  - tommygunn2011 · 2019-09-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2284475/article/33041417#33041417
  - Clipper · 2018-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2047011/article/29827580#29827580
  - McBartsomeville · 2025-03-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3483492/article/45847149#45847149
  - HP12C_guy · 2025-03-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3483492/article/45847162#45847162

    ### Can I produce ships at a space dock when enemy ships are in that system?
- id: `blockade-production` — grade: Per the rulebook — Producing Units
- A: No — a dock (or other Production unit) in a system with another player's ships and none of yours is blockaded and can only produce ground forces. You keep the planet, and your PDS still fire. If Space Cannon or combat clears the enemy ships before the production step, you produce normally. Ground forces alone, without ships, never blockade.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-11 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899555/article/27685002#27685002
  - Lowecore · 2023-04-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3058384/article/42028273#42028273
  - halbower · 2020-07-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2459980/article/35262516#35262516

    ### Can I build space docks or PDS with Production?
- id: `structures-not-produced` — grade: Per the rulebook — Control, Structures, and the Custodians Token
- A: No — unlike earlier editions, structures can't be built with Production; they come mainly from the Construction strategy card. A planet holds at most one space dock and two PDS, Mecatol Rex included.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-12-12 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - brettellmd · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2153470/article/31286925#31286925
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3427053/article/45373316#45373316
  - trud · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907990/article/27802140#27802140
  - Maarten Jeursen · 2018-10-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2076837/article/30236061#30236061

    ### If all my space docks or PDS are on the board, can I place another?
    - id: `moving-structures-when-out` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only by moving one: if none are in reinforcements, you may take one from a system without your command token and place it where the effect directs. You can't otherwise scuttle your own units, and nothing is refunded.
      - McBartsomeville · 2024-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3268313/article/43982845#43982845
      - hooliganj · 2024-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3268313/article/43982996#43982996
      - mliszewski · 2018-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2116817/article/30791611#30791611
      - Bizud · 2017-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1905898/article/27776986#27776986
      - Mordenthral · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1913529/article/27874627#27874627
      - SpookyNoodlez · 2024-04-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3282976/article/44111699#44111699

    ### Do units 'placed' by an effect cost resources, and do they count as producing?
    - id: `produce-vs-place` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both. An effect that says 'produce' means you pay the cost unless it says otherwise. 'Place' or 'gain' is free and isn't producing, so Muaat's Star Forge doesn't count as producing and doesn't trigger Magmus Reactor's trade good.
      - toskk · 2025-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3595458/article/46760675#46760675
      - Cacotopos · 2025-10-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3595458/article/46760803#46760803
      - Xelvonar · 2017-12-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894386/article/27593789#27593789
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2481206/article/35535603#35535603
      - Marcial · 2021-04-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2636561/article/37439590#37439590
      - AgentDrake · 2018-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2041573/article/29753484#29753484

    ### How does Sarween Tools work?
- id: `sarween-tools-rules` — grade: Community consensus
- A: It cuts the combined cost of one use of Production by 1, not 1 per unit or per dock, and can take it to zero, so 2 infantry or 2 fighters can be free. It needs a unit's Production ability, so it doesn't apply to effects that just let you produce, like Sling Relay, but works with the Warfare secondary.
  - hooliganj · 2018-04-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1984033/article/28949151#28949151
  - halbower · 2020-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2385019/article/34238726#34238726
  - hooliganj · 2018-03-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1961071/article/28600627#28600627
  - jradziew · 2019-07-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2238358/article/32465381#32465381
  - Ressless · 2024-06-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3311149/article/44357026#44357026
  - Clipper · 2020-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2340276/article/33689778#33689778

    ### Can a destroyed flagship be rebuilt?
    - id: `rebuilding-flagship` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it goes back to reinforcements and can be produced again; you're only limited to the one piece.
      - corkysru · 2018-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2103121/article/30587313#30587313
      - hooliganj · 2018-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2103121/article/30587315#30587315
      - Clipper · 2019-09-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2284540/article/33042085#33042085

    ### When all my plastic fighters or infantry are on the board, can I move some to where I'm producing?
- id: `taking-plastic-for-production` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — you may remove them from systems without your command token and produce them instead of using tokens. Tokens are optional, and each location using tokens still needs at least one plastic piece of that type.
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-03-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3260802/article/43914526#43914526
  - Marcial · 2024-03-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3260802/article/43914497#43914497
  - Clipper · 2021-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2706617/article/38247310#38247310


## Technology

    ### Does the Codex (Omega) Magen Defense Grid still work if the PDS on the planet has been hit by Disable?
    - id: `magen-defense-grid-disabled-pds` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Disable only strips the PDS's Planetary Shield and Space Cannon; it is still a structure, so Magen Defense Grid still produces its hit. That hit lands at the start of ground combat, before dice are rolled, so the destroyed unit never rolls. With only structures on the planet there's no ground combat and Magen can't trigger.
      - csouth154 · 2020-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2443173/article/35040019#35040019
      - sigmazero13 · 2020-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2443173/article/35043814#35043814
      - IncrediSteve · 2026-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3693883/article/47556951#47556951
      - GAFBlizzard · 2026-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3693883/article/47557204#47557204
      - haeberich · 2020-06-07 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2443173/article/35040128#35040128

    ### Does Graviton Laser System do anything during Space Cannon Defense?
    - id: `graviton-laser-system-space-cannon-defense` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — you may exhaust it, but the only targets during Space Cannon Defense are the invading ground forces, so there are never non-fighter ships to force hits onto.
      - hooliganj · 2025-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3578005/article/46623310#46623310
      - Falkman · 2025-09-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3578005/article/46622555#46622555

    ### How does Duranium Armor work?
    - id: `duranium-armor-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Once per combat round, after hits are assigned, it repairs one of your damaged units in that combat that did not use Sustain Damage that round, even if you had no hits to assign. Only units with Sustain Damage can be damaged, so only they benefit: ships in space combat, mechs in ground combat, never units elsewhere on the board. It isn't an extra hit point: a damaged unit that takes another hit is destroyed first. A unit damaged by Space Cannon before combat can be repaired at the end of round one.
      - Clipper · 2023-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3053577/article/41976067#41976067
      - Clipper · 2021-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2718518/article/38379756#38379756
      - Clipper · 2020-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2433194/article/34907591#34907591
      - stugal · 2019-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2270275/article/32868836#32868836
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904150/article/27745743#27745743
      - hooliganj · 2018-01-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912507/article/27858019#27858019

### Does Valkyrie Particle Weave trigger if I cancel all the opponent's hits?
- id: `valkyrie-particle-weave-cancelled-hits` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — it needs the opponent to produce hits, and they did; cancelling them afterwards doesn't undo that.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38201901#38201901
  - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38203253#38203253

    ### How does Graviton Laser System affect Space Cannon hits?
    - id: `graviton-laser-system-hits` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It makes Space Cannon hits be assigned to non-fighter ships when possible. They are still ordinary hits, so Sustain Damage can cancel them before assignment. It doesn't affect fighters or ground forces in ground combat, Naalu fighters included.
      - unethrope · 2018-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920866/article/27976837#27976837
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920866/article/27978740#27978740
      - Clipper · 2018-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920866/article/30710378#30710378
      - zamoose · 2021-08-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2706691/article/38247865#38247865

    ### Is Hyper Metabolism misprinted?
    - id: `hyper-metabolism-misprint` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — early copies are wrong; it should make you gain 3 command tokens in the status phase instead of 2. Base sets printed after 2019 have the corrected card. It stacks with Sol's extra token, so Sol with it gains 4.
      - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
      - hooliganj · 2025-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/46915034#46915034
      - pkitty · 2017-11-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1882640/article/27424825#27424825
      - nikobel · 2018-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1926803/article/28062768#28062768
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2800761/article/39321856#39321856
      - Falcone1983 · 2017-11-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1877608/article/27351516#27351516

    ### How do technology specialties work when researching?
- id: `technology-specialty-rules` — grade: Per the rulebook — Technology
- A: Exhaust a ready planet with a specialty to ignore one prerequisite of that color; several planets can skip several prerequisites on one technology, and it works for unit upgrades too. Exhausting a planet for its specialty is separate from spending it, so you don't also get its resources.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-02-16 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - COMaestro · 2019-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2152151/article/31321182#31321182
  - AgentDrake · 2021-10-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2743181/article/38662734#38662734
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1918678/article/27951148#27951148
  - Truji · 2024-01-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3229669/article/43639715#43639715

      ### Does a technology I just researched, or one that is exhausted, count as a prerequisite?
      - id: `prerequisites-and-exhausted-tech` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes to both. Owned technologies always count, exhausted or not, so the first technology from the Technology primary counts for the second, and you can exhaust AI Development Algorithm and still count it. A coloured faction technology counts as that colour for prerequisites and objectives.
        - zamoose · 2023-04-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3071291/article/42152693#42152693
        - Clipper · 2018-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1946697/article/28372792#28372792
        - AgentDrake · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917811/article/27936411#27936411
        - Clipper · 2023-09-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3153874/article/42956239#42956239
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2378689/article/34163038#34163038
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2431418/article/34869558#34869558

    ### How do unit upgrades work?
- id: `unit-upgrades-are-technologies` — grade: Per the rulebook — Technology
- A: They are technologies you research normally, prerequisites and all; Inheritance Systems can skip their prerequisites like any other. Once gained, lay the card over that unit on your faction sheet; all your units of that type use the new stats right away. Upgrades have no colour, so they don't count as prerequisites or for colour objectives.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2025-08-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Clipper · 2020-03-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2388779/article/34290108#34290108
  - Weltenreiter · 2019-03-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2162318/article/31411483#31411483
  - Clavos · 2026-01-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3640018/article/47114705#47114705
  - Clipper · 2020-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2560924/article/36527757#36527757

    ### Do faction technologies replace generic ones?
- id: `faction-unit-upgrades-replace-generic` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only faction unit upgrades replace the matching generic upgrade; all other faction technologies are extras. Faction unit upgrades do count as faction technologies for objectives.
  - Clipper · 2021-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2597377/article/36947059#36947059
  - Dulkal · 2019-12-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2339047/article/33677221#33677221
  - csouth154 · 2019-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2221242/article/32237975#32237975
  - csouth154 · 2019-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2221242/article/32238089#32238089

    ### If an effect lets me research a technology, do I pay for it?
    - id: `research-cost-only-when-stated` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only if the effect says so, as the Technology card does. You still meet prerequisites (specialties can help). 'Gain' is different from 'research': gaining ignores prerequisites and doesn't trigger 'when you research' effects.
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1968198/article/28702761#28702761
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1968198/article/28702866#28702866
      - Weltenreiter · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892844/article/27567035#27567035
      - Bluedevil310 · 2026-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3639075/article/47106781#47106781
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2795414/article/39260194#39260194

    ### Can I use a technology right after researching it, such as Fleet Logistics?
    - id: `new-tech-usable-immediately` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes. Research Fleet Logistics with the Technology primary on your turn, and once that strategic action, secondaries included, is done you may take a second action that turn; gaining it on another player's turn doesn't give an extra action then. Starting technologies work from the first turn.
      - Clipper · 2020-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2491537/article/35670740#35670740
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2558960/article/36502241#36502241
      - hooliganj · 2019-02-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2154239/article/31297994#31297994
      - Xelvonar · 2017-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1908552/article/27809523#27809523
      - donmakaron · 2021-05-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2653572/article/37639045#37639045

    ### What does Fleet Logistics allow?
    - id: `fleet-logistics` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: On each turn you may take two actions instead of one, of any type, even the same type, such as two tactical actions on two systems. You can't reuse an exhausted strategy card or a discarded action card, and the second action is optional. If the first is Warfare, other players resolve its secondary before your second action.
      - Clipper · 2017-12-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899023/article/27670717#27670717
      - Painkeeper · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904903/article/27758089#27758089
      - Libero03 · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904903/article/27758128#27758128
      - iswearihaveajob · 2019-10-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2301931/article/33253611#33253611
      - csouth154 · 2019-03-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2161268/article/31396024#31396024
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-03-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2626165/article/37314174#37314174

### Does Gravity Drive give +1 move to every ship?
- id: `gravity-drive-one-ship` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — each tactical action you pick one ship, one plastic piece, and it gets +1 move for that action.
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838856/article/26769927#26769927
  - Robo Fish · 2017-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838856/article/26770349#26770349
  - Aarontu · 2024-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3237207/article/43701319#43701319
  - Stinky_Wizzleteets · 2024-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3237207/article/43725641#43725641

    ### Is Plasma Scoring's extra die optional, and does it work with Experimental Battlestation?
    - id: `plasma-scoring-optional` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It is a 'may', so you can decline it. It adds a die whenever your units use Space Cannon or Bombardment, including a space dock firing through Experimental Battlestation, which then rolls 4 dice.
      - Normative · 2019-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2144187/article/31193966#31193966
      - Yakslapper · 2019-02-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2144187/article/31282613#31282613

      - UnalignedMagi · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903534/article/27736507#27736507
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903534/article/27736753#27736753

    ### What does Antimass Deflectors do?
    - id: `antimass-deflectors` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Your ships can move into, end in and pass through asteroid fields, and other players' Space Cannon rolls get -1 against all your units, ground forces in Space Cannon Defense included, wherever they are.
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1844197/article/26846085#26846085
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1844197/article/26848732#26848732
      - Clipper · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902919/article/27729271#27729271
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902919/article/27729626#27729626
      - zamoose · 2018-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014994/article/29387580#29387580
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2014994/article/29387582#29387582

    ### Does Light/Wave Deflector let my ships ignore blockades?
    - id: `light-wave-deflector-limits` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — it only lets your ships move through systems containing other players' ships. It doesn't let you produce ships in a blockaded system.
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2290158/article/33114735#33114735
      - csouth154 · 2019-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2290158/article/33114580#33114580

    ### Are Fighter II units still fighters for other effects?
    - id: `advanced-fighters-still-fighters` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Fighter II only lets fighters move without capacity (counting against the fleet pool when unsupported); they remain fighters for every other effect.
      - Xelvonar · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893395/article/27576061#27576061
      - pfischer · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893395/article/27576455#27576455

    ### What happens to infantry on the Infantry II card if I don't control my home system?
    - id: `infantry-ii-on-the-card` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: They return at the start of your next turn, which after passing is in the next round, onto planets you control in your home system, split as you like. If you control none there, they are lost (returned to reinforcements), per a relayed developer email; they don't wait on the card. Any destruction triggers Infantry II.
      - Clipper · 2019-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273561/article/32908077#32908077
      - ajmarshall · 2020-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2536601/article/36224636#36224636
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954208/article/28503879#28503879
      - Clipper · 2018-05-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1998745/article/29157343#29157343
      - Clipper · 2018-07-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2029393/article/29588783#29588783
      - Clipper · 2018-03-08 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954208/article/28492320#28492320

    ### Does a war sun shut off Magen Defense Grid?
    - id: `magen-defense-grid-versions-war-sun` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only the original version, which needs a unit with Planetary Shield: a war sun makes other players' units lose Planetary Shield. Effects that merely ignore Planetary Shield leave it, so the original still works then. The Codex Omega version only needs a structure and isn't affected by war suns. It only acts in the first round of ground combat.
      - iswearihaveajob · 2020-12-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2566568/article/36593979#36593979
      - Somnambulix · 2020-12-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2566568/article/36593986#36593986
      - pkitty · 2021-08-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2703417/article/38212202#38212202
      - TinTeardrop · 2021-08-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2703417/article/38216983#38216983
      - gpoirier · 2025-12-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3633553/article/47068298#47068298
      - halbower · 2019-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2312163/article/33371550#33371550

    ### How does X-89 Bacterial Weapon Omega work?
    - id: `x-89-omega-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It needs your Bombardment to destroy at least one infantry; if hits go to mechs and no infantry dies, nothing happens, and Planetary Shield, by blocking Bombardment, blocks it too. The original X-89 targets any enemy planet, even one with no infantry.
      - DauntlessK · 2021-04-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2644520/article/37534061#37534061
      - THLY · 2021-04-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2644520/article/37540553#37540553

      - Clipper · 2019-11-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2314452/article/33397386#33397386
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-11-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2314452/article/33402682#33402682

    ### What can Integrated Economy produce?
    - id: `integrated-economy` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Any units, ships or ground forces, up to the new planet's resource value in cost, paid normally; ships appear in that system, unless enemy ships are there. Each planet gained triggers separately. It isn't a Production ability, so Sarween Tools and the original Yin Spinner don't apply.
      - hooliganj · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917840/article/27936825#27936825
      - eumaies · 2018-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917840/article/28334154#28334154
      - Clipper · 2018-03-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917840/article/28441594#28441594
      - hooliganj · 2024-03-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917840/article/43971414#43971414
      - Painkeeper · 2019-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2302502/article/33260294#33260294
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1889336/article/27518169#27518169

  ### What can L1Z1X's Inheritance Systems be used for?
  - id: `inheritance-systems` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: It ignores all prerequisites for one research, unit upgrades and your own faction technologies like Super Dreadnought included, but can't get you another faction's technology. Researched as the first tech of the Technology primary, it can pay for the second. It can be used in the agenda phase too, though it then stays exhausted, since only planets ready at the end of that phase.
    - Marcial · 2020-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2456392/article/35215226#35215226

    - hooliganj · 2019-11-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2318454/article/33444811#33444811
    - Clipper · 2020-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2344964/article/33739596#33739596

    ### Can Xxcha's Instinct Training cancel any action card, even outside their own combats?
    - id: `instinct-training` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it cancels any other player's action card, Sabotage included, since it refers to 'another player', not an opponent. Unlike Nullification Field, it doesn't require you to be the target.
      - Marcial · 2023-10-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3171295/article/43114048#43114048

      - Clipper · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272708/article/32898298#32898298

    ### Is Creuss's Wormhole Generator mandatory, and where can its tokens go?
    - id: `creuss-wormhole-generator` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: A player relays a developer email saying it is mandatory each status phase, since it lacks 'may'. Only the alpha and beta Creuss tokens exist, so once both are out you move one. A system with a planet you control qualifies even if it's your home system; the non-home limit applies only to the option of a system without other players' ships.
      - kaiteinomen · 2018-03-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1966405/article/28677435#28677435

    ### Does the Creuss flagship's delta wormhole trigger Dimensional Splicer?
    - id: `dimensional-splicer-flagship-wormhole` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it is a wormhole, and the Creuss player then picks which enemy ship takes the hit, which can set up Direct Hit.
      - DarthBatman47 · 2018-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939408/article/28255642#28255642
