      - Clipper · 2020-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2465655/article/35340457#35340457

    ### How does Jol-Nar's Research Agreement work?
    - id: `jol-nar-research-agreement` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: When Jol-Nar researches a non-faction technology, by any means, while you hold the note, both of you end up with it: you gain the same technology. It doesn't trigger when Jol-Nar merely gains a tech, and it can't give you a unit upgrade for a unit your faction doesn't have. The Nekro get the technology, not tokens.
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1849901/article/26934992#26934992
      - Clipper · 2018-04-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1849901/article/28918364#28918364
      - csouth154 · 2019-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2205382/article/32016474#32016474
      - Weltenreiter · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892844/article/27567035#27567035
      - Clipper · 2018-06-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2011206/article/29333287#29333287
      - Clipper · 2019-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2145083/article/31170988#31170988

    ### How does Jol-Nar's Spatial Conduit Cylinder work?
    - id: `jol-nar-spatial-conduit` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It is exhausted right after activation, before movement, so the system must already contain your units. Then systems with your units count as adjacent to it for that action, so all your PDS II there can fire into it.
      - adrim · 2019-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2174550/article/31581912#31581912
      - Clipper · 2019-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2174550/article/31583152#31583152
      - Clipper · 2018-10-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2079870/article/30279817#30279817

    ### Can Bioplasmosis or Transit Diodes move ground forces onto enemy planets, or chain across systems?
- id: `bioplasmosis-and-transit-diodes` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No. Both only move ground forces to planets you control. Bioplasmosis moves each infantry once, to a planet in its own or an adjacent system, so no hopping across several systems.
  - baza26b · 2018-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2016462/article/29409295#29409295
  - Clipper · 2018-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1931176/article/28128767#28128767
  - Cacotopos · 2023-10-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3162863/article/43039209#43039209
  - hooliganj · 2023-10-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3162863/article/43039722#43039722
  - Weltenreiter · 2017-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1835065/article/26713988#26713988

    ### Can Winnu use Lazax Gate Folding's action without Mecatol Rex, and does the wormhole part work while exhausted?
    - id: `lazax-gate-folding-winnu` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The action needs you to control Mecatol Rex, so it can't be used as a stall. The passive part, Mecatol Rex counting as alpha and beta wormholes while you don't control it, doesn't need the card ready, so it works while exhausted.
      - Falcone1983 · 2018-04-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1980312/article/28898871#28898871
      - Falcone1983 · 2018-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1980312/article/29261586#29261586
      - Clipper · 2018-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1980312/article/28890307#28890307
      - PistonLoop · 2021-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722969/article/38429640#38429640
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272708/article/32896673#32896673
      - Falcone1983 · 2018-04-22 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1980312/article/28890830#28890830

### Can a technology ability that doesn't say 'exhaust' be used more than once per round?
- id: `tech-abilities-without-exhaust` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it applies every time its trigger occurs, and static abilities keep working even while the card is exhausted.
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2378689/article/34163038#34163038
  - LithuanianFury · 2020-03-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2378689/article/34192924#34192924
  - Clipper · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587423/article/36827035#36827035

### When does Magmus Reactor give a trade good for producing?
- id: `magmus-reactor-trade-good` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: When you produce in a system that contains a war sun (anyone's) or is adjacent to a supernova; at most one per production. A war sun next door, producing inside a supernova (a system isn't adjacent to itself), or a war sun built in that same production doesn't count, and Star Forge places rather than produces.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705060/article/38233675#38233675
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705060/article/38244253#38244253
  - AgentDrake · 2018-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2041573/article/29753484#29753484
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-07 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705060/article/38231568#38231568

### Does Prototype War Sun II cost less?
- id: `muaat-prototype-war-sun-ii-cost` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it costs 10 instead of 12.
  - Painkeeper · 2018-07-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2031543/article/29618767#29618767
  - Benj_ · 2018-07-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2031543/article/29618904#29618904


## Control, Structures and Mecatol Rex

      ### Does a home system stop being a home system when another player takes it?
      - id: `home-system-stays-home` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: No — it stays its original owner's home system, so it remains off-limits to effects that target non-home systems or planets, and its owner still needs it back to score public objectives.
        - alercah · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888170/article/27497789#27497789
        - hooliganj · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888170/article/27497950#27497950
        - GrumblingGamer · 2019-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2130277/article/30960781#30960781
        - Clipper · 2019-01-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2130277/article/30960819#30960819

    ### Do I lose control of a planet if my ground forces leave or are destroyed?
- id: `keeping-control-of-planets` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — you control a planet while you hold its card; with no units there, mark it with a control token. You only lose control when another player gains it, by invading or an effect that says so. That includes home planets you start with, and control never reverts to a former owner automatically.
  - gpoirier · 2023-07-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3120466/article/42644778#42644778
  - Ravendas · 2023-07-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3120466/article/42644958#42644958
  - Clipper · 2022-01-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2803734/article/39358197#39358197
  - Marcial · 2019-03-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2165628/article/31458799#31458799
  - chrisdk · 2024-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3429301/article/45390064#45390064
  - Bigland · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886681/article/27476710#27476710

    ### Do I control both planets in my home system at the start, and does that trigger 'gain control' effects?
    - id: `home-planets-controlled-at-start` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: You control every home planet from the start, empty or not; a control token on an empty one is just a reminder. Because you begin with control rather than gaining it, abilities like Saar's Scavenge don't trigger at setup.
      - Zumbar · 2024-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3389193/article/45039591#45039591
      - AgentDrake · 2024-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3389193/article/45039942#45039942
      - halbower · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903135/article/27731632#27731632
      - Clipper · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903135/article/27731820#27731820
      - Padish · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903135/article/27732736#27732736

    ### How do I take control of a planet?
- id: `taking-control-needs-ground-forces` — grade: Per the rulebook — Tactical Action
- A: Normally by landing at least one ground force on it in the invasion step; ships in the system, or fighters, even with the Naalu flagship, never take a planet. Each planet is taken separately, so one invasion can't claim a second planet you didn't land on. A few effects grant control directly, and say so, like Xxcha's Peace Accords.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2023-05-02 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - isterbat · 2023-07-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3116053/article/42602533#42602533
  - IncrediSteve · 2025-07-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3536344/article/46285695#46285695
  - Nyarlathotep90 · 2018-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1924242/article/28022338#28022338
  - IncrediSteve · 2017-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1898007/article/27654893#27654893

    ### When invading a system with two planets, does winning on one take both, and what if both sides are wiped out on a planet?
- id: `two-planets-separate-combats` — grade: Per the rulebook — Invasion and Ground Combat
- A: No — each planet has its own ground combat, and you take only the planets where your ground forces remain and the defender's don't. If both sides lose all their ground forces on a planet, the defender keeps control.
  - hooliganj · 2019-01-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2141150/article/31116275#31116275
  - IncrediSteve · 2019-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2141150/article/31116956#31116956

    ### Can different players control different planets in the same system?
    - id: `different-players-planets-same-system` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each planet is controlled separately and nobody controls the space area. Only ships can't share the space area without combat. If a planet's controller has no units on it while someone else's do, control stays put until that player invades.
      - Clipper · 2017-12-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1898948/article/27669858#27669858
      - afrag · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903115/article/27731387#27731387
      - Clipper · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904380/article/27748632#27748632
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904380/article/27748645#27748645
      - hooliganj · 2025-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3614463/article/46913992#46913992

    ### What happens to space docks and PDS when an enemy takes the planet?
    - id: `structures-destroyed-when-planet-taken` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: They are destroyed when another player's ground forces take control of the planet with none of the owner's ground forces left. Structures never fight or block an invasion. Fighters fighting via the Naalu flagship don't destroy them, since they aren't on the planet afterwards. L1Z1X's Assimilate replaces them instead, and the new dock can produce that same action.
      - Old_Man_gi · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563244/article/36563382#36563382
      - Clipper · 2017-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890123/article/27604838#27604838
      - Crikrunner · 2017-11-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890123/article/27528819#27528819
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-09-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2059057/article/30013039#30013039
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2059057/article/29988045#29988045
      - Clipper · 2018-09-14 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2059057/article/29995391#29995391

    ### Can PDS or space docks be moved or transported?
- id: `structures-cannot-move` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — structures stay where they are placed, except the Saar Floating Factories, which move as if they were ships.
  - Clipper · 2020-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2353341/article/33845366#33845366
  - halbower · 2020-02-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2370034/article/34055047#34055047
  - pfischer · 2018-01-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1919529/article/27961281#27961281
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-08-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1830950/article/26654659#26654659

    ### How do I take Mecatol Rex, and does the custodians point ever change hands?
    - id: `custodians-token-mecatol` — grade: Per the rulebook — Control, Structures, and the Custodians Token
    - A: Activate it like any system; during invasion, before committing ground forces, spend 6 influence to remove the custodians token, gaining it and 1 victory point, then land. Only the first player to do this gets the point, and it is never lost; later control of Mecatol Rex scores only through Imperial.
      - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2022-05-19 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
      - hooliganj · 2017-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892423/article/27560018#27560018
      - salsaVerdeguel · 2025-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3634750/article/47076024#47076024
      - AgentDrake · 2022-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2796791/article/39276412#39276412
      - pfischer · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903832/article/27740225#27740225

    ### If I kill every ground force on a planet with X-89 or Orbital Drop elsewhere, do I take the planet?
- id: `destroying-ground-forces-not-control` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — destroying defenders never changes control, and Orbital Drop only reinforces planets you already control. You must invade in a tactical action to take it.
  - Crikrunner · 2018-05-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000295/article/29177320#29177320
  - oldfan · 2018-05-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000295/article/29196997#29196997
  - zamoose · 2020-09-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2498255/article/35753592#35753592
  - Phoenix1147 · 2020-09-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2498255/article/35754087#35754087

### Can an enemy use a Saar Floating Factory to score objectives about ships in systems with enemy space docks?
- id: `saar-dock-not-blockadable-objective` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — a Floating Factory left alone with enemy ships is destroyed at once, so the condition never holds against the Saar.
  - alercah · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886885/article/27478868#27478868
  - Sande24 · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886885/article/27498548#27498548
  - pedro2112 · 2021-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2529070/article/36898900#36898900


## Action Cards

  ### Can I play two copies of the same action card, like Morale Boost or Flank Speed, in one timing window?
  - id: `same-name-action-cards-one-window` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — you cannot play more than one action card with the same name in a single timing window to affect the same units or effect. Cards with different names can all be played. Direct Hit can be played more than once because each copy targets a different ship.
    - IncrediSteve · 2021-06-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2683375/article/37987722#37987722
    - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2683375/article/38231977#38231977
    - AgentDrake · 2021-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2571574/article/36641882#36641882
    - sigmazero13 · 2020-08-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2478401/article/35499877#35499877
    - Clipper · 2022-06-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2879775/article/40225752#40225752

      ### If my action card is cancelled by Sabotage, can I play another copy of the same card in that window?
      - id: `replay-card-after-sabotage` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Yes — a cancelled card is treated as never having been played, so the same-name restriction doesn't stop a second copy. Likewise, if your Sabotage is itself cancelled (by Instinct Training, say), you may play another Sabotage on the original card. Some older answers said a second copy was not allowed.
        - IncrediSteve · 2022-06-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2879775/article/40222459#40222459
        - Clipper · 2022-06-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2879775/article/40225752#40225752
        - chrisdk · 2023-04-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3058294/article/42023637#42023637
        - jeffwagner · 2018-10-06 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2071239/article/30162076#30162076

### Can a player who isn't in the combat play Sabotage or Direct Hit?
- id: `third-party-sabotage-and-direct-hit` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Sabotage, yes: any player can cancel another player's action card (other than a Sabotage). Direct Hit, no: the hit must have been produced by your own units or abilities, so a bystander has nothing to play it on.
  - hooliganj · 2018-03-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1961720/article/28607320#28607320
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-06-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2003352/article/29223026#29223026
  - PorkStewJoe · 2018-06-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2003352/article/29222995#29222995

  ### Do Direct Hit and Shields Holding work on hits from Dimensional Splicer or Yin Devotion?
  - id: `direct-hit-and-shields-holding-vs-ability-hits` — grade: Designer ruling
  - A: Yes to both. Direct Hit was errata'd to cover hits produced by your units or abilities, and Shields Holding can cancel these hits too. An early FAQ entry saying otherwise was acknowledged as a mistake by the developer, who confirmed the Splicer is meant to be that powerful.
  - CreussEmissary · 2018-05-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29155108#29155108
  - Clipper · 2018-05-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29146908#29146908
  - Clipper · 2018-05-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29147251#29147251
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-05-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29153251#29153251
  - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
  - shakesignal · 2018-05-21 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1996325/article/29121505#29121505

    ### If I have 7 action cards and draw 2, do I discard as I go or after drawing both?
- id: `action-card-hand-limit-draw-two` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Draw both, then discard down to seven, choosing from your whole hand.
  - Clipper · 2018-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1997278/article/29132560#29132560
  - TheWhistler1967 · 2018-05-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1997278/article/29132567#29132567

    ### Can Tactical Bombardment be played in a system with no enemy planets?
    - id: `tactical-bombardment-no-enemy-planets` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it only requires that you have a unit with Bombardment in that system.
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2632097/article/37388088#37388088
      - Clipper · 2021-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2632097/article/37388647#37388647

    ### Does Cripple Defenses destroy every PDS on the planet?
    - id: `cripple-defenses-all-pds` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
    - A: Yes — it destroys each PDS on the chosen planet, which is all of them.
      - Morix_Jak · 2019-06-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2229736/article/32351234#32351234

    ### If my component action card is cancelled by Sabotage, have I used my action for the turn?
    - id: `sabotaged-action-card-turn-not-used` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — a cancelled card counts as not played, so you still take an action (or pass, if allowed). Anything that happened before you played it stands: if Flank Speed or Upgrade is Sabotaged after you activated a system, the system stays activated and you carry on without the bonus. If it was the optional extra action from Fleet Logistics, you can just end your turn.
      - Clipper · 2018-01-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1924431/article/28024597#28024597
      - ronaldsf · 2018-03-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954662/article/28507154#28507154
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2170050/article/31518946#31518946
      - Clipper · 2017-12-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894746/article/27598116#27598116
      - Quadrature_Strategy · 2022-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801538/article/39331111#39331111
      - GAFBlizzard · 2026-03-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3674506/article/47397944#47397944

    ### When exactly is Sabotage played: before or after the card's target is chosen?
    - id: `sabotage-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: After the player announces the card's choices (target planet, ship or player, rider prediction, retreat destination and so on), but before anything changes the game state, such as rolling dice or spending trade goods. Allow a short pause for it. Focused Research is the exception: its technology is chosen during resolution, after the Sabotage window.
      - MATTPROV3 · 2018-08-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2050608/article/29880548#29880548
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2050608/article/31060599#31060599
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2050608/article/29887052#29887052
      - chrisdk · 2023-02-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3027449/article/41712904#41712904
      - hooliganj · 2023-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3027449/article/41718898#41718898
      - hooliganj · 2018-08-28 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2050608/article/29878485#29878485

    ### Can Sabotage cancel another Sabotage or a promissory note?
    - id: `sabotage-what-it-cancels` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both. Sabotage only cancels action cards, and never one named Sabotage. Other cancel effects that lack that restriction can still cancel a Sabotage.
      - Clipper · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954002/article/28488150#28488150
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1954002/article/28489731#28489731
      - halbower · 2017-12-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894849/article/27599250#27599250
      - Clipper · 2017-12-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1894849/article/27599388#27599388

    ### Can I play action cards when it isn't my turn?
    - id: `playing-action-cards-off-turn` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes, whenever a card's timing text allows it — Sabotage can even go into a combat you aren't part of. Only cards whose timing is ACTION must be played as your action on your turn.
      - Clipper · 2019-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2148912/article/31223742#31223742
      - AgentDrake · 2019-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2148912/article/31225009#31225009

  ### When can the Upgrade action card be played?
  - id: `upgrade-card-timing` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Immediately after you activate a system containing your ships — not later in that tactical action, such as after producing a new cruiser.
    - hooliganj · 2018-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2043101/article/29775232#29775232
    - Weltenreiter · 2018-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2043101/article/29775266#29775266

  ### Does Unstable Planet destroy fewer than 3 infantry if there are only 1 or 2?
  - id: `unstable-planet-up-to-three` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — it was errata'd to destroy up to 3 infantry, so it removes whatever is there, up to 3. It can also target an exhausted hazardous planet, since its effects are joined by 'and'.
    - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
    - Sidious1027 · 2019-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/30991281#30991281
    - Esau of Isaac · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27928085#27928085
    - sigmazero13 · 2020-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2280071/article/34312411#34312411

  ### Can several Direct Hits or Shields Holdings be played in the same combat round?
  - id: `multiple-direct-hits-and-shields-holding` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Several Direct Hits, yes: each Sustain Damage use is its own trigger, so you can answer each with a Direct Hit on that ship. Sustain Damage is used unit by unit, so you can wait to see a Direct Hit before sustaining with more; hits already cancelled stay cancelled. Shields Holding: one per player against a set of incoming hits, though each combatant can play one.
    - mjdomask · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901762/article/27763984#27763984
    - Clipper · 2017-12-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901762/article/27713055#27713055
    - zamoose · 2023-03-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3052394/article/41965185#41965185
    - hooliganj · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1908026/article/27803404#27803404
    - Clipper · 2019-04-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2186846/article/31752859#31752859
    - Odinatos · 2017-12-14 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901762/article/27712474#27712474

    ### Does Shields Holding last for the whole combat?
    - id: `shields-holding-one-use` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — like other action cards it resolves once, against one round's hits, and is discarded.
      - Clipper · 2023-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3152212/article/42938807#42938807
      - pkitty · 2023-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3152212/article/42944152#42944152

    ### What happens to committed ground forces when Parley is played?
    - id: `parley-ground-forces-return` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: They return to the space area rather than being destroyed, within capacity. The invasion of that planet just doesn't happen.
      - Dalek5 · 2017-08-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1840229/article/26792750#26792750
      - CleanAndClear · 2017-08-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1840229/article/26792752#26792752

  ### Can I play Skilled Retreat if I have no command tokens in reinforcements?
  - id: `skilled-retreat-without-tokens` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
  - A: Yes — as with a normal retreat, you take a token from your command sheet instead, according to a relayed FFG answer. Skilled Retreat may also move into a system that already contains your command token.
    - JamesG · 2018-01-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911801/article/28004994#28004994
    - trud · 2018-01-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911801/article/27847564#27847564
    - possumman · 2018-01-02 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1911801/article/27848959#27848959

  ### When can Parley be played, and does it stop Bombardment?
  - id: `parley-timing` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Right after ground forces are committed to a planet, not later in the combat. Bombardment happens before that, so Parley can't stop it. The committed forces go back to space and that planet's invasion is off; to try again you need the system free of your command token.
    - Clipper · 2017-12-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901804/article/27713283#27713283
    - ksfga · 2018-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947582/article/28383649#28383649
    - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947582/article/28383653#28383653
    - Crikrunner · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892959/article/27568998#27568998
    - Dulkal · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892959/article/27569020#27569020

      ### Which hits can Shields Holding cancel?
      - id: `shields-holding-which-hits` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Hits during space combat, including Anti-Fighter Barrage in the first round and ability hits like Dimensional Splicer, Ambush or Devotion. Not Space Cannon Offense or Defense, which happen outside space combat, and not 'destroy' effects such as Courageous to the End. Each combatant may play one in the same round.
        - Clipper · 2019-02-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2152383/article/31274863#31274863
        - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2377204/article/34143337#34143337
        - Clipper · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719738/article/38394945#38394945
        - Clipper · 2018-01-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1920144/article/27967997#27967997
        - Amuk · 2018-03-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1959974/article/28581782#28581782

    ### Can I play Direct Hit, Maneuvering Jets or other combat cards in a combat I'm not part of?
    - id: `combat-cards-only-own-units` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: In practice no — Direct Hit needs a hit produced by your own units or abilities, Maneuvering Jets only cancels hits on your own units, and the other combat cards refer to you, your units or your opponent. Bystanders can still play Sabotage, and a few cards like Scramble Frequency.
      - donmakaron · 2020-10-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528919/article/36127710#36127710
      - hooliganj · 2020-10-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2528919/article/36128830#36128830
      - MollyMae · 2019-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2135695/article/31036369#31036369
      - Clipper · 2020-03-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2382923/article/34216296#34216296
      - Clipper · 2021-08-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2717591/article/38368350#38368350
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890734/article/27535458#27535458

  ### Can Emergency Repairs save a ship from Direct Hit?
  - id: `emergency-repairs-vs-direct-hit` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — Direct Hit is played as Sustain Damage is used, while hits are being assigned; Emergency Repairs comes at the end of the round, too late.
    - sigmazero13 · 2019-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2190263/article/31800098#31800098
    - csouth154 · 2019-04-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2190263/article/31803256#31803256

  ### Does Scramble Frequency reroll all the dice of a unit ability?
  - id: `scramble-frequency-all-dice` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — all of that ability's dice are rolled together, so the card rerolls all of them, across different values or bombarded planets.
    - Marcial · 2021-04-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2650750/article/37608030#37608030
    - Clipper · 2021-04-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2650750/article/37608948#37608948

    ### Does Signal Jamming take command tokens from more than one player?
    - id: `signal-jamming-one-player` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — it names a single other player, whose reinforcements supply the one token you place; that player must have one there for the card to be playable.
      - Platfus · 2022-09-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2938203/article/40832808#40832808
      - selcos21 · 2022-09-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2938203/article/40832816#40832816
      - Clipper · 2017-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1901183/article/27702312#27702312

    ### Does Maneuvering Jets protect infantry from Space Cannon Defense?
    - id: `maneuvering-jets-ground-forces` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it cancels Space Cannon hits on your ground forces as well as your ships, but only hits on your own units.
      - mordai · 2019-02-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2152471/article/31275284#31275284
      - MollyMae · 2019-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2135695/article/31036369#31036369


## Promissory Notes

    ### Do I have to play a promissory note, such as Ceasefire, as soon as its timing comes up?
    - id: `promissory-notes-optional-to-play` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — promissory notes are never mandatory unless the note says to play it immediately or place it face up. You can hold it and decide each time its timing window arises. This means the holder may choose not to play it; it does not let the note's owner refuse to honor it once played.
      - csouth154 · 2019-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2194667/article/31860525#31860525
      - halbower · 2026-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3666254/article/47330053#47330053
      - AgentDrake · 2026-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3666254/article/47330336#47330336
      - Clipper · 2020-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2566806/article/36595862#36595862
      - Falcone1983 · 2017-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892692/article/27564935#27564935
      - hooliganj · 2017-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1837428/article/26751973#26751973

    ### Does the Letnev War Funding promissory note still work if the Letnev player has fewer than 2 trade goods?
    - id: `letnev-war-funding-without-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the reroll still applies. The trade-good loss and the reroll are printed as separate paragraphs, so they are separate abilities, and the reroll does not depend on the Letnev being able to pay.
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27929184#27929184
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27929261#27929261
      - Clipper · 2018-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1974740/article/28802950#28802950
      - IndyBart · 2018-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1974740/article/28803784#28803784
      - IndyBart · 2017-09-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1854869/article/27017998#27017998

  ### Can L1Z1X Cybernetic Enhancements or Xxcha Political Favor be used if their owner has no command token in the strategy pool?
  - id: `l1z1x-and-xxcha-notes-need-a-token` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — each of these notes is a single paragraph where the benefit follows from removing the owner's strategy token, so if there is no token to remove, nothing on it resolves. The separate 'return this card' paragraph is judged on its own.
    - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1854869/article/27373126#27373126
    - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1917256/article/27928719#27928719
    - Clipper · 2018-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1974740/article/28802950#28802950
    - IncrediSteve · 2019-02-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2152389/article/31274929#31274929
    - IncrediSteve · 2019-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272253/article/32891173#32891173
    - RedLuigi · 2017-09-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1854869/article/27016159#27016159

    ### Does Sol's Military Support work if Sol has no strategy token, and can its 2 infantry go on different planets?
    - id: `sol-military-support-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It works without a token — the note only spends Sol's strategy token if able. But both infantry go on a single planet you control; the card says one planet, in English and in translations.
      - Pete Walsh · 2021-07-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2698985/article/38158589#38158589
      - Clipper · 2018-04-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1974740/article/28802950#28802950
      - Clipper · 2021-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2700852/article/38180611#38180611
      - Marcial · 2021-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2700852/article/38182934#38182934

  ### When must I return another player's Support for the Throne?
  - id: `support-for-the-throne-return` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: As soon as you activate a system containing any of that player's units, whether or not you move in or attack. Units include their ships, ground forces, space docks and PDS, but not control tokens, so activating a system where they only have control tokens is fine. Putting a command token in a system by other means is not activating it.
    - Clipper · 2018-08-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2035470/article/29670852#29670852
    - Clipper · 2018-08-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2035470/article/29670962#29670962
    - TomBoombs · 2018-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1960509/article/28588121#28588121
    - Clipper · 2018-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1960509/article/28587941#28587941

    ### If I hold Mentak's Promise of Protection without playing it, must I return it when I activate a Mentak system?
    - id: `unplayed-note-ignores-return-condition` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — a note you have not played face up ignores its own return conditions. But it also doesn't protect you from Pillage until it is face up in your play area.
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2052541/article/29906226#29906226
      - Clipper · 2018-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2052541/article/29906241#29906241

    ### Can the Creuss IFF note put a wormhole token in a system with enemy ships if I control a planet there?
    - id: `creuss-iff-placement` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the two placement options are alternatives: a system containing a planet you control, or a non-home system with no other players' ships. Meeting either is enough, so enemy ships or a blockade don't matter if you control a planet there.
      - Clipper · 2019-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237328/article/32452354#32452354
      - zamoose · 2019-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2274503/article/32921474#32921474
      - csouth154 · 2019-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2274503/article/32921563#32921563

          ### Can I receive Creuss IFF in a transaction at the start of my turn and play it in that same window?
          - id: `creuss-iff-start-of-turn-transaction` — grade: Community consensus
          - A: Yes — it is still the start of your turn until you actually take an action, so you can trade for the note and then resolve its start-of-turn ability.
            - Darkmancer · 2017-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1858530/article/27075554#27075554
            - Clipper · 2018-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1925344/article/28038061#28038061
            - DaNewHaida · 2018-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1925344/article/28038232#28038232

### Who can play the Yin Greyfire Mutagen note, and in which systems?
- id: `greyfire-mutagen-who-and-where` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Any player holding it can play it whenever a system is activated for a tactical action, regardless of which player is the active player.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702835/article/38200922#38200922
  - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702835/article/38201339#38201339

### Does the Arborec Stymie note stop the Arborec from producing in their home system?
- id: `arborec-stymie-home-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — Stymie only covers non-home systems containing the holder's units and the systems adjacent to them, so the Arborec home system is never blocked.
  - IndyBart · 2019-07-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2234239/article/32408738#32408738
  - Morix_Jak · 2019-07-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2234239/article/32408706#32408706

### When must Ceasefire be played?
- id: `ceasefire-timing` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Right after the system is activated, before any ships move; once ships have moved it's too late. The active player doesn't have to announce what will move first, and their own after-activation cards like Flank Speed get the first chance in that window.
  - Geoff Watson · 2019-07-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2230179/article/32355742#32355742
  - Morix_Jak · 2019-07-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2230179/article/32355763#32355763
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2518309/article/36000272#36000272

  ### Can Letnev's War Funding or The Crown of Thalnos reroll Anti-Fighter Barrage dice?
  - id: `war-funding-vs-anti-fighter-barrage` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
  - A: No — effects that modify or reroll combat rolls don't apply to Anti-Fighter Barrage, and players report the designer said War Funding wasn't meant to work with it. The card's 'any rolls this round' wording is read loosely by some.
    - ajmarshall · 2021-04-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37514642#37514642
    - Kazadoom · 2021-04-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37507721#37507721  
    - Kazadoom · 2021-04-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37513990#37513990
    - ajmarshall · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37497088#37497088
    - Kazadoom · 2021-04-17 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37514669#37514669


## Base Factions: Arborec to Letnev

### Who assigns the L1Z1X flagship's hits?
- id: `l1z1x-flagship-hits` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: The opponent, as with any hits unless an ability says otherwise; if only fighters are left, they take the hits.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2704696/article/38227037#38227037

    ### Can Creuss use a wormhole token to get into an asteroid field protected by Saar's Chaos Mapping?
  - id: `creuss-wormhole-vs-chaos-mapping` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — Chaos Mapping says other players can't activate it, and 'cannot' is absolute; Creuss can place the token there but still can't activate the system to move in.
    - halbower · 2020-03-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2392149/article/34337158#34337158
    - Marcial · 2020-03-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2392149/article/34337168#34337168

    ### When does the Arborec flagship produce, and can it move in first?
    - id: `arborec-flagship-production-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It produces right after its system is activated, before movement, so it only works if it is already in the active system; it can't move in and then produce. Its units go on planets you control or in the space area, never on enemy planets.
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-05-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000649/article/29181680#29181680
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-05-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000649/article/29181771#29181771
      - TomBoombs · 2018-05-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000649/article/29184077#29184077
      - halbower · 2017-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1843133/article/26829640#26829640

  ### Are the Ghosts of Creuss neighbors with a player on a beta wormhole when Creuss sits on an alpha wormhole?
  - id: `creuss-quantum-entanglement-neighbors` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — Quantum Entanglement makes alpha and beta wormholes adjacent for Creuss, and adjacency created that way also makes them neighbors, so the two players can transact.
    - Melkor101 · 2020-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2483759/article/35569313#35569313
    - Cacotopos · 2020-08-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2483759/article/35569010#35569010

### How many Creuss wormhole tokens are there, and is there a delta wormhole token?
- id: `creuss-wormhole-token-limit` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The tokens are limited, and abilities say to place or move them rather than adding more. A full copy with Prophecy of Kings has three Creuss tokens (alpha, beta and gamma). Their faction-symbol backs are not delta wormholes. The flagship's delta wormhole needs no token; it travels with the ship.
  - IncrediSteve · 2023-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3049660/article/41935102#41935102
  - Arcadious · 2023-03-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3049660/article/41935114#41935114

    ### When Jol-Nar uses Brilliant to resolve the Technology primary as a secondary, do they need another token for the second tech?
    - id: `jol-nar-brilliant-second-tech` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — they pay one strategy token, then resolve the full primary: research one technology and optionally spend 6 resources for a second.
      - AgentDrake · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893452/article/27577044#27577044
      - void_presence · 2017-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1893452/article/27577129#27577129

    ### How does the L1Z1X Harrow ability work?
    - id: `l1z1x-harrow-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Per errata it triggers at the end of each round of ground combat, as Bombardment from L1Z1X ships in the active system. Every ship with Bombardment there can join in, not just those that bombarded that planet. It still resolves after the round in which L1Z1X lose their last ground force; if it then kills the last defender, the combat is a draw. Planetary Shield stops it.
      - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939769/article/28291775#28291775
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939769/article/28315225#28315225
      - Clipper · 2018-02-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939769/article/28324906#28324906
      - Clipper · 2021-08-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2704135/article/38219895#38219895
      - Kesh · 2018-02-14 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1939769/article/28262154#28262154

  ### How many hits can the Jol-Nar flagship produce?
  - id: `jol-nar-flagship-hits` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Each die showing 9 or 10 produces its normal hit plus 2 more, so its two dice can make up to 6 hits.
    - Clipper · 2021-08-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2703624/article/38211963#38211963
    - Marcial · 2019-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2272552/article/32894706#32894706

    ### How does the Hacan flagship's trade-good bonus work?
    - id: `hacan-flagship-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Roll all your combat dice first, then spend 1 trade good for +1 on any of your units' dice in its system, at most once per die. It only modifies combat rolls, not Space Cannon or other ability rolls.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707295/article/38255173#38255173
      - IncrediSteve · 2025-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3631010/article/47049179#47049179
      - Clipper · 2020-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2465656/article/35340212#35340212
      - IncrediSteve · 2018-09-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2053158/article/29912063#29912063

### Can Letnev use Munitions Reserves every round, and does it cover Anti-Fighter Barrage?
- id: `letnev-munitions-reserves` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Every round, paying 2 trade goods each time, to reroll any of their dice that round. Whether it covers Anti-Fighter Barrage is debated; the better-supported view is yes, since barrage is part of the first combat round.
  - chrisdk · 2023-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3003645/article/41472251#41472251
  - IncrediSteve · 2023-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3003645/article/41472761#41472761
  - hooliganj · 2023-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3003645/article/41472637#41472637
  - zamoose · 2023-01-08 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3003645/article/41472567#41472567

    ### With Jol-Nar's Fragile, can their fighters still hit?
- id: `jol-nar-fragile-fighters` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes, on a 10: the -1 makes it 9, which still meets the fighter's combat value of 9. Fragile only affects combat rolls, not Space Cannon, Bombardment or Anti-Fighter Barrage.
  - Amuk · 2018-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2047023/article/29827797#29827797
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903825/article/27740354#27740354
  - Morix_Jak · 2019-08-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2257743/article/32720393#32720393

    ### How does Non-Euclidean Shielding work?
    - id: `non-euclidean-shielding` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Every time one of your units uses Sustain Damage, it cancels 2 hits instead of 1, and that unit can still be assigned one of the remaining hits. Unused cancellation is lost; it doesn't carry over to a later hit such as Devotion's.
      - Clipper · 2019-12-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2338591/article/33673124#33673124
      - csouth154 · 2019-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2274595/article/32923069#32923069
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2045797/article/29810560#29810560
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2045797/article/29811627#29811627

    ### How does Arborec infantry Production work?
    - id: `arborec-letani-production` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Each Letani Warrior I has Production 1 (II has 2) and can build any unit you can pay for, ships included, with no space dock; they still pay resources. Their Production pools with your other units in the system, and they can produce in a system they just moved into, or from aboard ships. Arborec space docks can never produce infantry, so infantry output is capped by the Letani Warriors' own Production. Blockaded Letani can only make ground forces.
      - Jimb0v · 2017-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838752/article/26943671#26943671
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1838752/article/26946751#26946751
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2571858/article/36644233#36644233
      - RoughFudge · 2021-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722961/article/38429643#38429643
      - Clipper · 2018-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915419/article/27897779#27897779
      - Clipper · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1899565/article/27676844#27676844

    ### How does the Arborec flagship's produce-5 ability work?
- id: `arborec-flagship-produce-five` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Right after you activate the system it is already in, before movement, you may produce up to 5 units there, paying as normal; it isn't the Production step, so the regular production step still follows later. It can't move in first and then produce. Structures can't be produced this way.
  - Clipper · 2018-11-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092737/article/30454514#30454514
  - hooliganj · 2018-11-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092737/article/30454522#30454522
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888831/article/27510226#27510226
  - DrewDecimal · 2020-02-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2375624/article/34120616#34120616
  - Crikrunner · 2017-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1853658/article/26998462#26998462
  - woofdine · 2018-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1932644/article/28151160#28151160

    ### How do the Hacan trade action cards?
    - id: `hacan-arbiters-trading` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Any number of action cards can go either way in a transaction the Hacan are part of, still within one transaction per player per turn. The Hacan, or anyone trading with them, can transact regardless of neighbors, on either player's turn, though the active player must be involved.
      - Duskwalker · 2019-01-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2142058/article/31130570#31130570

      - sigmazero13 · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888297/article/27500152#27500152
      - AgentDrake · 2026-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3638644/article/47103830#47103830


## Base Factions: Mentak, Naalu and Nekro

    ### How often can the Mentak use Pillage, and does it work in the agenda phase against everyone?
    - id: `mentak-pillage-limits` — grade: Designer ruling
    - A: Pillage triggers separately each time a neighbor gains trade goods or resolves a transaction and ends with 3 or more, so it can hit each qualifying neighbor, in any phase. But the agenda phase does not make everyone neighbors: transactions with non-neighbors are allowed then, yet Pillage still only works on the Mentak's actual neighbors, as the developer ruled.
      - CreussEmissary · 2019-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2132987/article/31003989#31003989
      - JesperPersson · 2018-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1930017/article/28108715#28108715
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1930017/article/28109488#28109488
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-05-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2196102/article/31881090#31881090
      - Severedeye · 2019-01-17 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2132987/article/31000791#31000791

    ### How often can the Naalu use Foresight, and what triggers it?
    - id: `naalu-foresight-limits` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: As often as they have strategy tokens and an eligible system to move to — there is no per-round limit. It needs another player's ships to actually move into and stop in the Naalu ships' system: not a ship merely passing through, and not an activation with nothing moved in. A Naalu command token already in that system doesn't block it, just as it wouldn't block a retreat.
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862122/article/40021554#40021554
      - ajmarshall · 2022-05-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862122/article/40021579#40021579
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1887073/article/27482016#27482016
      - LovingAngel · 2017-11-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1887073/article/27493543#27493543
      - Clipper · 2019-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888550/article/31312686#31312686
      - hooliganj · 2020-01-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888550/article/33887927#33887927

### How do ground forces fighting with the Nekro Virus flagship work?
- id: `nekro-flagship-ground-forces` — grade: Community consensus
- A: They take part in the space combat as if they were ships, so the opponent can assign hits to them. They are not ships for any other purpose, so they don't count against your fleet pool or your ships' capacity.
  - McBartsomeville · 2024-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3345778/article/44669844#44669844
  - pkitty · 2024-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3345778/article/44670653#44670653
  - Weltenreiter · 2018-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2096947/article/30510252#30510252
  - Soul_ · 2018-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2096947/article/30510265#30510265
  - hooliganj · 2018-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915059/article/27895435#27895435

### Can the Nekro gain technologies from both the space combat and the ground combat of one tactical action?
- id: `nekro-technological-singularity-per-combat` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — Technological Singularity works once per combat, and each space combat and each ground combat is a separate combat. Note that if you bombard the last defender before landing, there is no ground combat on that planet.
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907825/article/27800445#27800445
  - hooliganj · 2017-12-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1907825/article/27801009#27801009

    ### Does the Nekro Virus pay normally to use the Technology secondary?
    - id: `nekro-technology-secondary-cost` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — like anyone they spend a strategy token and 4 resources, and because they can't research, Propagation gives them 3 command tokens instead.
      - Clipper · 2017-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900187/article/27686537#27686537
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1900187/article/27686644#27686644

    ### Can Naalu fighters fighting with the Matriarch win a ground combat or take control of a planet?
    - id: `naalu-matriarch-fighters-ground-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No. They join ground combat with all bonuses, but per the errata they return to space when combat ends, before a winner is decided. If only fighters remain, the combat is a draw, Dacxive Animators doesn't trigger, and the defender keeps the planet. You need actual ground forces to take control.
      - Skootur · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1858921/article/27155461#27155461
      - aegiscruiser · 2017-10-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1858921/article/27155686#27155686
      - aegiscruiser · 2017-12-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27774700#27774700
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-02-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1938366/article/28324146#28324146
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-08-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705863/article/38239624#38239624
      - aegiscruiser · 2017-10-05 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1858921/article/27090615#27090615

    ### Which kills trigger Nekro's Technological Singularity?
    - id: `nekro-singularity-what-counts` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only an opponent's units destroyed during a space or ground combat the Nekro are part of; it triggers as soon as the unit dies. Kills from Space Cannon, Bombardment, or structures destroyed when control is established don't count, since those happen outside combat. Anti-Fighter Barrage is part of combat and counts.
      - Ndjcirnc · 2022-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2801430/article/39331227#39331227
      - Clipper · 2018-01-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1857381/article/28026452#28026452
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273270/article/32904191#32904191
      - Clipper · 2020-08-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2491537/article/35670740#35670740
      - Clipper · 2020-10-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2519158/article/36009377#36009377
      - DrewDecimal · 2020-10-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2519158/article/36010511#36010511

    ### How do Nekro ground forces fighting via the flagship count for other effects?
    - id: `nekro-flagship-combat-details` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: They take part as ships for that combat, so they count as non-fighter ships for things like the Winnu flagship's extra dice (per the LRR FAQ) and probably Assault Cannon. Effects naming a specific ship type, like fighters or destroyers, never apply to them. Carried infantry can join too, then land and fight on the ground the same action. If Assault Cannon hits Nekro when the flagship is their only non-fighter ship, the flagship must go.
      - divzip · 2021-10-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916774/article/38609014#38609014
      - GamerdadMich · 2018-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1916774/article/27920032#27920032

      - IncrediSteve · 2017-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890955/article/27537634#27537634
      - hooliganj · 2023-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3199649/article/43375588#43375588
      - mliszewski · 2020-06-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2452620/article/35164415#35164415

  ### Do Mentak ships that Ambush still roll in the first round, and how does The Fourth Moon block Sustain Damage?
  - id: `mentak-ambush-and-fourth-moon` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Ambush is an extra roll at the start of combat; those ships still roll normally from the first round. The Fourth Moon stops other players' Sustain Damage in any round it takes part in, including the round it is destroyed, because Sustain Damage is used before hits remove it.
    - Crikrunner · 2017-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1892306/article/27558067#27558067
    - musique · 2023-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3028372/article/41722683#41722683
    - Clipper · 2021-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722278/article/38421165#38421165
    - zamoose · 2021-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722278/article/38421197#38421197

  ### When does Naalu Foresight happen, and where can the ships go?
  - id: `naalu-foresight-timing-and-destinations` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Right after the other player's ships move in, before Space Cannon Offense, so the Naalu leave before PDS fire, and Space Cannon can't follow them. They may go to any adjacent system without other players' ships, including the one the attacker just vacated or one with only enemy structures, but can't land ground forces there. Intercept doesn't stop it, as it isn't a combat retreat.
    - Marcial · 2021-09-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719589/article/38393345#38393345
    - selcos21 · 2022-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2849842/article/39881947#39881947
    - IncrediSteve · 2022-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2849842/article/39883034#39883034
    - McBartsomeville · 2022-04-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2852780/article/39912873#39912873
    - IncrediSteve · 2022-04-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2852780/article/39913933#39913933
    - csouth154 · 2019-10-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2289317/article/33103010#33103010

  ### What combat value do Naalu fighters use in ground combat, and can they fight on two planets?
  - id: `naalu-fighters-in-ground-combat-values` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Their own fighter combat value, upgrades included; they act as ground forces but aren't infantry. All ground forces are committed in one step before any combat, so the same fighters can't fight on two planets in one invasion.
    - IndyBart · 2020-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2374164/article/34105353#34105353
    - sigmazero13 · 2020-02-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2374164/article/34105444#34105444
    - Clipper · 2017-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1903360/article/27734205#27734205
    - sigmazero13 · 2019-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2260882/article/32757681#32757681
    - Clipper · 2019-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2260882/article/32757839#32757839

    ### How does Mentak's Ambush work?
    - id: `mentak-ambush-details` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: At the start of space combat, before Anti-Fighter Barrage, roll one die for each of up to 2 cruisers or destroyers, mixed as you like. Hits are removed at once and destroyed ships never fire back. It isn't a combat roll, so Morale Boost doesn't apply, and the ambushing ships still roll normally from the first round.
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2224251/article/32278340#32278340

      - TomBoombs · 2018-06-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2007331/article/29412180#29412180
      - rico761028 · 2017-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1833561/article/26691240#26691240
      - Surge72 · 2020-12-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556692/article/36471497#36471497

### Does the Mentak flagship stop Sustain Damage against Space Cannon hits too?
- id: `mentak-flagship-sustain` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — other players' ships in its system can't use Sustain Damage at all, whatever the source of the hit.
  - Clipper · 2019-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2321838/article/33482328#33482328

    ### When the Nekro Virus gain a technology, does the other player lose it?
    - id: `nekro-gaining-technology` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — the Nekro take the matching card from their own technology deck, and gaining ignores prerequisites. The Nekro choose which eligible technology to take, and it works immediately, even for the rest of that combat. It triggers when any opponent's unit is destroyed in combat, even one the Yin sacrifice with Devotion or one killed by Assault Cannon or Anti-Fighter Barrage.
      - THLY · 2019-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2288660/article/33094449#33094449
      - trud · 2017-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1898140/article/27656611#27656611
      - Clipper · 2018-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2108342/article/30665570#30665570
      - Clipper · 2022-12-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2997033/article/41413629#41413629
      - TomBoombs · 2018-05-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2000532/article/29180160#29180160
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-04-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1979512/article/28880822#28880822

    ### What can the Nekro copy with Valefar Assimilator?
    - id: `nekro-valefar-what-can-be-copied` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Faction technology cards another player has actually researched, unit upgrades included, two at a time, one per token; to copy a third you move a token. Units printed on faction sheets, like Muaat's Prototype War Sun I or flagships, aren't technologies and can't be copied (the Nomad's Memoria II card is the exception). Copying a faction war sun technology does make the Nekro subject to Publicize Weapon Schematics.
      - Clipper · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1857381/article/27932428#27932428
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1857381/article/27937698#27937698
      - Clipper · 2019-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2331340/article/33592767#33592767
      - Clipper · 2018-04-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1983079/article/28935925#28935925
      - Clipper · 2022-07-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2897949/article/40422828#40422828
      - Marcial · 2020-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2546485/article/36345295#36345295

    ### What happens to the Nekro's own unit upgrades when they copy a faction upgrade for the same unit?
    - id: `nekro-valefar-unit-upgrades` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The copied faction upgrade covers your generic one, which you keep; only one is in effect. Move the token away and the generic upgrade applies again. A second faction upgrade for the same unit replaces the first, using the same token.
      - TomBoombs · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953498/article/28482823#28482823
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953498/article/28480210#28480210
      - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953498/article/28482498#28482498
      - hooliganj · 2019-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2145550/article/31178239#31178239
      - Clipper · 2019-02-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2145550/article/31178528#31178528
      - Nyarlathotep90 · 2018-03-07 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1953498/article/28480030#28480030

    ### What happens when the Nekro move a Valefar token off a technology their units depend on?
    - id: `nekro-losing-copied-tech` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The copied tech is swapped, not stacked. Lose a war sun technology with no replacement and all Nekro war suns are destroyed. Lose a copied Floating Factory II and each dock in space moves to a planet you control in its system, or is destroyed if none qualifies. Ships already in a supernova after losing Magmus Reactor are fine.

      - sigmazero13 · 2018-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1922304/article/27997496#27997496
      - PotBellyNinja · 2018-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1922304/article/27997478#27997478

### Can the Nekro use technology specialties?
- id: `nekro-cannot-use-specialties` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Not for research, since they never research; Propagation gives command tokens instead, and they still pay the normal cost to follow Technology.
  - csouth154 · 2019-09-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2279796/article/32984340#32984340
  - Robo Fish · 2018-05-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1993160/article/29074158#29074158

### What are the Nekro's X and Y tokens for?
- id: `nekro-x-y-tokens` — grade: Community consensus
- A: They mark which faction technologies the Nekro are copying with their two Valefar Assimilator technologies.
  - Clipper · 2017-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1896637/article/27628240#27628240
  - sigmazero13 · 2017-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1896637/article/27628290#27628290

    ### When exactly can Mentak use Pillage?
    - id: `mentak-pillage-triggers` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: After each instance of a neighbor gaining trade goods or resolving a transaction, if that neighbor then has 3 or more trade goods and no face-up Promise of Protection. Both sides of a transaction between two neighbors can each be pillaged, in the order Mentak chooses, and receiving converted commodities counts as gaining trade goods. Nobody can dump trade goods between the trigger and Pillage. A commodity taken becomes a trade good.

      - pkitty · 2022-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2922305/article/40676265#40676265
      - Clipper · 2021-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719977/article/38397251#38397251


## Base Factions: Saar to Yssaril

    ### If Yin's Indoctrination replaces the only defending infantry, have the Yin won the ground combat?
    - id: `yin-indoctrination-wins-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — Indoctrination is used once the ground combat has begun, so if it removes the last defender the Yin win, which counts for Dacxive Animators and 'win a combat' objectives, and any card the opponent played is wasted. If Bombardment had already killed every defender, there is no ground combat to win.
      - IncrediSteve · 2023-09-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3154700/article/42963459#42963459
      - TomBoombs · 2018-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1927442/article/28075027#28075027
      - Clipper · 2018-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1927442/article/28075005#28075005
      - Clipper · 2021-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2706040/article/38241398#38241398
      - dsjensen19 · 2018-01-26 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1927442/article/28073727#28073727

    ### Are Saar Floating Factories ships, and do they count against the fleet pool?
    - id: `saar-floating-factory-not-a-ship` — grade: Designer ruling
    - A: No — Floating Factories are never ships; they only move and retreat as if they were. They don't count against your fleet pool, but every ability that affects movement, such as Gravity Drive, Flank Speed, Antimass Deflectors or Light/Wave Deflector, still applies to them and the ships moving with them.
      - CreussEmissary · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27882476#27882476
      - Pete Walsh · 2018-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27842568#27842568
      - Clipper · 2018-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27841782#27841782

    ### Can Space Cannon fire at, or hits be assigned to, a Saar Floating Factory when it moves?
    - id: `saar-floating-factory-space-cannon` — grade: Designer ruling
    - A: No — PDS cannot fire at a moving Floating Factory, and hits cannot be assigned to it because it is not a ship. The developer originally ruled the other way, then publicly reversed himself in January 2018 and put the corrected ruling in the FAQ.
      - CreussEmissary · 2018-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27878031#27878031
      - Padish · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27758193#27758193
      - halbower · 2017-12-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27757696#27757696
      - kaiteinomen · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/28525165#28525165
      - Clipper · 2018-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1904890/article/27841473#27841473

    ### Where do Saar Floating Factories go, and how many can share a system?
    - id: `saar-floating-factory-placement` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: In the space area, never on a planet. If an effect places a space dock on a planet, choose the planet as the effect says, then the Floating Factory's own rule puts it in that system's space area. There is no limit on how many share a system, and their production value is fixed rather than based on a planet. A Floating Factory is destroyed if it is left without your ships against enemy ships.
      - CplPunishment · 2019-09-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2277451/article/32955557#32955557
      - jradziew · 2019-09-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2277451/article/32955554#32955554
      - IncrediSteve · 2019-04-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2181147/article/31676844#31676844
      - Clipper · 2021-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723483/article/38438238#38438238

    ### What is Yssaril's Stall Tactics for, and can it be Sabotaged?
    - id: `yssaril-stall-tactics` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It lets Yssaril spend an action discarding an action card instead of passing, so they can wait for others to act first. It is a faction ability, not an action card, so Sabotage cannot cancel it.
      - hooliganj · 2017-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1896020/article/27615940#27615940
      - j76goatboy · 2017-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1896020/article/27616032#27616032
