      - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2704900/article/38229385#38229385

### Can units carried by the Yssaril flagship pass through systems with enemy ships, and can other Yssaril ships move with it?
- id: `yssaril-flagship-transported-units` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Fighters and ground forces it transports go wherever the flagship goes, including through such systems. Other Yssaril ships moving alongside it don't gain the ability.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2704900/article/38229385#38229385

    ### Can Xxcha's Peace Accords take a planet another player controls if no units are on it?
    - id: `xxcha-peace-accords-enemy-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the condition refers to units on the planet itself, so an enemy-controlled planet with no units on it qualifies, as long as it meets the ability's other conditions, such as adjacency.
      - Weltenreiter · 2019-07-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2247390/article/32586225#32586225
      - ajmarshall · 2021-06-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2683049/article/37984846#37984846

  ### Can Sol's Orbital Drop be used more than once a round, and can its 2 infantry go on different planets?
  - id: `sol-orbital-drop` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: It can be used as many times as you have tokens in your strategy pool, each use as a separate action. Both infantry go on one planet you control.
    - DauntlessK · 2019-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2156962/article/31333485#31333485
    - Sande24 · 2019-02-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2156962/article/31333549#31333549
    - Clipper · 2021-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2700852/article/38180611#38180611

      ### When must Xxcha use Nullification Field, and what can still happen after it ends the turn?
      - id: `xxcha-nullification-field-timing` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: It must be used right after the system is activated, before movement; Xxcha may ask the active player to pause. The active player gets first turn in that window, so their own after-activation cards like In the Silence of Space or Flank Speed resolve first and stay spent. Once the turn ends, nothing more happens in it: no further after-activation abilities, no transactions and no extra action from Master Plan.
        - Clipper · 2023-07-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3123022/article/42666536#42666536
        - Falkman · 2023-07-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3123022/article/42668092#42668092
        - Sabutai · 2018-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2107546/article/30652916#30652916
        - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563244/article/36562838#36562838
        - Clipper · 2021-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2714099/article/38330286#38330286
        - ajmarshall · 2022-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2821344/article/39557412#39557412

    ### Does Yssaril's Scheming trigger on cards taken from other players, and does their unlimited hand protect against forced discards?
    - id: `yssaril-scheming-and-discards` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both. Scheming only triggers when you draw action cards from the deck, not when you take or receive them from a player. Ignoring the hand limit doesn't stop effects that make you discard.
      - Weltenreiter · 2019-04-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2191561/article/31818004#31818004
      - Clipper · 2019-04-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2191561/article/31818070#31818070

    ### How does the Yin's Devotion work?
- id: `yin-devotion-rules` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: After a round of space combat, Yin may destroy one of their cruisers or destroyers in the active system — the errata adds 'in the active system' — to produce a hit that the Yin player assigns to an enemy ship. It is a single faction ability, so once per combat round. It has different timing from Impulse Core, so both can be used in one combat. Indoctrination works once per ground combat, at its start.
    - Verkan · 2018-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/28159661#28159661
    - Clipper · 2018-02-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/28185766#28185766
    - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261
    - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38203253#38203253
    - TheSupremeAdmiral · 2020-10-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2515140/article/35958779#35958779
    - IncrediSteve · 2019-07-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2247497/article/32587464#32587464

    ### What happens to a Saar Floating Factory when enemy ships arrive, and can it retreat?
    - id: `saar-floating-factory-in-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: If it is alone with enemy ships, it is destroyed before anything can be done, as there is no combat to retreat from, Skilled Retreat included. If other Saar ships, even fighters, are with it, it can retreat or Skilled Retreat with them as long as it is never left alone with enemy ships.
      - garfielder · 2017-09-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26955405#26955405
      - sigmazero13 · 2017-09-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26952456#26952456
      - Clipper · 2019-04-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2159188/article/31833259#31833259
      - Clipper · 2018-03-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1964309/article/28644574#28644574
      - TomBoombs · 2018-03-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1964309/article/28646859#28646859

        ### When exactly can Exotrireme II use its ability?
        - id: `sardakk-exotrireme-timing` — grade: Community consensus
        - A: At the end of a round of space combat, after hits are assigned and retreats announced; it can't be used if that Exotrireme was destroyed or a retreat was announced. A dreadnought can use Sustain Damage that round and still be sacrificed afterwards. Each Exotrireme II has its own copy, resolved one at a time.
          - TomBoombs · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912928/article/27871105#27871105
          - Dulkal · 2018-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1912928/article/27865937#27865937
          - christhomas226 · 2022-04-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2847345/article/39850022#39850022
          - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38201901#38201901
          - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38203253#38203253

    ### How do Sardakk's Unrelenting and flagship bonuses work?
- id: `sardakk-unrelenting-and-flagship` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Unrelenting adds +1 to Sardakk combat rolls only, not to Bombardment or other unit abilities. The flagship gives other ships in its system another +1, so a war sun there gets +2 and the flagship itself +1.
  - IncrediSteve · 2024-10-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3383942/article/44995360#44995360
  - Clipper · 2021-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702914/article/38201901#38201901

    ### Does Greyfire Mutagen stop the Yin flagship from destroying the ships in its system?
- id: `yin-flagship-vs-greyfire` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — a flagship's ability is a unit ability, not a faction ability, so Greyfire Mutagen doesn't affect it.
  - Clipper · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956513/article/28526589#28526589
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956513/article/28526616#28526616
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956513/article/28548972#28548972
  - aegiscruiser · 2017-11-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1891266/article/27541735#27541735

    ### Can each Winnu flagship die score a hit?
    - id: `winnu-flagship-dice` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it rolls one die per enemy non-fighter ship, and each die that meets its combat value is a hit.
      - Amuk · 2018-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947019/article/28376140#28376140
      - Weltenreiter · 2018-02-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1947019/article/28376214#28376214

    ### How does Saar's Ragh's Call work in an invasion?
    - id: `saar-raghs-call` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It is played after ground forces are committed, before Space Cannon Defense, and moves the Saar ground forces from one planet to another single planet, so it covers one planet per use.
      - Clipper · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273220/article/32905118#32905118
      - UtileDulci · 2023-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3134715/article/42782693#42782693
      - isterbat · 2023-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3134715/article/42782875#42782875

### Does the Yin flagship's self-destruct destroy a Saar Floating Factory?
- id: `yin-flagship-and-floating-factory` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it destroys ships, and Floating Factories aren't ships.
  - stugal · 2019-01-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2136423/article/31045938#31045938

    ### Can Saar Floating Factories move and produce in the same action, and how many can share a system?
    - id: `saar-floating-factory-production` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — nothing requires a dock to start the turn in the active system, so a Floating Factory can move in, carry ground forces to invade, and produce. Any number can share a system, since they sit in space and aren't bound by one dock per planet, and two of them combine Production. A Floating Factory is blockaded, and destroyed, the moment enemy ships arrive without Saar ships.
      - hooliganj · 2026-08-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3761163/article/48119603#48119603
      - IncrediSteve · 2017-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845175/article/26863719#26863719

      - hooliganj · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1888346/article/27502108#27502108
      - IndyBart · 2019-01-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2134935/article/31027857#31027857
      - sigmazero13 · 2019-11-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2312559/article/33376494#33376494

    ### How does Saar's Chaos Mapping production work?
    - id: `saar-chaos-mapping` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: At the start of each of your turns, including the turn you pass, you may produce one unit in a system with your Production units; one unit total, not one per system. It needs no command token and you still pay for it, but no unit's Production ability is used, so Sarween Tools doesn't apply.
      - GrumblingGamer · 2019-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2124495/article/30878928#30878928

      - Darkmancer · 2017-09-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1845347/article/26930037#26930037
      - Clipper · 2019-09-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2273220/article/32905118#32905118
      - Clipper · 2018-08-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033437/article/29809847#29809847

    ### How many infantry does Yin Spinner give, and does it trigger off Integrated Economy?
    - id: `yin-spinner-versions` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The original gives one infantry per use of Production, however many units produced, on any planet you control in that system, and works with the Warfare secondary; it doesn't trigger from Integrated Economy, which uses no Production ability. The Omega version gives 2 infantry, triggers whenever you produce, and changes placement.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2702835/article/38200922#38200922

### How long does Winnu's Hegemonic Trade Policy last?
- id: `winnu-hegemonic-trade-policy` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Only for that one use of Production, per its errata.
  - pkitty · 2017-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902562/article/27725261#27725261

### What does Lazax Gate Folding do?
- id: `lazax-gate-folding` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: If you don't control Mecatol Rex, during your tactical actions its system counts as containing both an alpha and a beta wormhole.
  - Marcial · 2021-09-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2730244/article/38515052#38515052


## Elimination and Game End

      ### When does the game end if nobody reaches 10 victory points?
      - id: `game-end-without-10-points` — grade: Per the rulebook — Winning the Game
      - A: When the speaker has to reveal a public objective in the status phase and none remain. The game ends right there, with no agenda phase, and the player with the most points wins. With five stage I and five stage II objectives, that is round 9 at the latest.
        - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-08-16 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
        - Clipper · 2020-10-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2515672/article/35963934#35963934
        - Clipper · 2021-08-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2716167/article/38355942#38355942
        - CBpegasus · 2017-12-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1902074/article/27718029#27718029

  ### If two players could both reach 10 points in the same status phase, who wins?
  - id: `two-players-reach-10-in-status-phase` — grade: Per the rulebook — Object of the Game
  - A: The first one in initiative order. Objectives are scored one player at a time in initiative order and the game ends the instant someone reaches the goal, so later players never score. The speaker-order tiebreak only covers truly simultaneous gains.
    - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2018-05-22 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
    - mliszewski · 2018-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033733/article/29645158#29645158
    - Clipper · 2018-07-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033733/article/29645955#29645955
    - IncrediSteve · 2018-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2092325/article/30447967#30447967
    - Ragnarwarbar · 2018-07-30 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033733/article/29645719#29645719

    ### If two players reach 10 points at the same moment in the agenda phase, who wins?
- id: `simultaneous-10-points-agenda-phase` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Start with the speaker and go clockwise: the tied speaker wins, otherwise the first tied player clockwise from the speaker. Strategy cards have no bearing in the agenda phase, so initiative order doesn't apply.
  - alercah · 2017-11-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886825/article/27511698#27511698
  - Technopinky · 2017-11-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886825/article/27479230#27479230
  - hooliganj · 2018-08-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2033733/article/29663486#29663486
  - Clipper · 2020-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2417840/article/34681770#34681770
  - IncrediSteve · 2018-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1915292/article/27896118#27896118
  - Dalek5 · 2017-11-19 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1886825/article/27478378#27478378

    ### If two players swap Support for the Throne and both would hit 10, who wins?
    - id: `support-for-the-throne-swap-win` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only one player can win. The swapped notes resolve one after the other in normal timing order, so the player who gains the point first wins and the game ends before the other scores. There are no shared victories.
      - hooliganj · 2019-07-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237283/article/32452507#32452507
      - Geoff Watson · 2019-07-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237283/article/32451899#32451899
      - Clipper · 2019-07-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237283/article/32460756#32460756
      - hooliganj · 2019-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2237283/article/32484218#32484218

    ### Can a player win during the action phase?
- id: `winning-during-action-phase` — grade: Per the rulebook — Winning the Game
- A: Yes — the game ends the moment anyone reaches the goal. Points can come in the action phase from action-phase secret objectives, the Imperial card (including scoring a public objective), the custodians token and some agendas.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-10-07 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - hooliganj · 2019-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2288718/article/33097552#33097552
  - ysirian · 2019-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2288718/article/33095364#33095364
  - sigmazero13 · 2019-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2288718/article/33095368#33095368

    ### Is there an official longer game?
- id: `fourteen-point-game` — grade: Per the rulebook — Object of the Game
- A: Yes — flip the victory point track to its 14-point side and play to 14 instead of 10.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2017-08-17 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook
  - Becq · 2017-08-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831876/article/26670229#26670229

    ### When is a player eliminated?
- id: `elimination-conditions` — grade: Per the rulebook — Elimination
- A: Only when all three are true: no ground forces on the board, no units with Production, and no planets controlled. Ships alone don't keep you in the game if the rest is gone.
  - Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition rulebook · 2019-09-10 — https://www.rulespal.com/twilight-imperium-4/rulebook

    ### After a player is eliminated, do the remaining players pick more strategy cards?
- id: `strategy-cards-after-elimination` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — keep the strategy card rules for the player count you started with, so a five-player game doesn't become a four-player game; the extra cards just collect trade goods. An eliminated player's unused strategy card is never resolved and returns at the end of the round, and agendas they owned are discarded.
  - IncrediSteve · 2025-03-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3471493/article/45745229#45745229
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-10-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2076630/article/30232324#30232324
  - Clipper · 2018-10-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2076630/article/30232410#30232410
  - Clipper · 2019-02-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1831234/article/31170539#31170539
  - Crikrunner · 2018-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2037831/article/29701476#29701476
  - SnakePlissken72 · 2025-03-03 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3471493/article/45744969#45744969

    ### When a player is eliminated, do their wormhole tokens or Naalu 0 token leave the game?
- id: `eliminated-player-tokens` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Not automatically — the elimination rule doesn't list them, so placed Creuss wormhole tokens stay on the board, and a Naalu 0 token held by another player through the promissory note stays until the end of that round.
  - sigmazero13 · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956528/article/28526941#28526941
  - Clipper · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956528/article/28526998#28526998
  - MollyMae · 2018-03-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1956528/article/28526789#28526789

    ### What happens to a technology the Nekro copied with Valefar Assimilator if its owner is eliminated?
- id: `eliminated-player-assimilated-tech` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The Nekro takes that technology card into their play area and keeps it until they move the token or the game ends; then it returns to the box with the eliminated player's components.
  - Vidyaraja · 2017-09-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26946549#26946549
  - ThierryV · 2017-09-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1850777/article/26951822#26951822


## Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition – Prophecy of Kings (Expansion)

    ### How does strategy card selection work under Checks and Balances?
    - id: `checks-and-balances-selection` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Turn order stays the same, but on your pick you give the card you choose to a player who doesn't yet have their full number of cards. Only if nobody else can take it, as for the last picker, do you keep it.
      - DistantOasis · 2021-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2724392/article/38445429#38445429
      - Clipper · 2021-09-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722609/article/38425715#38425715

  ### If Miscount Disclosed revotes Political Censure, who gains or loses points?
  - id: `political-censure-revote` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Political Censure says you lose its point when you lose the card, so the previous owner loses 1 and the new owner gains 1; the same player re-elected loses and regains it. That differs from Shard of the Throne and similar laws.
    - Clipper · 2021-08-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38305242#38305242
    - Clipper · 2021-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38310918#38310918
    - TomBoombs · 2021-08-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707675/article/38310697#38310697

    ### If several Council Keleres mechs are on a planet, does the invader pay 1 influence for each?
    - id: `keleres-mech-influence-per-mech` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each mech is its own instance of the ability, so the attacker pays 1 influence per mech. The influence can come from any mix of planets and trade goods; it doesn't have to be one planet per mech.
      - pkitty · 2024-02-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3238701/article/43716662#43716662
      - isterbat · 2024-02-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3238701/article/43717506#43717506
      - Cacotopos · 2024-02-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3238701/article/43766244#43766244
      - nemorian · 2024-02-02 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3238701/article/43722860#43722860

### How do the Council Keleres choose their home system and hero?
- id: `keleres-hero-choice` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Through The Tribunii: at setup they pick an unused faction from Mentak, Xxcha and Argent Flight and take that faction's home system, tokens and matching Keleres hero.
  - AgentDrake · 2022-04-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2857225/article/39962766#39962766

### Does the tactical action granted by the Codex Naalu agent use a token from the tactic pool, and whose turn is it?
- id: `naalu-omega-agent-tactical-action` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No token is spent from the tactic pool. The tactical action happens during the Naalu player's turn, so an effect that ends the turn, like Xxcha's Nullification Field, ends the Naalu turn and the borrowed tactical action with it.
  - Old_Man_gi · 2022-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2884087/article/40273436#40273436
  - guestid1250 · 2022-06-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2884087/article/40273354#40273354
  - brusazorzo · 2022-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2888733/article/40326376#40326376
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2888733/article/40327168#40327168

### Does Custodia Vigilia give command tokens when another player scores a public objective with Imperial?
- id: `custodia-vigilia-imperial-objectives` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it only triggers when a player gains the victory point from Imperial's Mecatol Rex ability, not when they score a public objective using Imperial.
  - Strider809 · 2023-02-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2856076/article/41809310#41809310

### Can Mahact use Decoy Operation to move a Starlancer mech into the active system and then use the mech to end the active player's turn?
- id: `mahact-decoy-operation-starlancer` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Probably yes. Both trigger after the system is activated, and a player resolving several abilities in one window chooses their order, so Decoy Operation can go first and the Starlancer ability second. Some players think it fails, by analogy with other rulings.

  ### Can the Xxcha agent ready another player's planet and remove their infantry?
  - id: `xxcha-agent-other-players-planet` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — it can ready any planet, including another player's, and if that planet is adjacent to a planet you control, the infantry you remove can be another player's too.

    ### How do you capture fighters and infantry?
    - id: `capturing-fighters-and-infantry` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only the Vuil'raith capture units, through their own abilities (or a faction copying those). A captured ship or mech goes on the captor's faction sheet. A captured fighter or infantry goes back to its owner's reinforcements as if destroyed, and the captor places a matching token on their faction sheet instead; those tokens still count for Cabal abilities.

    ### Can Titans put more than one sleeper on a planet with Terragenesis, or one on Mecatol Rex?
- id: `titans-terragenesis-limits` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No to both. Terragenesis triggers after you explore a planet without a sleeper and places one token, so a planet holds at most one. Mecatol Rex and home planets have no trait and can't be explored, so they never get one this way.

    ### Does the Vuil'raith flagship capture units destroyed at the same moment it is destroyed?
- id: `cabal-fleet-destroyed-by-yin-flagship` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes, according to the developer's later FAQ answers that players compiled: it captures every other unit destroyed in the same timing window, even if it is destroyed there too, such as by the Yin flagship or Exotrireme II; it never captures itself. That reversed an earlier answer, and some players still disagree. If the flagship was destroyed earlier, it isn't there to capture later losses.

    ### Does Ceasefire stop Sardakk N'orr from committing ground forces from adjacent systems with their commander?
    - id: `sardakk-commander-vs-ceasefire` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Ceasefire only prevents movement, and committing ground forces from an adjacent system with the commander is not movement.

### Does the Muaat hero remove tokens like the Mirage or frontier tokens from the system it converts?
- id: `muaat-hero-tokens-remain` — grade: Community consensus

  - Clipper · 2021-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2632097/article/37388647#37388647

    ### Does the Creuss commander trigger if my ship just passes through a wormhole system?
    - id: `creuss-commander-wormhole-use` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only if the ship actually moved through the wormhole, whether it started in the wormhole's system or entered it along the way. Passing through a system without using its wormhole doesn't count.

### If a Vuil'raith dimensional tear is on Mallice, which systems does Dimensional Anchor affect?
- id: `vuilraith-tear-on-mallice` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Mallice is adjacent to every system with a matching wormhole, so ships in those systems are affected. Systems that are merely next to those wormhole systems are not.

    ### Which exploration deck do I draw from when a planet has more than one trait?
- id: `explore-multi-trait-planet` — grade: Community consensus
- A: You choose one of the matching trait decks and draw a single card from it.

### Can a mech deploy ability, like the Letnev mech's, take a mech from the board if none are left in reinforcements?
- id: `deploy-only-from-reinforcements` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

    ### Can I play Counterstroke on a system I activate that already has my command token?
- id: `counterstroke-own-command-token` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No in practice — you can't normally activate a system that contains your own command token, so the trigger never comes up. Mahact's commander doesn't help either, since activating that way ends your turn at once with no after-activation abilities.

### In the Codex alliance variant, does my ally become an active player when we both move into the same system?
- id: `alliance-variant-active-player` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — only the player who activated the system is the active player. The alliance rules simply let both players' units take part in combat and ability rolls.

### What is the Codex Mahact 'Thrall of the Kings' card for?
- id: `mahact-thrall-of-the-kings` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

    ### How does Argent Flight's Raid Formation work, including against an opponent with no fighters?
    - id: `argent-raid-formation-no-fighters` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Anti-Fighter Barrage hits beyond the number of enemy fighters damage that many enemy ships with Sustain Damage, chosen by the Argent player. It produces no hits and those ships don't use Sustain Damage, so it can't destroy anything and Direct Hit or Non-Euclidean Shielding don't come into it. Argent fire barrage even when the opponent has no fighters, making every hit excess.

      ### Is a legendary planet's ability card exhausted when I take the planet?

- id: `titans-scanlink-awaken-terragenesis` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

    ### Can the Mirage sit in a supernova, and can I activate a supernova, build there and leave?
    - id: `supernova-planets-and-activation` — grade: Community consensus

    ### How does the Muaat hero's Nova Seed treat Muaat's own units and systems a war sun passes through?
    - id: `muaat-hero-nova-seed` — grade: Community consensus

    ### Can I use Scanlink Drone Network or other activation abilities when activating a system with the Mahact commander?
- id: `mahact-commander-ends-turn` — grade: Community consensus

### Can Mahact use the Arborec commander to build a mech when their system is activated, then use that mech to end the active player's turn?
- id: `mahact-arborec-commander-mech` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Generally accepted, yes: both abilities share the after-activation window, and the mech is on the board before the window closes.

    ### How does Armed Forces Standardization affect Mahact holding other players' fleet tokens?
    - id: `armed-forces-standardization-mahact` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It applies normally. Mahact chooses which tokens to remove, and other players' tokens removed go back to their owners' reinforcements.

### Does an agenda resolved with the Xxcha hero that destroys an enemy flagship score Destroy Their Greatest Ship?
- id: `xxcha-hero-destroy-objective` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Probably not — the agenda destroyed it, not your units or abilities, and only those count. No official ruling.

    ### Does Psychoarchaeology give 1 trade good per planet or 1 total?
    - id: `psychoarchaeology-per-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: 1 per planet: each use exhausts one planet with a specialty for 1 trade good, and you can repeat it for each such planet. One player reads it as one trade good total.

    ### For the Creuss mech's Icarus Drive, which system does 'this system' mean?
    - id: `creuss-mech-this-system` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The system the mech was in, not necessarily the one another player activated; a unit's 'this system' always means its own system.

    ### What does spending a command token 'from your reinforcements' mean?
    - id: `spend-token-from-reinforcements` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: You need a token in reinforcements to pay; it isn't purged or moved to your sheet, so it normally just stays in reinforcements, or goes on the board if the effect says so.

    ### Do several Muaat mechs each place infantry when Star Forge is used?
    - id: `muaat-mech-stacks` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each Muaat mech in or adjacent to the Star Forge system places its own infantry.

      - Hunamz · 2020-08-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2476462/article/35489363#35489363

    ### Can Direct Hit destroy a mech that uses Sustain Damage?
    - id: `direct-hit-vs-mechs` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Direct Hit targets ships, and mechs are ground forces, which players report the designers intended. A ship repaired by the Empyrean flagship after sustaining can still be Direct Hit, since it did use Sustain Damage.

    ### Are exploration cards public, and what happens when a deck runs out?
- id: `exploration-cards-public` — grade: Community consensus
- A: They resolve openly; relic fragments are kept face up and attachments go on planets, while other cards are discarded. When a deck runs out, shuffle its discards into a new deck; used relic fragments are purged, so they never come back.
  - hooliganj · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554104/article/36439776#36439776
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554104/article/36439790#36439790

  - IncrediSteve · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641119/article/37495809#37495809

    ### When does the Ion Storm flip, and does using a matching wormhole in the same system flip it?
    - id: `ion-storm-flipping` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It flips whenever any player's ships move into or out of a system through it, so you can exit through it and later use the other side, even in one long move. With several wormhole routes, the moving player chooses which, if any, they use, which decides whether the Ion Storm flips, per a compiled developer answer.
      - Clipper · 2021-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2643063/article/37518162#37518162
      - DistantOasis · 2021-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2643063/article/37519775#37519775

      - MollyMae · 2022-01-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2792170/article/39221624#39221624

    ### When I find Mirage by exploring, do I control it and explore it too?
    - id: `exploring-mirage-control` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — take the planet card, place a control token, and explore it as a cultural planet, since you gained control of a planet nobody controlled.

    ### How does Scanlink Drone Network work?
- id: `scanlink-needs-unit-on-planet` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Each time you activate a system, you may explore one planet there that already contains any of your units (infantry, mech, PDS or dock), even one explored before, so the same planets can be explored again and again. It won't explore planets your ships merely fly past.

    ### Can mechs use tokens beyond the 4 plastic pieces?
- id: `mechs-plastic-limit` — grade: Community consensus

    - id: `naalu-commander-agenda-peek` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it works at any time, and once an agenda is revealed the next card is the top card. A FAQ answer quoted by a player says it can't peek at the card Covert Legislation draws or interrupt the Politics primary.

    - id: `nekro-prophets-tears` — grade: Community consensus

### Can the Naalu use Foresight when the Mahact hero pushes ships into their system?

    ### Can abilities be used after an exploration card is revealed but before it resolves?

- id: `empyrean-aetherpassage-refusal` — grade: Community consensus

    ### Does Naaz-Rokha's Distant Suns work with more than one mech on the planet?
    - id: `naaz-rokha-distant-suns` — grade: Community consensus

    - id: `heroes-movement-nexus-invasion` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only by the hero's text: no path or move value, from any number of systems for the Argent hero, ignoring command tokens in the systems they leave. So the Argent hero can move ships into the Wormhole Nexus, which flips to its active side at the end of that action. The ships can carry units starting with them, but can't pick up along a path, and since it isn't a tactical action there's no invasion and nothing like Dark Energy Tap triggers.

### How does the Mahact commander work?
- id: `mahact-commander-how-it-works` — grade: Community consensus
- A: You may activate a system that already holds your command token; you then remove both your tokens from it and your turn ends at once, so nothing else, production included, happens there that turn. In effect you spend a turn and a token to undo an earlier activation, so the system can be activated again later.
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554481/article/36444123#36444123
  - Jake4Prezident · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554481/article/36444417#36444417

### Do Nekro-copied Dimensional Tears affect the Vuil'raith, and do they count for the Vuil'raith hero?
- id: `nekro-dimensional-tears` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Tears only hinder other players' units: Vuil'raith ignore their own, Nekro ignore Nekro's, but Vuil'raith are affected by Nekro tears. All tears on the board count for the Vuil'raith hero.

    ### What are captured units good for, and how do Amalgamation and Riftmeld use them?
- id: `vuilraith-captured-units` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Returning a captured unit of a type lets the Vuil'raith produce one unit of that type without paying (Amalgamation) or ignore prerequisites on its upgrade (Riftmeld). Each captured unit or token gives one unit, even fighters or infantry, and its printed cost still counts toward limits like Integrated Economy's. Any captured unit of the base type works, a Prototype War Sun counting as a war sun. Amalgamation is producing, so it can trigger Sling Relay.
  - ajmarshall · 2022-06-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2876061/article/40179717#40179717

  - zamoose · 2025-03-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3472912/article/45756110#45756110

    ### What does the Vuil'raith flagship capture?

If the Yin flagship blows up the system, the Cabal flagship is destroyed too but still captures everything else destroyed there, the Yin flagship included.

### Does the Nomad's Cavalry note give my ship Anti-Fighter Barrage itself, or just the value?

- id: `exploring-frontier-in-supernova` — grade: Community consensus

    - id: `argent-zeal-voting-order` — grade: Community consensus

    ### Which side of the sleeper token is used, when does Terragenesis move one, and can Awaken work with no PDS left?
    - id: `titans-sleeper-details` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Use the plain sleeper side; the Titans-logo side marks ownership, and the hero and note attachment tokens aren't sleepers. A planet holds one sleeper; Awaken says 'one or more' because a system may have several planets. Replaced sleepers return and can be placed again, and Terragenesis moves one only once all yours are on the board. Awaken takes a PDS from reinforcements and isn't production, so with no PDS left you can't awaken.

      - Old_Man_gi · 2022-05-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862778/article/40029147#40029147

    ### Can the Titans deploy their mech instead of placing a PDS with Construction?
- id: `titans-construction-mech-deploy` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — Construction places PDS, so the Titans' deploy works there, even twice with the primary, but only where you could legally place a PDS and with one available.

  ### Which trade good gains count for the Nomad agent Artuno?
  - id: `nomad-agent-artuno` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Trade goods gained from the supply, from action cards, agendas, the Trade card or exploration. Converting commodities doesn't count, and as written neither do trade goods taken off strategy cards.

    - serdudds · 2020-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2536510/article/36223802#36223802

    ### Does Argent's Aerie Hololattice stop ships with Light/Wave Deflector moving through?
    - id: `aerie-hololattice-vs-lightwave` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it says other players can't move through systems with Argent structures, and 'cannot' is absolute; Light/Wave Deflector only covers systems with enemy ships.

  ### Can Coup d'Etat stop a primary ability resolved through the Winnu hero or Overrule?
  - id: `coup-detat-vs-component-primaries` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — those are component actions that resolve a primary, not strategic actions, and Coup d'Etat only cancels strategic actions.

    ### How do players reach the Wormhole Nexus, and can the Creuss agent help on the first turn?
    - id: `reaching-wormhole-nexus` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Usually through a gamma wormhole, such as one from exploration. You can activate the inactive Nexus, and the Creuss agent, used after activation and before movement, makes it adjacent to a delta wormhole system, so ships can move in that turn. Once a unit enters or someone gains Mallice, the Nexus flips.

  ### How do the Yin and Naalu commanders' extra infantry and fighters work?
  - id: `yin-naalu-commanders-extra-unit` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: When producing those units you may produce one extra for its cost, outside your Production limit, so 1 resource buys 3 infantry (Yin) or 3 fighters (Naalu), and it works even when producing just one.

      - Marcial · 2022-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2789506/article/39192107#39192107

### What counts as being in my play area, for objectives about promissory notes?
- id: `play-area-meaning` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Cards in your hand aren't; a note is in your play area only if it's placed there face up, as Alliance or Support for the Throne instruct.
  - Pendragon22 · 2021-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594457/article/36915129#36915129
  - Pendragon22 · 2021-02-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2594457/article/36915174#36915174

    ### Can units leave a system with my command token, Saar Floating Factories included, and does Dominus Orb ignore range?
    - id: `dominus-orb-and-locked-docks` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No unit can move out of a system holding your command token, Floating Factories included, unless an effect like Dominus Orb allows it. Dominus Orb doesn't extend range; the units still need to reach the active system.

      - cojami · 2021-01-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2571937/article/36645265#36645265

### Does Mahact's Genetic Recombination force a player to vote a certain way?
- id: `mahact-genetic-recombination` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the player votes freely, but if they don't cast at least 1 vote for Mahact's chosen outcome, they lose a fleet pool token.
  - McBartsomeville · 2025-08-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3556146/article/46447465#46447465
  - Jake4Prezident · 2020-11-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2548069/article/36364974#36364974

### Are Mirage, the destroyed-planet marker and Ion Storm tokens attachments?
- id: `pok-tokens-not-attachments` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — they modify systems or planets but aren't exploration attachments.
  - zamoose · 2021-09-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2732243/article/38534921#38534921

### When an exploration card says to remove a ground force, where does it go?
- id: `exploration-remove-ground-force` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Back to reinforcements; it isn't destroyed, so Infantry II doesn't trigger.
  - Clipper · 2021-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2585893/article/36813286#36813286

  ### Who goes first when the Muaat hero and Naalu Foresight both trigger as a war sun moves in?
  - id: `muaat-hero-vs-foresight` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Muaat, as the active player, so the hero can destroy the Naalu ships before Foresight.

    - ajmarshall · 2022-01-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2787900/article/39170906#39170906

    ### If several Crimson Legionnaires die at once, does each trigger separately?
    - id: `crimson-legionnaire-triggers` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each has its own trigger, resolved one after another.

      - hooliganj · 2023-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3202813/article/43405788#43405788

    ### Do 'during the action phase' agent abilities use my action, and does 'a player' include me?
    - id: `agents-and-a-player` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No action is used; only 'Action:' abilities take your turn, so such agents can be used any time in that phase, often on others. 'A player' includes you; only 'another player' excludes you.
      - IncrediSteve · 2023-10-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3167109/article/43078012#43078012

      - AgentDrake · 2022-06-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2882179/article/40250432#40250432

      ### How does the Xxcha hero's ability to combine a planet's resources and influence work?
      - id: `xxcha-combined-values-ability` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: When you exhaust a planet, it provides its combined value as resources or influence, so Mecatol Rex gives 7, even when exhausted for Uprising. It doesn't change printed values, so it doesn't raise a space dock's Production.
        - ajmarshall · 2022-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868302/article/40091241#40091241
        - ajmarshall · 2022-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868302/article/40094863#40094863

        - Clipper · 2022-05-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868302/article/40105852#40105852
        - Clipper · 2022-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868302/article/40099965#40099965

    ### Are the Arborec and Hacan heroes limited by Production?
    - id: `arborec-and-hacan-heroes-production` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The Arborec hero's units aren't produced with Production, so only resources limit them; infantry still come two per resource. The Hacan hero makes every unit you produce free, though fleet and unit limits still apply.

    ### Do Creuss wormhole tokens count for unlocking the Creuss commander?
    - id: `creuss-tokens-for-commander` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — the tokens are wormholes for all purposes.

      - sigmazero13 · 2025-09-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3579126/article/46633165#46633165

### Can Anti-Fighter Barrage hit ground forces fighting via the Nekro flagship?
- id: `alastor-forces-and-afb` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — barrage only hits fighters, and those ground forces are not fighters.

  ### Can L1Z1X mechs bombard through Harrow?
  - id: `l1z1x-mech-harrow` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Only when not in that ground combat: a mech fighting on a planet can't bombard it, but one on another planet in the system can.

    ### Does the Naalu Omega mech stop opponents rolling Anti-Fighter Barrage at all?
    - id: `naalu-iconoclast-omega-afb` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — other players can't use barrage in that system, so no dice are rolled and effects tied to it, like Argent's, don't trigger.

    ### Does the Yin hero commit 3 infantry per planet or 3 in total?
    - id: `yin-hero-three-total` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: 3 in total, spread across one or more non-home planets, which are then invaded.

### With the Vuil'raith agent, who gets the trade goods converted from the target's commodities?
- id: `cabal-agent-trade-goods` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The targeted player keeps them as their own trade goods; the Cabal only capture the unit.

    ### Is flipping Naaz-Rokha mechs to ships mandatory, and do they count against the fleet pool?
    - id: `naaz-rokha-mech-flip` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Flipping is mandatory, as nothing says 'may', but only for mechs already in the space area when combat starts; mechs on planets can't join. They still use capacity while in space, so they don't count against the fleet pool.

    ### How does Insider Information interact with Covert Legislation?
    - id: `insider-information-covert-legislation` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Covert Legislation is a 'when' effect that takes the next agenda first, so Insider Information, an 'after' effect, sees the card after that.
      - Geoff Watson · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555239/article/36451769#36451769
      - pedro2112 · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555239/article/36451809#36451809

    ### Can Self-Assembly Routines place a mech when Muaat produce in a supernova?
    - id: `self-assembly-routines-supernova` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — there is no planet in a supernova to place it on.

      - ajmarshall · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552899/article/36422744#36422744

  ### Can the Scepter of Emelpar help pay spend-token objectives?
  - id: `scepter-of-emelpar-objectives` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — a token spent from reinforcements through the Scepter in place of a strategy token counts, though the rest must still come from your pools.

### Can the Trade primary or the Winnu hero choose zero other players for the free secondary?

### Does Sarween Tools reduce units produced with the Muaat agent?
- id: `muaat-agent-no-sarween` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

### Which ground forces can Ghost Squad move?
- id: `ghost-squad-which-ground-forces` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Only ground forces already on a planet you control in the active system, moved to one other planet you control in that same system — from one planet to one planet. It cannot bring ground forces in from other systems.
  - TinTeardrop · 2021-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2713880/article/38327791#38327791

    ### Can Reverse Engineer take an action card another player just played or had Sabotaged, or one I discarded?
    - id: `reverse-engineer-just-played-card` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It takes a component action card another player discards for any reason, so one they just played or one cancelled by Sabotage; that is its usual use. Not one you discarded yourself, since the card needs another player's discard.

    ### What happens if Rout forces a retreat but there's no legal system, or the combat ends first?
- id: `rout-without-legal-retreat` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Rout needs a legal destination; without one it does nothing and the ships keep fighting, they aren't destroyed. If the combat ends before the retreat, for example because Assault Cannon kills the last ship at the start, no retreat happens.

  - brettellmd · 2022-04-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2851938/article/39903091#39903091

### In a seven- or eight-player setup, does the speaker look at the extra tiles before placing them by Mecatol Rex?
- id: `seven-eight-player-extra-tiles` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — they are placed unseen. The hyperlane maps, which need no extra tiles, are recommended instead.

### If another player already holds my home planet when I gain the Shard of the Throne relic, do they take it?
- id: `shard-relic-already-controlled-planet` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

### Can I decline to resolve an exploration card I drew?

### Do the Nomad use all three of their agents?
- id: `nomad-three-agents` — grade: Community consensus

    ### Does the Nekro hero need prerequisites for the technology it gains?

    ### Does the Nekro commander draw an action card for the very technology that unlocks it?

### For Betray a Friend, must I still hold the other player's note when I win the combat?
- id: `betray-a-friend-timing` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

### Can Imperial Arms Vault place a mech when I have none in reinforcements?
- id: `imperial-arms-vault-mech` — grade: Community consensus

### Which base game components does Prophecy of Kings replace?
- id: `pok-replaced-components` — grade: Community consensus

### How do the Creuss place and move wormhole tokens with their promissory note and mech?
- id: `creuss-gamma-wormhole-placement` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Placing puts an off-board Creuss token on the board; moving relocates one already there. The note affects one token per use. Only the Creuss-assigned tokens can be used; the Creuss gamma token comes out through Wormhole Generator or the Creuss mech, which reacts to any system activation by putting a token in the mech's own system, as intended.

### How many extra dice does the Argent Flight commander give?
- id: `argent-commander-extra-die` — grade: Community consensus
- A: One per roll of a unit ability that rolls dice (Anti-Fighter Barrage, Space Cannon, Bombardment): one of the rolling units rolls one extra die, not one per unit.
  - ajmarshall · 2022-03-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2833057/article/39694917#39694917
  - sigmazero13 · 2022-03-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2833057/article/39696269#39696269
  - THLY · 2022-04-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2852620/article/39911013#39911013
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-04-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2852620/article/39911085#39911085

    ### How does exploration work?
    - id: `exploration-basics` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: When you gain control of a planet nobody controlled, draw a card from the exploration deck matching its trait; this happens before 'after you gain control' effects like Integrated Economy or Pre-Fab Arcologies, which you then order as you like. Frontier tokens mark planetless systems still to be explored. Abilities like Naaz-Rokha's let you re-explore planets you hold.
      - raptor666 · 2021-08-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2712696/article/38326804#38326804
      - AgentDrake · 2021-08-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2712696/article/38316666#38316666
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2774297/article/39024237#39024237
      - TinTeardrop · 2021-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2774297/article/39026433#39026433
      - Clipper · 2021-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2770235/article/38980861#38980861
      - Gylthinel · 2021-12-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2770235/article/39058157#39058157

    ### What does Political Censure stop, and how is it removed?
    - id: `political-censure-rules` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only playing action cards; strategy cards are unaffected. The elected player can't discard it themselves; it leaves only through other effects, and then the restriction ends.
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-06-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2674012/article/37878885#37878885
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-06-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2674012/article/37879020#37879020
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-11-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2963171/article/41081238#41081238
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-11-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2963171/article/41082635#41082635

### Can Stellar Converter target a planet in the same system as my Bombardment unit?
- id: `stellar-converter-same-system` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — a planet counts as adjacent to the system containing it.
  - Spinners71 · 2021-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2661688/article/37737645#37737645
  - THLY · 2021-05-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2661688/article/37738909#37738909

### Does the Naaz-Rokha flagship give one extra die total or one per mech?
- id: `naaz-rokha-flagship-dice` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: One per mech in its system.
  - Nurgle84 · 2021-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2599538/article/36978360#36978360
  - Kazadoom · 2021-02-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2599538/article/36981323#36981323
  - Heiko Doubleu · 2021-02-11 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2599538/article/36978010#36978010

    ### Does Defy Space and Time need more than one unit in the Wormhole Nexus?
- id: `defy-space-and-time-one-unit` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — one unit is enough; the plural is just the card template.
  - Kazadoom · 2021-02-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2598416/article/36960958#36960958
  - mendeleev · 2021-02-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2598416/article/36961295#36961295

    ### Are my unplayed promissory notes in my hand, so effects that take notes from hands can get them?
    - id: `promissory-notes-in-hand` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — they are your hand until placed face up in a play area, so they can be traded or taken by Diplomatic Pressure or the Naalu hero. With the Naalu hero, each player chooses which note to give.
      - pedro2112 · 2021-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2593314/article/36902283#36902283
      - pkitty · 2021-02-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2593314/article/36905846#36905846
      - jeffwagner · 2021-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2593314/article/36900959#36900959
      - AgentDrake · 2021-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2707832/article/38261258#38261258

    ### Can a planet with the Demilitarized Zone attachment be invaded?
    - id: `demilitarized-zone-invasion` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — nobody can put units on it, so it can't be invaded. Effects that take control without placing units, such as Peace Accords, still can.
      - TerTer · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581843/article/36762426#36762426
      - zamoose · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2581843/article/36762487#36762487

    ### Can Genetic Recombination target a player who isn't voting, such as one who played a rider?
    - id: `genetic-recombination-non-voters` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — it triggers before a player casts votes, and a non-voter never reaches that point.
      - THLY · 2021-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2580885/article/36754485#36754485
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2580885/article/36755042#36755042

    ### How do Raid Formation and Waylay interact?
    - id: `raid-formation-and-waylay` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Raid Formation works off the barrage roll without using up the hits. With Waylay, those hits still hit ships and the opponent assigns them normally, using Sustain Damage on undamaged ships if they like.
      - hooliganj · 2020-12-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559976/article/36518041#36518041
      - Blaze_Of_Glory · 2020-12-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559976/article/36515501#36515501
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559976/article/36514392#36514392
      - Geoff Watson · 2020-12-15 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2559976/article/36514806#36514806

    ### Can ships use the Wormhole Nexus's alpha and beta wormholes on the move that flips it?
- id: `wormhole-nexus-flip-timing` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — it becomes active only at the end of the movement step, so you can't come in through the gamma wormhole and out through its alpha or beta in the same move. Once active, it is ordinary adjacency.
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553413/article/36429687#36429687
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553413/article/36446477#36446477
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553413/article/36445459#36445459

    ### Can the Titans deploy their flagship for free, and with a PDS placed by Awaken that turn?
    - id: `titans-flagship-deploy` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It is free, swapping a PDS in a system you activate for the flagship, at the cost of the PDS. But the PDS must be there when you activate, so one placed by Awaken during that activation doesn't qualify. Their mech deploys only when a sleeper is awakened, in place of the PDS.
      - Jake4Prezident · 2020-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2550820/article/36395001#36395001
      - ElectroNugget · 2020-11-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2550820/article/36396842#36396842
      - McBartsomeville · 2023-04-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3074687/article/42188621#42188621
      - hooliganj · 2023-05-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3074687/article/42197898#42197898
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2735188/article/38570269#38570269
      - TinTeardrop · 2021-10-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2735188/article/38573111#38573111

### Is the Dyson Sphere attachment +2 resources and +1 influence or the reverse?
- id: `dyson-sphere-values` — grade: Community consensus
- A: +2 resources, +1 influence, as on the card; the rulebook picture shows the Lazax Survivors token by mistake.
  - ajmarshall · 2020-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542598/article/36297051#36297051
  - Parzival1996 · 2020-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542598/article/36297510#36297510

    ### If Winnu place a mech with Hope's End, can the Winnu mech ability then place a space dock?
    - id: `winnu-mech-hopes-end` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — Hope's End resolves at the end of your turn, after the window where the mech's ability triggers. Likewise, a dock placed by Winnu's Reclamation comes after the tactical action, too late to produce in it.
      - Clipper · 2023-01-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3005358/article/41488942#41488942

      - aegiscruiser · 2017-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/1890123/article/27605555#27605555

### Does Dark Energy Tap let me retreat without a combat?
- id: `dark-energy-tap-retreat` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — its retreat part only lifts the usual destination requirement for retreats during space combat, and ground forces carried in a retreat can't land in the new system.
  - HP12C_guy · 2022-10-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2946679/article/40921088#40921088
  - Clipper · 2022-10-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2946679/article/40921094#40921094
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2558960/article/36502241#36502241

### Can the Yin mech let Indoctrination replace an opponent's mech?
- id: `yin-mech-indoctrination` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — Indoctrination still replaces an opponent's infantry; the mech only changes your replacing unit into a mech.
  - Clipper · 2021-11-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2756391/article/38820862#38820862

### During the Xxcha hero, can a player holding the Xxcha alliance vote?
- id: `xxcha-hero-no-other-voters` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — the hero resolves agendas outside the agenda phase as if Xxcha voted, so nobody else votes.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2711039/article/38297592#38297592

### When the Mahact mech ends a player's turn, where does that player's command token go?
- id: `mahact-mech-ends-turn-token` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: They take it back into a pool of their choice on their command sheet.
  - Cacotopos · 2021-07-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2694624/article/38110126#38110126

### Can the Naalu agent give a player a tactical action in a system they already activated?
- id: `naalu-agent-tactical-action-limits` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it grants a tactical action, which must activate a system without that player's command token.
  - IncrediSteve · 2023-06-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3094665/article/42390447#42390447

### If Brother Milor places infantry during Colonial Redistribution, is there a ground combat?
- id: `colonial-redistribution-brother-milor` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — the agenda destroys each ground force in the system, new ones included, and ground combat only happens during invasions or when an ability says so.
  - Clipper · 2022-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2974502/article/41196883#41196883
  - ajmarshall · 2022-11-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2974502/article/41198573#41198573

### Does the Empyrean home system have a planet?
- id: `empyrean-home-planet` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it is a nebula containing a home planet with normal resource and influence values.
  - guestid1250 · 2022-07-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2903715/article/40489521#40489521
  - DarkThomy · 2022-07-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2903715/article/40520903#40520903

### Does the Winnu commander unlock if another player attacks Winnu ships at Mecatol Rex?
- id: `winnu-commander-defending` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the condition is a combat in the Mecatol Rex system, which defending counts as.
  - MollyMae · 2023-08-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3141495/article/42845122#42845122

    ### Can the Mahact agent, or other effects used instead of spending a strategy token, work with an empty strategy pool?
    - id: `mahact-agent-instead-of-token` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — they replace the token, so none is needed; the same goes for the Scepter of Emelpar and the Naaz-Rokha hero. With the Mahact agent on Construction's secondary, the active player's own token goes in the system, blocking them there.

      - Old_Man_gi · 2020-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2558557/article/36498135#36498135

    ### With Riftmeld, must the captured unit belong to a player who has the upgrade?
    - id: `vuilraith-riftmeld-any-captured` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — any captured unit of the matching type lets you ignore that upgrade's prerequisites.
      - AgentDrake · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553337/article/36428592#36428592

### Can the L1Z1X agent upgrade an infantry that moves into the active system?
- id: `l1z1x-agent-timing` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it is used right after activation, before movement, so the unit must already be there.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2588815/article/36844051#36844051

### How does Rearmament Agreement's Against outcome replace mechs?
- id: `rearmament-agreement-against` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Each mech is replaced by one infantry where it stood, so four mechs become four infantry.
  - PropheticPlatypus · 2022-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2962743/article/41076443#41076443
  - pkitty · 2022-10-31 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2962743/article/41078535#41078535

### Does removing infantry for an exploration card trigger the Crimson Legionnaire?
- id: `removed-not-destroyed-legionnaire` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — removing a unit isn't destroying it, so 'after this unit is destroyed' abilities don't trigger.
  - sigmazero13 · 2023-06-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3099289/article/42435419#42435419

    ### If the Titans retreat into a system with a sleeper, does it awaken?
    - id: `titans-retreat-no-awaken` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — a retreat places a command token but doesn't activate the system.
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-04-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2846482/article/39842397#39842397
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-04-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2846482/article/39842937#39842937

### Can Saar take Scavenge's trade good and spend it on their mech's deploy for the same planet?
- id: `saar-scavenge-then-mech` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — both trigger after gaining control, so you choose the order.
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2804734/article/39367804#39367804

### How does the Arborec mech's deploy work with Mitosis?
- id: `arborec-mech-mitosis` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Instead of placing a new infantry, you swap one of your infantry on the board for a mech, ending with one fewer infantry and one more mech.
  - Kazadoom · 2022-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2800249/article/39317349#39317349

    ### Can Transit Diodes move Titans Hel-Titans or ordinary PDS?
    - id: `transit-diodes-hel-titans` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Hel-Titans yes: they are ground forces, and Transit Diodes removes and places units rather than transporting them. Ordinary PDS aren't ground forces, so no.
      - Nagual84 · 2021-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2678543/article/37931374#37931374
      - AgentDrake · 2021-06-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2678543/article/37931411#37931411

      - Ketos · 2021-05-20 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2662444/article/37746957#37746957

    ### Is using my agent for another player a transaction, and must we be neighbors?
    - id: `agents-not-transactions` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No to both — it is just an ability; it can be part of a deal. Agents trigger on their own timing on anyone's turn and, unless worded 'you' or 'your', on anyone's units, though a few, like Artuno or the Mahact agent, only help their owner. The Yssaril agent copying the Hacan agent works alongside it.
      - sigmazero13 · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587912/article/36832584#36832584
      - AgentDrake · 2021-01-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2587912/article/36832623#36832623
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862582/article/40026551#40026551
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-05-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2862582/article/40028245#40028245
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947531/article/40930163#40930163

    ### Are mechs ground forces?
    - id: `mechs-are-ground-forces` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes, for every purpose. They aren't infantry, so infantry-only rules, like the Arborec dock's ban on producing infantry, don't apply to them. Deploy places a unit without producing it, and you don't need one on the board already.
      - Clipper · 2021-07-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2698246/article/38150760#38150760
      - Clipper · 2021-08-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705516/article/38235934#38235934
      - Clipper · 2021-08-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705516/article/38239651#38239651
      - Clipper · 2021-08-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2716842/article/38361221#38361221
      - AgentDrake · 2021-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2575335/article/36685934#36685934

    ### Can the Xxcha agent target a ready planet just to remove an infantry?
- id: `xxcha-agent-needs-exhausted-planet` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — you can't ready a planet that is already ready, so the target must be exhausted for any of it to work.
  - hooliganj · 2021-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2591072/article/36874569#36874569
  - TerTer · 2021-01-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2591072/article/36874521#36874521

### Does the Keleres I.I.H.Q. Modernization make systems adjacent for movement?
- id: `keleres-iihq-neighbors-only` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it only makes players neighbors for transactions.
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2984307/article/41291568#41291568

    ### Does the Letnev commander give a trade good for each unit that uses Sustain Damage?
    - id: `letnev-commander-per-sustain` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each use triggers it separately.
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-10-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947542/article/40930224#40930224
      - Kazadoom · 2022-10-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2947542/article/40942771#40942771
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719914/article/38396528#38396528

### Can the Jol-Nar commander reroll several kinds of unit ability rolls?
- id: `jol-nar-commander-rerolls` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — each Anti-Fighter Barrage, Bombardment or Space Cannon roll; Infantry II's revival roll isn't a unit ability roll.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719914/article/38396528#38396528

### Does the route my ships take matter for abilities that care where ships move?
- id: `movement-path-irrelevant` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — beyond being legal and triggering gravity rifts, only where the ships end up matters.

    ### If an exhausted technology returns to my deck and is researched again, is it exhausted?
    - id: `re-researched-tech-ready` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — cards in the deck have no state, so it comes back ready.
      - Kazadoom · 2021-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607093/article/37069341#37069341
      - sigmazero13 · 2021-02-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2607093/article/37069350#37069350

    ### When does a commander unlock?
    - id: `commanders-unlock-immediately` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: As soon as its condition is met, mid-turn if need be, so a third mech arriving unlocks the Naaz-Rokha commander before combat even if it then dies. It is checked only after the current effect fully resolves, and Mahact's Imperia can only use another player's commander once it is unlocked.
      - Spinners71 · 2022-05-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2868656/article/40095878#40095878
