      - hehaheho · 2021-07-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2698626/article/38154194#38154194

### Do the faction reference sheets give rules?
- id: `codex-reference-sheets-not-rules` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — they are simplified summaries for choosing factions; the faction sheet text governs.
  - AgentDrake · 2022-05-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2863502/article/40035661#40035661
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-05-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2863502/article/40035980#40035980
  - AgentDrake · 2022-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2785574/article/39145579#39145579

    ### Under Checks and Balances, does a player who was given a card still pick one on their turn?
    - id: `checks-and-balances-given-card` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — you always pick and give away a card, so they still pick and pass one to an eligible player.
      - IncrediSteve · 2022-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2809901/article/39425139#39425139
      - hooliganj · 2022-02-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2809901/article/39425208#39425208

    ### Is a Hel-Titan a valid target for effects on non-structures, and can it produce?
    - id: `hel-titan-structure-and-production` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It is a structure as well as a ground force, so the Vuil'raith vortex, which needs a non-structure, can't take it. It has Production, so it can produce in a tactical action's production step, though it can't be produced itself or used with Sling Relay, which needs a space dock.
      - thomsonbriand · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2582038/article/36764337#36764337
      - AgentDrake · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2582038/article/36764769#36764769
      - zamoose · 2021-01-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2582038/article/36764747#36764747
      - Marcial · 2021-09-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2729941/article/38511795#38511795
      - pkitty · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679837/article/37945617#37945617
      - AgentDrake · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679837/article/37945436#37945436

### With the Hacan commander, can I vote using only trade goods?
- id: `hacan-commander-trade-goods-votes` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — casting votes means exhausting any number of planets, zero included, so you can vote purely with trade goods spent through the commander.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2573271/article/36658677#36658677

    ### Do the Vuil'raith roll for ordinary gravity rifts?
    - id: `vuilraith-normal-gravity-rifts` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — their exemption covers only rifts created by their own Dimensional Tear docks. Those dock systems are anomalies, which counts for objectives like Making History. Their hero makes each enemy ship there roll as if leaving a rift, capturing it on a 1-3.
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2557664/article/36486251#36486251
      - Nurgle84 · 2021-02-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2600971/article/36996818#36996818
      - halbower · 2020-11-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2550206/article/36389061#36389061
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2597329/article/36947672#36947672

### How do the Argent agent's placement and the Argent commander's unlock work?
- id: `argent-agent-and-commander-unlock` — grade: Community consensus
- A: The agent places ground forces only on planets that player controls, in the producing or adjacent systems, and since production follows invasion it never invades. For the commander, any mix of units with Anti-Fighter Barrage, Space Cannon or Bombardment counts toward 6, PDS included.
  - Geoff Watson · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552960/article/36423250#36423250
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552960/article/36423320#36423320
  - Wh0isTh3D0ct0r · 2020-12-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552960/article/36433906#36433906
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2552960/article/36423426#36423426

### Does the tactical action from the Naalu agent cost a command token?
- id: `naalu-agent-no-token` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — no token is spent from the tactic pool.
  - hannibal555 · 2022-08-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2916134/article/40616439#40616439

### Do ships moved by the L1Z1X hero out of a gravity rift still roll?
- id: `heroes-and-gravity-rifts` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — ships leaving a rift roll as usual whatever moved them.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2706707/article/38248091#38248091

### How many fighters does the Creuss commander give?
- id: `creuss-commander-fighters` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: One per ship with capacity that moved through a wormhole, not per spare capacity, and you can only keep fighters you have capacity for once movement ends.
  - zamoose · 2021-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2681913/article/37971474#37971474
  - zamoose · 2021-06-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2681913/article/37971521#37971521

### Can the Sardakk commander move ground forces onto planets I already control?
- id: `sardakk-commander-friendly-planets` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — committing happens in the invasion step whether or not you own the planet.
  - hooliganj · 2021-02-27 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2610946/article/37123250#37123250

### Can Nomad mechs cancel hits with Quantum Manipulator when the Mentak flagship is present?
- id: `nomad-mech-vs-fourth-moon` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the Mentak flagship stops other players' ships using Sustain Damage, and a mech isn't a ship.
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-08-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2925877/article/40711428#40711428

### In the Alliance variant, do 'when you gain control' abilities trigger when my ally takes a planet?
- id: `alliance-gain-control-effects` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — allies' planets count as yours for abilities about planets you control, but only the player who takes a planet gains control of it. Joining an ally's combat means placing your own token there, so you would lose a Support for the Throne from the defender.
  - ajmarshall · 2022-03-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2827533/article/39628954#39628954
  - Old_Man_gi · 2021-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2755062/article/38804311#38804311

### Is the trade good from Minister of Exploration mandatory?
- id: `minister-of-exploration-mandatory` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the law has no 'may'.
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2795414/article/39260194#39260194

    ### Do the Crucible note's +1 and a gravity rift's +1 stack, and can I use several end-of-turn abilities?
- id: `stacking-movement-and-end-of-turn` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes to both: movement bonuses stack, and you can resolve each end-of-turn ability at the end of your turn, even the turn you pass.
  - IncrediSteve · 2022-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2785470/article/39145218#39145218
  - Spinners71 · 2022-01-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2785453/article/39144761#39144761
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553452/article/36430729#36430729

    ### Does Infantry II trigger on infantry removed by the Jol-Nar agent?
- id: `removed-not-destroyed-sucaban` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — removing isn't destroying.
  - pkitty · 2021-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2729300/article/38504110#38504110
  - Kazadoom · 2021-09-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2729300/article/38504119#38504119

    ### When can Psychoarchaeology be used?
    - id: `psychoarchaeology-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: At any point in the action phase, not only on your turn, but never in the middle of resolving another player's ability; in a shared window players alternate from the active player.
      - hooliganj · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555422/article/36455570#36455570
      - Kazadoom · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555422/article/36454712#36454712
      - pkitty · 2021-06-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2684360/article/37999840#37999840
      - Old_Man_gi · 2021-06-29 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2684360/article/37999418#37999418

    ### Can the Titans awaken a sleeper on a planet another player has taken?
- id: `titans-awaken-on-taken-planet` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — the sleeper stays until Awaken, and if enemy ground forces are there a ground combat follows, the winner keeping the planet. Awaken placing a PDS also counts as placing one for their mech's deploy.
  - LordChanter · 2021-06-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679969/article/37946476#37946476
  - zamoose · 2021-06-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2679969/article/37947599#37947599
  - PistonLoop · 2021-10-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2743864/article/38671763#38671763

### As Hacan holding the Empyrean's Dark Pact, can I trigger it?
- id: `empyrean-dark-pact-hacan` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it checks your own maximum commodities, and you can give that many in one transaction; trades needn't be one-for-one.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-04-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2646353/article/37556057#37556057
  - AgentDrake · 2021-04-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2646353/article/37556100#37556100

### What happens when an exploration card adds a technology specialty to a planet that already has one?
- id: `research-facility-specialty` — grade: Community consensus
- A: It gives +1 resource and +1 influence instead; a planet never has two specialties. Show only the half of the card that applies. An attached specialty counts as the planet's own and is used by exhausting the planet.
  - HiveFleetWormwood · 2021-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2632620/article/37393848#37393848
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-04-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2632620/article/37394231#37394231

  - IncrediSteve · 2021-05-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2654023/article/37645052#37645052

  - pedro2112 · 2020-11-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2537899/article/36238809#36238809

    ### If an exploration card on a planet makes me remove my ground forces, do I keep the planet?
- id: `exploring-removes-forces-keep-control` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — you explore after gaining control. Cards that remove infantry as a cost, like Volatile Fuel Source or Core Mine, are optional; skip the cost and you skip the benefit. Exploration cards like Demilitarized Zone always affect your own units.
  - zamoose · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563532/article/36560592#36560592
  - zamoose · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563532/article/36560602#36560602

  - Viraux · 2021-10-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2744732/article/38681914#38681914
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-11-26 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2548884/article/36374597#36374597

### If Xxcha take a neutral planet with Peace Accords, do they explore it?
- id: `peace-accords-explore` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — you explore any planet you gain from no other player, however you took it.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2773987/article/39021362#39021362

### Does Letnev's War Funding let me choose whether my opponent rerolls?
- id: `war-funding-opponent-reroll` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the user decides whether the opponent rerolls all their dice. Like other combat rerolls it doesn't touch Anti-Fighter Barrage.
  - Clipper · 2021-09-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2722200/article/38422630#38422630

    ### Does Yin Spinner Omega trigger off Integrated Economy?
- id: `yin-spinner-omega-integrated-economy` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — unlike the original, it doesn't require a unit to use Production, so it triggers there as well as in the production step. The original gives a single infantry however many units produce. Sarween Tools still doesn't apply to Integrated Economy.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2704742/article/38227672#38227672
  - Clipper · 2019-09-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2267706/article/32839315#32839315

    ### Do Naaz-Rokha mechs keep their extra dice under Articles of War?
- id: `articles-of-war-naaz-rokha-dice` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — extra dice are part of the combat value, not a printed ability.
  - Clipper · 2021-07-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2698235/article/38150818#38150818
  - PistonLoop · 2022-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2961961/article/41072161#41072161

### Does the Yin hero work on planets with none of my infantry?
- id: `yin-hero-needs-infantry` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — 'any number of your infantry' here means at least one on the planet.
  - Clipper · 2021-05-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2656900/article/37677856#37677856

### Does Mahact's Scepter of Dominion make the Mahact player place a token too?
- id: `scepter-of-dominion-mahact` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — 'each other player' means everyone but the note's holder.
  - ajmarshall · 2021-04-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2646446/article/37557363#37557363

    ### Does producing with Sling Relay trigger the Prophecy of Ixth drawback?
    - id: `sling-relay-and-prophecy-of-ixth` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — it only applies when you use a unit's Production ability, which Sling Relay doesn't. Sarween Tools works the same way: it needs a unit's Production, not just an effect that lets you produce.
      - ajmarshall · 2021-03-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2616272/article/37189100#37189100
      - Kazadoom · 2021-03-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2612651/article/37142942#37142942

### Does the Mentak hero still give ships in a round where all Mentak ships die?
- id: `mentak-hero-all-ships-lost` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the new ships are placed before the game checks whether combat is over, so if Mentak destroyed any enemy ships that round, the fight goes on.
  - Clipper · 2021-02-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2602811/article/37017396#37017396

### Can other players play action cards during the Xxcha hero's action?
- id: `xxcha-hero-action-cards-blocked` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — no one else may resolve abilities then, and action card effects are abilities.
  - ajmarshall · 2020-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2547021/article/36351360#36351360
  - AgentDrake · 2020-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2547021/article/36351373#36351373

    ### Can the Nomad agent be used in combats the Nomad aren't in?
    - id: `thundarian-other-combats` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — it has no participation requirement, so it can be used for, or sold to, others, as most agents can.
      - Nurgle84 · 2021-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2597512/article/36948535#36948535
      - pkitty · 2021-02-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2597512/article/36949200#36949200

    ### If a planet card is traded in the agenda phase, can the receiver vote with it?
- id: `demilitarized-zone-traded-agenda-phase` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Trading the card transfers control, and it arrives exhausted, so it can't be passed on ready to vote again on the same agenda; its state matters little since planets ready afterwards.
  - IncrediSteve · 2021-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2593339/article/36899809#36899809
  - sigmazero13 · 2021-02-02 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2593339/article/36903031#36903031

    ### When the Creuss hero swaps two system tiles, do the units move with them?
    - id: `creuss-hero-swaps-units` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — units are on the tile's planets and space area, so they go with it.
      - IncrediSteve · 2021-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2582238/article/36767222#36767222
      - sigmazero13 · 2021-01-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2582238/article/36766245#36766245

  ### Can the Vuil'raith produce a captured war sun without the War Sun technology?
  - id: `vuilraith-captured-war-sun` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No, by analogy with rulings on Mentak's Salvage Operations and Nekro war suns: you need the War Sun technology.
    - matenason · 2020-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2560730/article/36524798#36524798
    - Kazadoom · 2020-12-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2560730/article/36524442#36524442

### Does Naaz-Rokha's Supercharge give +1 to one unit or all?
- id: `naaz-rokha-supercharge` — grade: Community consensus
- A: All your units' combat rolls that round.

  - hooliganj · 2020-12-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2551872/article/36408965#36408965

### Does the Jol-Nar commander reroll Infantry II revival rolls?
- id: `jol-nar-commander-infantry-ii` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — that isn't a unit ability roll; it works on Anti-Fighter Barrage, Bombardment and Space Cannon.

  - IncrediSteve · 2021-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719914/article/38396528#38396528

    ### Are relics public?
- id: `relics-public` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — a gained relic goes face up in your play area. Purged cards, like used relic fragments or leaders, leave the game for good.
  - AgentDrake · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567963/article/36606764#36606764
  - bleached_lizard · 2020-08-06 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2479940/article/35520088#35520088
  - Clipper · 2021-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2770235/article/38980861#38980861

    ### How does Galactic Crisis Pact work when Leadership is elected?
    - id: `galactic-crisis-pact-leadership` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Every player may resolve Leadership's secondary; there's no token cost to waive, but spending influence for tokens still applies.
      - Kazadoom · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555422/article/36454712#36454712
      - hooliganj · 2020-12-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2555422/article/36455570#36455570

### Is the Muaat supernova faction tile placed during setup?
- id: `muaat-supernova-tile` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it comes into play only through a Muaat leader ability.
  - bleached_lizard · 2020-11-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2550716/article/36393932#36393932

### Can the Mahact agent be used if the active player has no command token on the board?
- id: `mahact-agent-needs-board-token` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it removes a token from the board; there's no taking one from reinforcements instead.
  - ajmarshall · 2020-11-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2542417/article/36295379#36295379

### Does the Wormhole Nexus count as on the edge of the board?
- id: `wormhole-nexus-edge` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the Living Rules Reference says so, so units there count for edge objectives.
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2564728/article/36575847#36575847
  - IncrediSteve · 2025-04-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3493855/article/45937541#45937541

    ### Which strategy cards can the Winnu and Naaz-Rokha heroes use, and do players pay for secondaries?
    - id: `winnu-and-naaz-rokha-heroes-secondaries` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The Winnu hero can resolve the primary of any strategy card, even an unchosen one, and players who follow pay the secondary's normal costs. The Naaz-Rokha hero resolves the secondaries of up to two readied or unchosen cards, paying tokens from reinforcements, so Construction still places a token, but other costs like resources still apply.
      - Sartavos · 2020-12-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556676/article/36471093#36471093
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-09 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556676/article/36471969#36471969

    ### Can the Titans play their own Terraform note, or reuse it after it's attached?
    - id: `titans-terraform-note` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — you can't play your own faction's note; the receiver attaches it to their own planet, and once attached it can't be played again or traded.
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2561968/article/36543535#36543535
      - AgentDrake · 2020-12-18 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2561968/article/36543565#36543565

### Why are Cormund and Everra printed in red?
- id: `cormund-everra-anomalies` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: They are in anomalies: Everra in a nebula, Cormund in a gravity rift.
  - TerTer · 2020-12-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2556098/article/36462835#36462835

### What can Bio-Stims ready?
- id: `bio-stims-targets` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: At the end of your turn, one exhausted planet with a technology specialty, or one of your other exhausted technologies, never itself.
  - sigmazero13 · 2020-11-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2546852/article/36349183#36349183

    ### If Saar take several planets in one action, can they deploy a mech on each?
    - id: `saar-mech-per-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — each planet gained is its own timing window.
      - AgentDrake · 2021-10-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2739364/article/38617779#38617779
      - ajmarshall · 2021-09-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2719943/article/38396760#38396760

### Does Rout make the attacker leave with no combat rolls?
- id: `rout-still-one-round` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it forces a retreat announcement; a round of combat is still rolled first.
  - Spinners71 · 2021-11-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2754767/article/38801114#38801114

    ### With hyperlanes, which systems are on the edge of the board?
    - id: `edge-of-board-with-hyperlanes` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: A system is on the edge only if one of its sides touches neither another system nor a hyperlane tile, so tiles beside hyperlanes, and a system enclosed by them, aren't edge systems. The Wormhole Nexus does count as on the edge.
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2714915/article/38340735#38340735
      - AgentDrake · 2021-01-24 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586930/article/36822464#36822464

      - AgentDrake · 2020-12-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2554522/article/36444418#36444418
      - Old_Man_gi · 2025-04-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3493855/article/45935662#45935662
      - IncrediSteve · 2025-04-10 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3493855/article/45937541#45937541

    ### Does a system whose planet was destroyed by Stellar Converter count as having no planets for objectives?
    - id: `stellar-converter-no-planet` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Most likely yes — the destroyed planet can't be controlled or invaded, so players treat the system as planetless. No official ruling.
      - Clipper · 2021-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2601851/article/37006563#37006563
      - DistantOasis · 2021-02-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2601851/article/37006450#37006450

### Does the no-adjacent-anomalies setup rule apply to anomaly home systems like the Empyrean nebula?
- id: `anomaly-home-system-setup-adjacency` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Disputed. One reading: home systems are on the board first, so their anomaly counts and you avoid putting anomalies next to them if possible. The other: home systems are placed before the surrounding tiles and don't block anything. Since the rule is only 'if possible', it rarely matters, and with a premade map you ignore it anyway.
  - toskk · 2026-03-20 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3680403/article/47448087#47448087
  - Marcial · 2026-03-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3680403/article/47450529#47450529
  - Hennu_TRM · 2026-09-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2564686/article/48225591#48225591
  - AgentDrake · 2023-06-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3093505/article/42377721#42377721
  - pkitty · 2023-06-01 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3093505/article/42377895#42377895
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2564686/article/36575644#36575644

    ### When does the Council Keleres choose its starting technologies?
    - id: `keleres-starting-tech-timing` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: After every other faction has gathered its starting technologies, regardless of speaker order. Winnu and Argent Flight, who also choose theirs, go before the Keleres.
      - Lowecore · 2024-05-16 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3302028/article/44276030#44276030

      - ajmarshall · 2022-05-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2860678/article/40002131#40002131
      - ajmarshall · 2022-05-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2860678/article/40002185#40002185

### Which systems get frontier tokens, and do hyperlanes get them?
- id: `frontier-tokens-placement` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Every system with no planets gets one, the Creuss Gate included. Hyperlane tiles are not systems, so they don't.
  - TinTeardrop · 2021-08-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2713501/article/38324226#38324226

### With Prophecy of Kings, must I make sure alpha and beta wormholes are among the dealt tiles?
- id: `pok-wormholes-in-dealt-tiles` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — setup leaves the mix to chance, and the Wormhole Nexus still connects whatever wormholes appear.
  - halbower · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567916/article/36606380#36606380
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567916/article/36606460#36606460

    ### How does the Xxcha hero resolve agendas, and can Xxcha play riders on them?
- id: `xxcha-hero-how-it-works` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Resolve the hero's text in order: any law discard comes first, before you look at the agendas. Then you pick two of the drawn agendas and choose one outcome for each as if you had cast a single vote; nobody votes and you can't abstain, and the rest are discarded. No other player may use abilities during it, but the agendas are revealed, so Xxcha can play riders such as Imperial Rider on them. One player argues nothing is revealed.
  - Cacotopos · 2021-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38440830#38440830
  - Clipper · 2021-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38441771#38441771
  - Clipper · 2021-09-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38445524#38445524
  - McBartsomeville · 2021-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38446976#38446976
  - Clipper · 2021-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38447504#38447504
  - Clipper · 2021-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38447318#38447318

    ### After the Xxcha hero resolves agendas in the action phase, do exhausted planets stay exhausted, and does Public Execution carry over?
    - id: `xxcha-hero-follow-up-effects` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Planets exhausted for any votes it triggers stay exhausted until readied normally. A player elected for Public Execution can still vote in the next agenda phase. Transactions during the hero's action still need neighbors, since it isn't the agenda phase. Agenda-phase secret objectives such as Drive the Debate can't be scored from it, since it isn't the agenda phase.
      - Clipper · 2021-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38447504#38447504
      - McBartsomeville · 2021-09-13 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723828/article/38449734#38449734
      - ajmarshall · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37497088#37497088
      - Kazadoom · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37498085#37498085

    ### What does the Xxcha commander allow, and does it beat Political Secret or a rider?
    - id: `xxcha-commander-vs-cannot-vote` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Each planet you exhaust to vote gives 1 extra vote, so even a 0-influence planet gives 1. It is a lasting ability that permits voting, and such abilities beat one-time effects, even ones that say 'cannot', so Xxcha can vote despite a rider or Political Secret. Through the Xxcha alliance even the Nekro can vote.
      - AgentDrake · 2021-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586120/article/36815998#36815998
      - pedro2112 · 2021-01-23 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2586120/article/36815436#36815436
      - ajmarshall · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37497088#37497088
      - Kazadoom · 2021-04-15 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2641241/article/37498085#37498085
      - sigmazero13 · 2021-03-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2625981/article/37312088#37312088
      - Kazadoom · 2021-03-22 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2625981/article/37314849#37314849

### When is Deadly Plot played?
- id: `deadly-plot-timing` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: After all votes are cast and any tie is broken, and after the Legal Text cards, but before the outcome resolves, so before any dice for it are rolled. The agenda is discarded with no effect, and predictions can't pay out since that isn't an outcome.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2714161/article/38342946#38342946
  - Clipper · 2021-08-25 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2714161/article/38343422#38343422

    ### Under Articles of War, do mechs lose Bombardment, Space Cannon and Production too?
    - id: `articles-of-war-mechs` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes — mechs lose all their printed abilities except Sustain Damage. Their cost and combat stats stay, so they still fight, and a mech that rolls 2 dice still does.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2709260/article/38278058#38278058
      - McBartsomeville · 2022-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2961961/article/41069339#41069339
      - PistonLoop · 2022-10-30 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2961961/article/41072161#41072161

    ### How does Checks and Balances' Against outcome work?
    - id: `checks-and-balances-against` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: It replaces the readying at the end of the agenda phase for everyone, however they voted: each player readies only 3 exhausted planets, and planets that weren't exhausted stay ready. Under For, players must take whatever strategy card they are given.
      - Clipper · 2021-08-14 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2709260/article/38278058#38278058
      - StrangeBeacon · 2026-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3684732/article/47482907#47482907
      - GhostTrail · 2026-03-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3684732/article/47483012#47483012
      - hooliganj · 2026-03-29 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3684732/article/47483776#47483776

### Do Jol-Nar's Brilliant or the Jae Mir Kan trigger off a free secondary from Galactic Crisis Pact?
- id: `galactic-crisis-pact-free-secondary` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — both need a command token spent on a secondary during a strategic action, and the agenda's free secondary spends no token outside a strategic action.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-17 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2709260/article/38290013#38290013
  - Clipper · 2021-08-19 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2709260/article/38303422#38303422

### Does Nexus Sovereignty stop Space Cannon through the Wormhole Nexus?
- id: `nexus-sovereignty-scope` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — it only turns off the nexus wormholes for moving units. Other wormhole adjacency, including for Space Cannon, still works.
  - hooliganj · 2022-09-05 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2929033/article/40742548#40742548

    ### When can a player make a prediction for the Nomad's Future Sight?
    - id: `nomad-future-sight-predictions` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Only when an ability lets you predict, usually a rider. Predictions are declared after the agenda is revealed and before any votes, and predicting means you can't vote on that agenda.
      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2558241/article/36494615#36494615
      - AgentDrake · 2020-12-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2558241/article/36494616#36494616

### Does Winnu's Acquiescence skip placing a token from Diplomacy, and can Mathis Mathinus use any strategy card?
- id: `winnu-acquiescence-and-mathis` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Acquiescence only waives the cost of following Winnu's own strategic actions; the Diplomacy primary still makes you place your token. Mathis Mathinus can resolve any of the eight strategy cards, regardless of who holds it or whether it is exhausted.
  - Clipper · 2021-08-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2705757/article/38238201#38238201

  ### Can the Sardakk commander commit ground forces from adjacent systems without moving any ships in, even past enemy ships?
  - id: `sardakk-commander-no-ships` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — the invasion step happens in every tactical action, so you can activate the system, move nothing, and commit ground forces from adjacent planets. If only enemy ships are there, no space combat occurs since you have no ships in it. A player reports the developer confirmed this in an AMA.

### Can mechs use Sustain Damage against Bombardment?
- id: `mech-sustain-vs-bombardment` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — Bombardment produces hits, so a mech can cancel one. Sustain Damage only fails against effects that destroy units outright.
  - TinTeardrop · 2021-01-08 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2575585/article/36688921#36688921
  - Phoenix44 · 2021-01-08 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2575585/article/36688906#36688906

  ### Can a ship repaired by the Empyrean flagship use Sustain Damage again in the same round?
  - id: `empyrean-flagship-repairs` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: No — a unit can sustain only once per instance of the timing window, so it can't cancel more of that round's hits, but it can sustain again next round. Each repair is paid separately.
    - hooliganj · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567314/article/36600261#36600261
    - hooliganj · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567314/article/36600417#36600417
    - joepinion · 2020-12-28 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2567314/article/36600135#36600135

### Does a Nomad mech on the Memoria flagship give it an extra Sustain Damage?
- id: `nomad-mech-on-memoria` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: No — the mech uses its own Sustain Damage in space, and the flagship uses its own, so together they can cancel two hits. Direct Hit can't be played when the mech cancels a hit.
  - McBartsomeville · 2022-06-12 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2882323/article/40251619#40251619

    ### Can mechs carried in the space area use Space Cannon?
- id: `xxcha-mechs-space-cannon-transported` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — nothing limits Space Cannon to units on planets, so carried mechs, such as Xxcha mechs, can fire in the active system under the normal targeting rules.

    ### Can Sol's commander place infantry if no ground combat happens?
- id: `sol-commander-needs-ground-combat` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — it triggers at the start of ground combat, which only happens if a defending ground force (a mech counts; structures don't) survives to that point.

    ### Does the Vuil'raith hero make a ship roll once per adjacent dimensional tear?
    - id: `vuilraith-hero-one-roll` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — each non-fighter ship in or next to a dimensional tear rolls once, however many tears it touches. It concerns dimensional tears, not gravity rifts generally.

    ### How does the Mentak hero work, and can it be escaped?
    - id: `mentak-hero-mid-combat` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Purge it at the start of space combat; for the whole combat, each enemy ship destroyed gives you a matching ship, before a winner is decided, so the combat can continue even if your other ships died. The opponent can limit it by retreating, or escape before any losses with Skilled Retreat, which comes at the start of a combat round, after the hero.

      - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-03 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2553176/article/36426110#36426110

  ### Do abilities that trigger on ships moving into a system, or through a wormhole, count systems merely passed through?
  - id: `moving-through-is-not-moving-into` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — only the system where movement ends is 'moved into', so the Empyrean commander returns at most one token per move. Likewise, a ship only moves through a wormhole for the Creuss commander if it actually uses the wormhole's connection; crossing a wormhole system doesn't count.

### Can ships stay in a supernova after entering it with an ability like Nav Suite?
- id: `nav-suite-supernova` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — a supernova only stops units entering; nothing forces them to leave afterwards.

### Does the Saar agent stack with Gravity Drive?
- id: `saar-agent-and-gravity-drive` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — it compares current move values, bonuses included, not printed ones, as a FAQ answer players quote confirms.

  - iswearihaveajob · 2021-07-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2690781/article/38069003#38069003

    ### How does the Mahact hero's Benediction work?
    - id: `mahact-hero-benediction` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Pick one system, with your fleet or another player's, and move all units in its space area (fighters, ground forces in space and Saar docks included) into one adjacent system containing another player's ships, where they must fight. The moved ships' owner must be able to enter that system, so no pushing them into asteroid fields or supernovas they can't enter, and Creuss's alpha-beta adjacency can't be used. Neither side may retreat or use abilities that move ships, Skilled Retreat included.

      - Zejety · 2021-01-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2584678/article/36796945#36796945

### How does the Nomad flagship Memoria's adjacency to mechs work?
- id: `nomad-memoria-mech-paths` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: The Memoria alone treats every system containing a Nomad mech as adjacent, mech on a planet or in space, so it can reach that system for 1 move however far away, though enemy ships there still block it. A system next to the mech costs 2. It is not a wormhole: other players' Space Cannon can't use it and it doesn't work in reverse. A mech being carried in the same move doesn't count, since ships move together, but the Memoria can pick up mechs from systems it enters.
  - IncrediSteve · 2020-12-21 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563306/article/36557999#36557999

  - bigGameGeek · 2020-12-21 · disagrees — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2563306/article/36561292#36561292

### How does Aerie Hololattice's Production 1 work?
- id: `aerie-hololattice-production` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Each planet with your structure gets Production 1 as if it were a unit, which raises the system's total and lets a planet with only a PDS produce. It isn't actually a unit, so effects that look for a unit with Production, such as the Arborec commander, don't see it, and it adds nothing to the Warfare secondary, which uses only one space dock's Production.

### If one of my units is captured, can I still produce more of that type?
- id: `captured-unit-type-production` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — the restriction applies only to the captured unit itself; other units of that type in reinforcements can be produced.

    ### Can I unlock the Naalu commander partway through a production and use it for the rest?
    - id: `naalu-commander-mid-production` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — production is one step, so the unlock condition is only met after it finishes; use the commander from your next production.

      - KingOfStumps · 2021-09-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/2723733/article/38439922#38439922

### Can the Argent Flight agent place ground forces on planets the player doesn't control?
- id: `argent-agent-placement` — grade: Community consensus
- A: No — only on planets that player controls, in the producing system or adjacent systems. TI4 has planet control, not system control.

      ### With Prophecy of Kings capture rules, when exactly is a space dock blockaded?
      - id: `blockade-and-capture-timing` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: Use the Rules Reference definition: a system with another player's ships and none of yours. Players point out it matters for captures, since during combat both sides have ships, so a dock isn't blockaded until combat ends.

### Can I own both no-prerequisite technologies of one color from Prophecy of Kings, and do they count together?
- id: `two-no-prerequisite-techs-same-color` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Yes — each counts as a technology of that color, so together they meet a two-of-that-color prerequisite.

### Can Jol-Nar use their own agent when they research?
- id: `jol-nar-agent-on-self` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: Yes — agents worded 'a player' can target yourself; only those saying 'another player' exclude you.

### Can the Yin commander let me research Destroyer II when only Argent owns Strike Wing Alpha II?
- id: `yin-commander-faction-upgrade` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

## Twilight Imperium: Fourth Edition – Thunder's Edge (Expansion)

### Do I need to swap Ta Zern for Agnlan Oln when combining Thunder's Edge with Prophecy of Kings?
- id: `ta-zern-replaced-by-agnlan-oln` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Only for theme and clarity — the two cards work identically. Ta Zern now belongs to the Deepwrought, and the swap avoids two components sharing a name. Keeping Ta Zern changes nothing in play.
  - AgentDrake · 2025-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3604742/article/46836252#46836252
  - IncrediSteve · 2025-11-04 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3604742/article/46836311#46836311

### What counts as a unit ability for Agnlan Oln's reroll?
- id: `agnlan-oln-unit-ability` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: An ability printed on a unit, structures included: Bombardment, Anti-Fighter Barrage, Space Cannon and the like. Rolls for those abilities can be rerolled.
  - Zangzabar · 2025-11-11 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3609114/article/46871917#46871917

### Can Plane Splitter move an ingress token into a system in the Fracture?
- id: `plane-splitter-into-fracture` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**
- A: By the rules as written, yes — the Fracture's hexes are separate systems, so one adjacent to your units qualifies. It only triggers at the start of your strategic actions, so this rarely matters. Some players read it as main-board only.

### How many systems can the Argent Flight breakthrough affect?

    ### Is The Fracture 3 systems or 7, and is it adjacent to the galaxy it touches?
    - id: `fracture-seven-systems` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Most players treat each hex as its own system, making 7, and players report the developer said the same. Fracture systems that touch are adjacent to each other but not to the galaxy tiles beside them; you reach it only through ingress and egress tokens.     ### How does the Thunder's Edge Warfare strategy card work?
    - id: `te-warfare-card` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: The primary lets you redistribute your tokens and perform a tactical action without placing a command token, even in a system that already holds yours or a Diplomacy token, so newly produced ships can move later that round; that system still counts as activated. You can move ships into an already-activated system that way, but not out of one. The secondary produces with all your units in your home system.

    - id: `te-construction-card` — grade: Community consensus

    ### How does the Thunder's Edge expedition finish, and who gets the planet?

  ### How does coexistence work?
  - id: `coexistence-rules` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Only units with an effect granting it can coexist, mostly from the Deepwrought; Deepwrought may choose it whenever any player commits ground forces to the planet. The player whose units triggered it never controls the planet or takes its card, though they count as controlling it for objectives and neighbors; if they held it, they lose control. Coexisting structures still work but count as blockaded, and you can't build on a planet you only coexist on.
    - hooliganj · 2026-01-07 — https://boardgamegeek.com/thread/3640757/article/47121409#47121409

  ### Can a coexisting player end coexistence and attack?
  - id: `ending-coexistence` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: Yes — activate the system and commit the coexisting units against the planet; win the ground combat and you take control as normal. Planetary Shield on the planet protects everyone there from Bombardment, both players can fire Space Cannon Defense at an invader, and Support for the Throne still counts coexisting units.

    ### How do Crimson Rebellion breaches work?
    - id: `crimson-breaches` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: A breach goes in the combat's system at the end of combat, whoever won, if one of your destroyers is in range then; it needn't have fought.     ### Who places ingress tokens when Rapid Mobilization brings the Fracture in?
- id: `fracture-rapid-mobilization` — grade: Community consensus
- A: Treat it as coming in through another ability: the triggering player places ingress tokens in four different systems, each with a planet of a different specialty if able.

    ### Can ships pass through the Fracture in one move, and do touching ingress systems stop being adjacent?
    - id: `fracture-ingress-movement` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: Yes, they can go in through one ingress and out via an egress to another ingress system if their move allows. The rule that ingresses aren't adjacent to each other only means the tokens don't link like wormholes; physically touching systems stay adjacent.

### After forming the Naaz-Rokha Eidolon Maximum, can I rebuild my mechs?
- id: `eidolon-maximum-no-rebuild` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

    ### Does an agenda voted through Keleres's Executive Order count as the agenda phase?
    - id: `zeal-with-executive-order` — grade: Community consensus
    - A: No — phase steps like readying don't happen and agenda-phase secrets, Drive the Debate included, can't be scored, but 'when/after an agenda is revealed' effects still work. Argent's Zeal gives its extra votes but not the vote-first part.

  ### Can a researching player use both the Deepwrought commander and alliance for -2?
  - id: `deepwrought-commander-alliance-stack` — grade: Community consensus

    ### Do neutral fighters need capacity, and do neutral units count for Total War?
    - id: `neutral-units` — grade: Community consensus

    ### How do the Last Bastion's spaceport card, Helios upgrade and Galvanize tokens work?
    - id: `last-bastion-components` — grade: Community consensus

      ### Are breakthroughs technologies, and do their passive abilities work while exhausted?
      - id: `breakthroughs-not-technologies` — grade: Community consensus
      - A: They aren't technologies, so a breakthrough like Mentak's Corsair doesn't count as a unit upgrade or for anything that cares about technologies. Their passive abilities keep working while exhausted. Synergy lets a technology of one linked colour count as the other for colour objectives.

### In the Alliance variant, do 'another player' abilities trigger on my ally?
- id: `alliance-another-player` — grade: Best reading — unconfirmed **[NOT USED — unconfirmed]**

  ### Are there quick answers for Watchtower, Reflect, Garbozia's legendary ability, Brilliance, Ignis and Ral Nel Linkships?
  - id: `te-small-clarifications` — grade: Community consensus
  - A: The Watchtower is one system, so one anomaly. Reflect lets you choose per trade good, in sequence. Garbozia's legendary ability lets you choose which discarded action card to take. Brilliance's chosen player gains their own breakthrough. Ignis places a token without activating, so Nullification Field can't answer it. Each Linkship II rolls one Space Cannon die however many PDS share its space.
